### [1] [Conformal Prediction for Time Series with Deep Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02357)
*Junghwan Lee,Jonghyeok Lee,Yao Xie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了深度序列模型在时间序列保形预测中的三种使用方式，并提供渐近条件覆盖的理论保证与实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习时间序列预测的进展提升了对可靠不确定性量化的需求。保形预测作为无分布假设、具有覆盖保证的区间构造框架受到关注，但其覆盖保证依赖数据可交换性，而该假设在时间序列中通常不成立。已有研究虽常将深度序列模型（如RNN、Transformer）用于时间序列保形预测，但缺乏对其使用方式的系统性研究。

Method: 通过三种途径系统探究深度序列模型在时间序列保形预测中的应用：（1）条件分位数回归；（2）条件分位数函数估计；（3）局部化保形预测。

Result: 在适当假设下，为上述三种方法建立了渐近条件覆盖保证的理论分析；并在真实世界数据集上进行了大量实验，证明了将深度序列模型引入时间序列保形预测的有效性。

Conclusion: 深度序列模型可有效融入时间序列保形预测，且三种途径在合宜假设下均具备渐近条件覆盖保证，实验验证了其实际有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning for time series prediction have amplified the need for reliable uncertainty quantification. Conformal prediction has gained attention as a distribution-free framework for constructing prediction intervals with coverage guarantees. However, its coverage guarantees rely on data exchangeability, an assumption generally violated in time series. Active research has focused on developing conformal prediction methods for time series that overcome this limitation. While deep sequence models, such as recurrent neural networks and Transformers, have often been used in conformal prediction for time series, limited work has systematically studied how deep sequence models can be utilized in conformal prediction for time series. In this work, we systematically investigate the use of deep sequence models in conformal prediction for time series through three approaches: conditional quantile regression, conditional quantile function estimation, and localized conformal prediction. We provide a theoretical analysis establishing asymptotic conditional coverage guarantees for all three approaches under suitable assumptions. Through comprehensive experiments on real-world datasets, we demonstrate the effectiveness of leveraging deep sequence models into conformal prediction for time series.

</details>


### [2] [Learning Style, Forgetting Semantics: A Case Study of SFT and RFT on Classification Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02437)
*Haodong Liang,Yanhao Jin,Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Lifeng Lai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文从理论上解释为何监督微调（SFT）比强化微调（RFT）更容易导致遗忘：在共享语义规则、风格偏好不同的分类任务上，SFT 会沿风格维度产生"离轴漂移"，而 RFT 保持风格对称性，从而在显式条件下建立遗忘的分离结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFT 导致的遗忘普遍多于 RFT，即使教师示范在语义上全部正确，这一现象缺乏机理解释。论文关注在同一语义规则下、但提示分布与教师风格偏好不同的任务序列，试图刻画 SFT 与 RFT 在语义与风格两个成分上的更新差异。

Method: 采用可解析的线性-softmax 策略，对参数更新做语义成分与风格成分的精确分解；对比同一策略与提示下 SFT 与 RFT 的语义、风格动力学；并分析从无类内风格偏好的策略出发，RFT 使用精确策略梯度与 SFT 使用非均匀教师时的演化差异。

Result: 在同一策略和提示下，SFT 与 RFT 的语义更新平行，但风格动力学不同。RFT 保留类内风格对称性，而 SFT 在非均匀教师下会沿非零任务均值产生离轴风格漂移。据此在显式条件下建立分离：从共同且完美拟合的检查点出发做总体更新时，SFT 的遗忘在有限训练区间内存在严格正下界，而 RFT 保持零语义误差；任务序列上的模拟支持该预测。

Conclusion: 遗忘差异可归因于 SFT 的风格漂移机制而非语义学习本身；风格对称性的保持是 RFT 抗遗忘的关键。

Abstract: Why does supervised fine-tuning (SFT) lead to more forgetting than reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT), even when all teacher demonstrations are semantically correct? We study this question on classification tasks where tokens within each semantic class express the same semantic answer in different styles. The tasks share an underlying semantic rule but differ in their prompt distributions and teachers' stylistic preferences. Using a tractable linear-softmax policy, we derive an exact decomposition of the updates into semantic and style components. We show that, at a common policy and prompt, SFT and RFT have parallel semantic updates but differ in their style dynamics. Starting from a policy with no within-class style preference, RFT with exact policy gradients preserves this symmetry, whereas SFT with a nonuniform teacher develops off-axis style drift along a nonzero task mean under population updates. We use this drift to establish a separation under explicit conditions: for population updates from a common perfectly fitted checkpoint, SFT forgetting admits a strictly positive lower bound over a finite training interval, while RFT retains zero semantic error. Simulations over task sequences support these theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [3] [ENCORE: Exact Non-equilibrium COntrol with Replica Exchange for Diffusion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02538)
*Jiahao Yu,Saifuddin Syed,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Jiajun He*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 ENCORE，首个精确的并行推断时控制方法，用于将预训练生成模型导向倾斜目标分布 π₀∝G₀p₀，通过存储轨迹避免不可解的时间反演，并给出稳定性分析与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推断时控制无需重训练即可将预训练生成模型导向目标分布，现有方法分两类：序列退火+序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）虽精确但需大量粒子；并行退火+副本交换（RE）虽并行但无精确方法，其RE校正近似了不可解的时间反演因而有偏。作者旨在填补"精确并行控制方法"的空白。

Method: 提出 ENCORE（Exact Non-equilibrium COntrol with Replica Exchange）：每个副本存储其生成轨迹，使向上移动变为截断操作，从而无需模拟不可解的时间反演；证明目标不变性，并表明所得动力学等价于以精确时间反演为前向提议的非平衡副本交换；在正则条件下通过扩散分析研究离散化细化下的稳定性，并给出调度与计算预算的调参诊断。

Result: 扩散分析表明：无引导时，序列与并行控制在时间离散化细化下均不稳定；有引导提议则保持稳定，并提供调度与预算调参诊断。在合成目标、生物分子玻尔兹曼采样与图像生成任务上，ENCORE 达到有竞争力的精度与多样性，对采样器扰动稳健，且适用于蒸馏采样器等现有RE校正不可用的场景。

Conclusion: ENCORE 是首个精确的并行推断时控制方法，兼具精确性与并行效率，理论上有目标不变性保证与稳定性诊断，实证上在多样任务中表现稳健且适用范围更广。

Abstract: Inference-time control steers a pretrained generative model towards a target distribution without retraining. We study tilted targets $π_0\propto G_0\,p_0$, where $p_0$ is the sampler output distribution and $G_0$ is an evaluable reweighting function. Existing approaches rely on sequential annealing with sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) or parallel annealing with replica exchange (RE). Sequential control is exact but needs large particle populations, whereas no exact parallel control method exists: existing RE corrections approximate an intractable time reversal and are biased. We propose Exact Non-equilibrium COntrol with Replica Exchange (ENCORE), the first exact parallel control method. Each replica stores its generation trajectory, so the upward move is a truncation and the intractable time reversal is never simulated. We prove target invariance and show that the resulting dynamics are those of non-equilibrium replica exchange with the exact time reversal as forward proposal. Under regularity conditions, our diffusion analysis shows that both sequential and parallel control become unstable under refinement of the time discretisation without guidance, whereas guided proposals remain stable and yield diagnostics for tuning the schedule and the computational budget. Across synthetic targets, Boltzmann sampling of biomolecules, and image generation, ENCORE achieves competitive accuracy and diversity, remains robust to sampler perturbations, and applies to distilled samplers where existing RE corrections are unavailable.

</details>


### [4] [High-Dimensional Asymptotics and Dataset Selection for Private Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02578)
*Filip Kovačević,Edwige Cyffers,Stefano Sarao Mannelli,Marco Mondelli*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究在仅能获取汇总统计、存在模型与协变量漂移、且需满足差分隐私的约束下，如何决定是否购买或使用外部数据集进行迁移学习；作者以多异质源高维回归与加权岭估计为框架，推导出测试误差的确定性等价式，据此可优化权重与正则参数并判断私有外部数据的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 购买外部数据或参与协作学习前，需判断额外数据带来的预测增益是否值得其成本。该决策面临三重挑战：只能依赖公开的聚合统计而非个体级数据；协变量与模型漂移可能引发负迁移，使额外数据反而降低性能；若数据敏感，隐私化所注入的噪声可能抵消样本量扩大的收益。因此需要一个仅用总体层面量、同时考虑漂移与隐私噪声的可理论分析的选择框架。

Method: 将数据集选择建模为多异质源下的高维回归问题，采用加权岭估计器。方法仅使用汇总统计量，并在标签单独或特征与标签联合两种情形下提供 ρ-零集中差分隐私（ρ-zCDP）保证。核心推导是测试误差的确定性等价（deterministic equivalent），刻画样本量、协方差结构、模型漂移、正则化与隐私噪声之间的交互作用。

Result: 所得确定性等价式可用于优化超参数（源权重与岭正则项），并能在不访问数据本身、仅依赖总体层面量的条件下判断私有外部数据集是否有用。合成数据与真实数据实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文为私有迁移学习提供了一个理论可处理的基础：通过测试误差的确定性等价，将数据获取决策、超参数调优与隐私—效用权衡统一到仅依赖汇总/总体统计的分析框架中。

Abstract: To commit to buying external data or participate in collaborative learning, one must decide whether the additional data will improve prediction enough to justify the cost. This comes with several challenges: (i) the decision often relies only on aggregated statistics available publicly, rather than individual-level data; (ii) covariate and model shifts can induce negative transfer, so the additional data deteriorates rather than improves performance; (iii) if the data is sensitive, its privatization requires the injection of noise, which can also offset the benefit of a larger sample size. In this paper, we model the problem of dataset selection through high-dimensional regression with multiple heterogeneous sources and a weighted ridge estimator. Our approach uses only summary statistics and it gives privacy guarantees either on labels only or jointly on features and labels, in terms of $ρ$-zero-concentrated differential privacy. The main technical contribution is a deterministic equivalent of the test error, which captures the interactions between sample size, covariance structure, model shift, regularization and privacy noise. Our theory allows to optimize hyperparameters (weights and ridge regularizers) and, more broadly, to decide when private external datasets are useful without accessing the data itself but only relying on population-level quantities. This provides a theoretically tractable foundation for private transfer learning, which we support via experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [5] [Generalization Properties of Score-matching Diffusion Models for Intrinsically Low-dimensional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02663)
*Saptarshi Chakraborty,Quentin Berthet,Peter L. Bartlett*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究flow-matching模型的统计泛化，证明其Wasserstein-p误差界依赖数据的内在维度而非环境维度，从而缓解维数灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: flow-matching模型经验上很成功，但统计泛化理论不完善。已有分析常对估计的速度场施加较强假设，且收敛速率无法体现自然图像、分子结构等真实数据固有的低维结构。

Method: 从有限样本学习未知分布P_data的统计泛化分析。给定n个i.i.d.样本，针对所有p≥1推导学习生成分布在Wasserstein-p距离下的有限样本误差界；对每个d>d_p^*(P_data)选择合适网络架构与超参数，其中d_p^*(P_data)为目标测度的Wasserstein-p维度。

Result: 以至少1-ξ的概率，学习分布满足W_p(P_hat^FM, P_data) ≲ n^{-1/d}+n^{-1/(2p)}(log(1/ξ))^{1/(2p)}。收敛指数由内在维度而非环境维度决定。

Conclusion: flow matching能自然适应数据的内在几何并缓解维数灾难，其保证在高维情形下仍有意义，并在比已有分析更宽松的假设下为结构化数据上的经验成功提供理论解释。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable empirical success of flow-matching models, their statistical generalization guarantees remain underdeveloped. Existing analyses often impose restrictive assumptions on the estimated velocity field and yield convergence rates that fail to reflect the intrinsic low-dimensional structure common in real data, such as natural images and molecular geometries. In this work, we study the statistical generalization of flow-matching models for learning an unknown distribution $P_{\mathrm{data}}$ from finitely many samples. We derive finite-sample error bounds on the learned generative distribution, measured in the Wasserstein-$p$ distance, for all $p\geq 1$. Specifically, given $n$ i.i.d. samples from $P_{\mathrm{data}}$, we show that, for every $d>d_p^\ast(P_{\mathrm{data}})$ and appropriately chosen network architectures and hyperparameters, the learned distribution $\widehat{P}^{\mathrm{FM}}$ satisfies $ \mathbb{W}_p(\widehat{P}^{\mathrm{FM}},P_{\mathrm{data}}) \lesssim n^{-1/d}+n^{-1/(2p)}\bigl(\log(1/ξ)\bigr)^{1/(2p)}$ with probability at least $1-ξ$, where $d_p^\ast(P_{\mathrm{data}})$ denotes the Wasserstein-$p$ dimension of the target measure. Our results demonstrate that flow matching naturally adapts to the intrinsic geometry of data and mitigates the curse of dimensionality, as the convergence exponent depends on the intrinsic rather than ambient dimension. These guarantees remain meaningful in high-dimensional regimes and provide a theoretical explanation for the empirical success of flow matching on structured data distributions under substantially more relaxed assumptions than those in existing analyses.

</details>


### [6] [Hold-Out Scoring for Efficient Gaussian DAG Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02785)
*Donguk Shin,Byeongguk Kang,Inseol Lee,Gunwoong Park*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: HOST是一种高效的高维高斯DAG学习算法，用节点级留出评分与凸回归替代子集搜索，无需预知入度上界，可在多项式时间内以约d log p的样本复杂度精确恢复DAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维高斯DAG学习存在统计-计算鸿沟：具有紧样本复杂度的方法依赖计算昂贵的子集搜索且需要外部提供入度上界，而多项式时间方法样本复杂度较差。

Method: HOST用节点级留出评分与凸回归替代子集搜索，无需提供入度上界。核心洞见在于：恢复正确排序并不需要排序分数的一致小估计误差，只需对这些误差进行单侧控制。在排序步骤中，利用留出样本的分数估计在期望上会高估排序分数，这对"尚不应被选中"的候选是有利方向；给定排序后，通过递归地从两节点间的总效应中移除间接效应来恢复父节点。

Result: 在适当条件下，HOST能在多项式时间内以约d log p阶的样本复杂度精确恢复具有最大入度d的p节点DAG。实验表明HOST在实现有竞争力的图恢复效果的同时，具有良好的运行时扩展性。

Conclusion: HOST弥合了高维高斯DAG学习的统计-计算鸿沟，兼具紧样本复杂度与多项式时间效率，且无需预先指定入度上界。

Abstract: High-dimensional Gaussian DAG learning faces a statistical-computational gap: methods with sharp sample complexity rely on computationally expensive subset search and a supplied indegree bound, whereas polynomial-time alternatives have less favorable sample complexity. We introduce HOST, an efficient DAG learning algorithm that replaces subset search with nodewise hold-out scoring and convex regression, without requiring a supplied indegree bound. Our key insight is that recovering a correct ordering does not require uniformly small estimation errors in ordering scores but only one-sided control of those errors. In the ordering step, HOST exploits the fact that score estimation using hold-out samples inflates ordering scores in expectation, which is the favorable direction for candidates that should not yet be selected. Given the ordering, HOST recovers parents by recursively removing indirect effects from total effects between two nodes. Under suitable conditions, HOST exactly recovers a $p$-node DAG of maximum indegree $d$ with sample complexity of order $d\log p$ in polynomial time. Experiments show that HOST achieves competitive graph recovery while exhibiting favorable runtime scaling.

</details>


### [7] [Invariance of Clustering Operations in Causal Effect Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03101)
*Jani Nykänen,Otto Tabell,Santtu Tikka,Juha Karvanen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究因果图中变量聚类问题，提出一类基于原图c-分量条件的聚类操作，证明其为"识别不变"的，即同时保持可识别性与不可识别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类变量可缩减因果图规模并简化因果推断，但任意聚类会改变变量间关键因果关系、导致错误结论。已有结论表明聚类图中因果效应可识别可推出原图可识别，然而聚类图不可识别并不能推出原图不可识别（除非附加假设），因此需要刻画何种聚类能同时保持两种性质。

Method: 引入"识别不变"（identification invariant）概念，即同时保持可识别性与不可识别性的聚类操作；基于原图c-分量（c-components）的相关条件，构造并刻画一大类满足识别不变性的聚类操作。

Result: 给出一类广泛的、基于c-分量条件的识别不变聚类操作，并展示了这些结果在实际场景中的使用。

Conclusion: 当聚类同时保持可识别性与不可识别性时，其不改变原图的因果识别结论；满足该性质的聚类可由原图c-分量条件刻画，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Clustering variables in causal graphs reduces the size of the graph and simplifies causal inference. However, arbitrary clustering can alter crucial causal relations among variables and lead to erroneous conclusions. While the identifiability of a causal effect in the clustered graph implies the identifiability in the original graph under mild conditions, nonidentifiability in clustered graph does not imply nonidentifiability in the original graph without further assumptions. When both identifiability and nonidentifiability are preserved, the clustering operation is called identification invariant. We present a broad class of clustering operations that are identification invariant based on conditions related to the c-components of the original graph. Finally, we demonstrate use of the results in practical settings.

</details>


### [8] [Predictively Oriented Gaussian Process Posteriors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03201)
*Callum Lau,Jeremias Knoblauch,Louis Sharrock*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出预测导向高斯过程（PrO-GPs），以预测不确定性为核心推断目标，通过推导简化形式与实用采样方案实现高效计算，在模型误设定下获得比标准GP更好的校准预测分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程（GPs）是建模与量化函数关系不确定性的有力工具，但依赖使用者做多项设计决策（如核函数与观测模型选择）。次优选择会导致模型误设定，无法捕捉真实数据生成过程，因此需要一种对误设定更稳健的替代方法。

Method: 提出Predictively Oriented Gaussian Processes (PrO-GPs)，将预测不确定性作为主要推断目标；针对非参数模型下PrO后验直接计算不可解的问题，推导出简化（reduced）形式并给出实用的采样方案以实现高效计算。

Result: 在合成数据与真实数据实验中，相较于标准GP方法，PrO-GPs在模型误设定情形下能产生校准更好的预测分布。

Conclusion: PrO-GPs通过以预测不确定性为导向的重构，为模型误设定提供了更稳健的高斯过程替代方案，并具备可行的计算实现。

Abstract: Gaussian Processes (GPs) are a powerful tool for modelling and quantifying uncertainty in functional relationships. However, they require practitioners to make a number of design decisions, such as the choice of the kernel and the observation model. Suboptimal choices can produce misspecified models that do not capture the underlying data generating process. We introduce Predictively Oriented Gaussian Processes (PrO-GPs), which treat predictive uncertainty as the primary inferential target and provide a robust alternative to standard GPs. Although direct computation of a PrO posterior for nonparametric models is intractable, we derive a reduced formulation and practical sampling scheme for efficient computation. Through synthetic and real data experiments, we show that PrO-GPs produce better calibrated predictive distributions under model misspecification compared to standard GP approaches.

</details>


### [9] [SDECast: Probabilistic Weather Forecasting in Continuous Time with Neural SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03313)
*Maria Marchenko,Martin Andrae,Fredrik Lindsten,Christian A. Naesseth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: SDECast：基于神经随机微分方程的连续时间概率天气预报框架，用SDE Matching在物理空间直接学习随机动力学，训练时无需反复模拟SDE，在模拟地球物理流上恢复漂移动力学，并实现逐小时全球概率预报，有效预见期达5天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气预报模型通常在固定时间分辨率下进行自回归滚动预测。该范式虽对长程预测高效，但在更短时间步长下会出现严重的误差累积，且未显式建模大气动力学的局部性与时间连续性。

Method: 提出SDECast，一种用于连续时间概率天气预报的神经随机微分方程（SDE）框架。该方法扩展SDE Matching，在物理空间中直接学习随机动力学，训练过程中无需重复进行SDE模拟。

Result: 在模拟地球物理流上，SDECast能够恢复有意义的漂移动力学，并忠实再现潜在的连续时间行为；在全球天气预报任务中展现可扩展性，可在逐小时分辨率下生成有效（skillful）的概率预报，预见期最长可达5天。

Conclusion: SDECast通过神经SDE与SDE Matching将天气预报建模为连续时间随机动力学学习，缓解了固定步长自回归带来的误差累积问题，并在全球逐小时概率预报上验证了其有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Existing machine learning weather forecasting models typically generate forecasts through autoregressive rollouts at a fixed temporal resolution. While highly efficient for long-range prediction, this formulation can suffer from severe error accumulation when used with shorter time steps and does not explicitly encode the locality and temporal continuity of atmospheric dynamics. To address these limitations, we introduce **SDECast**, a Neural Stochastic Differential Equation (SDE) framework for continuous-time probabilistic weather forecasting. SDECast extends SDE Matching to learn stochastic dynamics directly in physical space, without requiring repeated SDE simulation during training. On a simulated geophysical flow, we show that SDECast recovers meaningful drift dynamics and faithfully reproduces the underlying continuous-time behavior. We then demonstrate its scalability to global weather forecasting at hourly resolution, where SDECast produces skillful probabilistic forecasts for lead times of up to five days.

</details>


### [10] [DAWIS: Data Assimilation with Windowed Inverse Sampling via Multitask Interpolants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03314)
*Erik Wikingsson,Martin Andrae,Tomas Landelius,Fredrik Lindsten*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出DAWIS，一种统一的扩散/流生成式数据同化框架，可同时实现滤波、固定滞后平滑与块平滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于生成模型的数据同化滤波器仅以固定历史为条件并只同化最新观测，无法在获得新观测后修正过去状态，导致估计被后续观测可能否定的历史所束缚，误差在递归过程中累积。

Method: DAWIS用一个覆盖连续状态窗口的多任务随机插值（stochastic interpolant）替代状态级先验的单一流时间，为窗口内每个状态分配各自独立的流时间；一个同化循环将窗口反演为逐状态转折点（turning points）向量，并在观测引导下重新生成该窗口，转折点决定每个状态被固定、修正或从头生成的程度。该构造也可将预报融入同化循环，从而无需单独的预报模型。

Result: 在具有挑战性的非线性系统上的实验表明，在稀疏、含噪及非线性观测条件下，DAWIS 的性能优于滤波和平滑的基线方法。

Conclusion: DAWIS 将滤波、固定滞后平滑和块平滑整合于单一生成式框架，通过多任务随机插值的逐状态流时间机制实现对历史状态的灵活修正，并能吸收预报过程，为高维非高斯数据同化提供了统一且有效的新途径。

Abstract: Flow- and diffusion-based generative models have recently emerged as flexible and highly efficient forecasting models for dynamical systems. When combined with inference-time guidance, they offer a promising route to high-dimensional non-Gaussian data assimilation (DA), the problem of combining forecasts with observations to estimate latent system states. Existing filters, however, condition on a fixed history and assimilate only the most recent observation, leaving them unable to revise past states when new observations arrive. Estimates then stay tethered to a history that later observations may contradict, and errors accumulate over the assimilation run. To this end, we introduce **DAWIS**, a unified DA method covering filtering, fixed-lag smoothing, and block smoothing within a single framework. DAWIS replaces the single flow time of a state-level prior with a multitask stochastic interpolant over a window of consecutive states, assigning a separate flow time to each. An assimilation cycle inverts the window to a vector of per-state turning points and regenerates it under observation guidance, with the turning points controlling how strongly each state is held fixed, revised, or generated from scratch. The same construction can also absorb the forecast into the assimilation cycle, removing the need for a separate forecasting model. Experiments on challenging nonlinear systems show that DAWIS improves on both filtering and smoothing baselines under sparse, noisy, and nonlinear observations. The code for DAWIS is available at https://github.com/Erik-Wikingsson/DAWIS

</details>


### [11] [Iterating Consistency Models: Stability, Error Bounds and Noise Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03414)
*Alessio Spagnoletti,Abdul-Lateef Haji-Ali,Andrés Almansa,Alain Oliviero Durmus,Eric Moulines,Marcelo Pereyra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文将多步一致性模型采样建模为加噪与近似去噪算子的复合，在显式可验证的稳定性假设下推导非渐近误差界，分离初始误差收缩与近似误差累积，揭示噪声调度在其中的双重作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一致性模型（CMs）是少步生成高质量样本的主流方法，但增加步数对样本质量的影响高度依赖噪声调度，现有理论无法充分解释这一现象，也缺乏指导采样器设计的精度保证。

Method: 将多步CM采样分解为加噪与近似去噪算子的复合，在显式、可验证的稳定性假设下建立非渐近误差界，使初始化误差的收缩与近似误差的累积得以分离；作为推论给出强对数凹与半对数凹目标下的显式常数；并建立以单步精度与稳定性为假设的互补保证，其假设可在给定训练模型上估计。

Result: 误差界表明调度具有不同作用：早期大噪声水平驱动收缩，后期小噪声水平控制残余偏差；实验验证误差界中的收缩与近似剖面可被可靠测量，且与预测的函数形式高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作为多步一致性模型提供了有意义的收敛理论，并给出可用于采样器设计的实用路径。

Abstract: Consistency models (CMs) have become a leading approach for generating high-quality samples in few steps. However, adding steps can improve or degrade sample quality in ways that are highly sensitive to the schedule and that existing theory does not fully explain. To provide accuracy guarantees and guide CM sampler design, we analyze multistep CM sampling as a composition of noising and approximate denoising operators. Under explicit, verifiable stability assumptions, we derive a non-asymptotic error bound that separates contraction of the initialization error from accumulation of approximation error. The bound assigns distinct roles to the schedule: large early noise levels drive contraction, while small late noise levels control the residual bias. As a corollary, we obtain explicit constants for strongly log-concave and semi-log-concave targets. We further establish a complementary guarantee whose assumptions, one-step accuracy and stability, can be estimated for a given trained model. Experiments show that the contraction and approximation profiles entering our bounds can be reliably measured and closely match the predicted functional forms. Together, these results provide a meaningful convergence theory for multi-step CMs and a practical route to sampler design.

</details>


### [12] [When Is Accuracy Evidence? A Unified Theory of Generalisation, Validation, and Information Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03465)
*JM Gorriz*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 γ-CUBV 统一框架，通过累积量包络 γ(λ) 控制泛化间隙的矩生成函数，将 K 折交叉验证的点估计替换为真风险的保守上界，并覆盖 Hoeffding、Bernstein、依赖感知、PAC-Bayes 与异构源融合等多种设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K 折交叉验证被广泛用作样本外性能证据，但在异构数据下各折既不独立、信息量也不均等；同时，仅凭 CV 点估计难以直接给出风险陈述，且增大 K 未必增加统计证据。需要一种能显式分离观测性能、不确定性、依赖、模型复杂度与置信度的统一验证准则。

Method: 以指数框架推广 CUBV：用累积量包络 γ(λ) 约束泛化间隙的矩生成函数，导出一族风险上界。对 K 折 CV，用联合次高斯代理矩阵建模折间依赖，在等相关假设下得到有效折数 Keff = K/[1+(K-1)ρ]。框架进一步扩展到预测器后验分布与加权多源融合，其中权重通过最小化未来风险上界而非仅经验误差来选择。实验使用异构多模态高斯混合上的已训练线性分类器，以覆盖率和紧致度评估各类界。

Result: 在低维小样本设置中，K 折划分可能因各折欠代表少数模态而增加不确定性，而经风险校正的重代入法仍可保持有效且更紧；当样本量增大后该效应消失。等相关下有效折数公式表明，折间强依赖时增大 K 不一定提升统计证据。

Conclusion: γ-CUBV 将观测性能、不确定性、依赖、模型复杂度与置信度拆分为显式项，为从 CV 分数到风险陈述提供统一路径，并为神经影像等异构小样本应用给出有原则的验证准则。

Abstract: K-fold cross-validation (CV) is widely used as evidence of out-of-sample performance, although folds are neither independent experiments nor equally informative under heterogeneous data. Cross Upper-Bound Validation (CUBV) replaces point-wise CV accuracy by conservative upper bounds on true risk. Here we generalise CUBV through a single exponential framework in which the moment-generating function of the generalisation gap is controlled by a cumulant envelope gamma(lambda). This yields a family of risk bounds covering Hoeffding-, Bernstein-, dependency-aware, PAC-Bayesian, and heterogeneous source-fusion settings. For K-fold CV, dependence between fold-wise gaps is modelled through a joint sub-Gaussian proxy matrix. Under equicorrelation, this gives an effective number of folds, Keff = K/[1+(K-1)rho], showing that increasing K does not necessarily increase statistical evidence when folds are strongly dependent. The framework is also extended to posterior distributions over predictors and weighted multi-source fusion, where weights are selected by minimising an upper bound on future risk rather than empirical error alone. Experiments with trained linear classifiers on heterogeneous multimodal Gaussian mixtures compare K-fold CV with full-sample resubstitution plus risk correction. Bounds are evaluated by coverage and tightness. In low-dimensional small-sample settings, K-fold partitioning can increase uncertainty because individual folds under-represent minority modes, while corrected resubstitution can remain valid and tighter; this effect disappears as sample size increases. Overall, gamma-CUBV separates observed performance, uncertainty, dependence, model complexity, and confidence into explicit terms, providing a unified route from CV scores to risk statements and a principled validation criterion for heterogeneous small-sample applications such as neuroimaging.

</details>


### [13] [AREX: Affine-Residual Exponential Integrator for Few-Step Sampling in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03483)
*Shizheng Lin,Soon Hoe Lim,N. Benjamin Erichson*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: AREX 是一种免训练采样器，通过利用目标的均值与协方差解析求解一部分采样动力学，将速度场分解为仿射分量与神经残差，从而在少步采样下提升生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练 flow matching 模型在少步采样时保真度下降。已有的标量指数积分器只能解析处理各向同性的线性动力学，无法充分利用目标分布的统计结构，因此需要一种能解析处理更一般线性动力学的采样方法。

Method: 作者提出 AREX。首先证明矩匹配高斯目标的速度场是边缘速度场的 L2 最优仿射近似，据此将学习到的动力学沿整条采样路径分解为由目标前两阶矩（均值与协方差）确定的仿射分量与神经残差项。AREX 保留仿射分量并用显式矩阵值传播子（propagator）进行积分，仅对残差项做数值积分。该方式区别于只解析处理各向同性线性动力学的标量指数积分器。

Result: 在图像生成与文生图任务上，AREX 在少步采样情形下持续提升样本保真度，且无需对底层模型进行重新训练。

Conclusion: AREX 表明，利用目标分布前两阶矩解析求解仿射动力学是一种有效且免训练的采样增强策略，可在少步采样下改善生成质量，并推广了传统标量指数积分器的适用范围。

Abstract: We introduce AREX, a training-free sampler for pretrained flow matching models that uses the target mean and covariance to capture an analytically tractable part of the sampling dynamics. We show that the velocity field of the moment-matched Gaussian target is the $L^2$-optimal affine approximation to the marginal velocity field. This motivates decomposition of the learned dynamics into an affine component over the whole sampling path, determined by the first two target moments, and a neural residual term. AREX keeps the affine component and integrates it using an explicit matrix-valued propagator. In turn, we only require to integrate over the residual term. This differs from scalar exponential integrators, which analytically handle only isotropic linear dynamics. Across image and text-to-image generation tasks, AREX consistently improves sample fidelity in the few-step sampling regime without retraining the underlying model.

</details>


### [14] [Amortized Structured Stochastic Variational Inference for Gaussian Process Latent Variable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03647)
*Maksym Tretiakov,Sarah Lucie Filipp,Vincent Fortuin,Ruth Misener,Ruby Sedgwick,James Odgers*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文将摊销结构化随机变分推断引入高斯过程潜变量模型，使潜空间变分后验条件依赖于诱导点取值，从而突破平均场假设，提升流形重构相关指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习方法常需近似数据所在的低维流形，并应刻画该学习流形的认知不确定性。高斯过程潜变量模型（GPLVM）通过从潜空间到观测空间的GP映射来估计流形不确定性，但其不确定性估计受限于GP诱导点与潜变量之间的平均场变分近似，导致后验表达力不足。

Method: 采用摊销结构化随机变分推断（Amortized Structured Stochastic Variational Inference），构造条件依赖于诱导点取值的潜空间变分后验，替代原有的平均场近似，从而刻画诱导点与潜变量之间的后验相关性。

Result: 实验表明，这种更灵活的变分后验在多项与数据流形上点重构相关的指标上均有改善。

Conclusion: 放松平均场假设、引入结构化摊销变分后验可有效提升GPLVM对数据流形重构的表现，为更准确的不确定性估计提供途径。

Abstract: Many machine learning methods aim to approximate the lower-dimensional manifold on which the data lives. A desirable feature of such methods is that they should capture the epistemic uncertainty of this learned manifold. One model that achieves this is the Gaussian Process Latent Variable Model, in which a Gaussian Process (GP) mapping from the latent space provides an estimate of the uncertainty of the manifold. However, the effectiveness of this uncertainty estimation is limited by the mean-field variational approximation between the GP inducing points and the latent variables. In this work, we apply Amortized Structured Stochastic Variational Inference to allow the variational posterior for the latent space to be conditionally dependent on the value of the inducing points. We demonstrate that this more flexible variational posterior improves several metrics relating to the reconstruction of points on the data manifold.

</details>
