### [15] [Hybrid Machine Learning-Assisted Raman Spectroscopy with Generative Feature Augmentation for Pharmaceutical Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02224)
*Quach Thi Thai Binh,Ton Nu Quynh Trang,Thang B. Phan,Vu Thi Hanh Thu,Nguyen Tuan Hung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyMLRaman混合框架：深度特征提取+生成模型+经典分类器，识别6种药物残留，EfficientNet-B3+SVM达96.31%准确率，DDPM特征增强在低数据下对KNN有选择性收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速可靠的药物残留识别对公共健康、食品安全及拉曼光谱筛查应用至关重要；同时需解决常规CNN基线性能有限与标注数据不足的问题。

Method: 将拉曼光谱转为光谱图像，用多种深度骨干网络编码，其中EfficientNet-B3最优，输出1536维嵌入；以此为输入训练SVM、KNN、逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost与ANN，采用分层10折交叉验证；并在PCA降维的EfficientNet-B3隐空间中引入基于DDPM的生成式特征增强，用于低数据消融实验；最后构建交互式拉曼药物分析器验证部署可行性。

Result: EfficientNet-B3–SVM组合取得最强基线：准确率96.31%、宏F1 96.36%，优于单独CNN基线；低数据消融显示DDPM增强效果具有选择性，在训练比例较低时对KNN提升明显，且整体效果依赖分类器类型。

Conclusion: HyMLRaman为拉曼药物快速筛查提供了实用且可解释的技术路线，兼具深度表征能力、经典分类器的稳健性与生成增强的数据适应性。

Abstract: Rapid and reliable identification of pharmaceutical residues is important for safeguarding public health, ensuring food safety, and enabling practical Raman-based screening. In this study, we propose HyMLRaman, a hybrid Raman spectroscopy framework that combines deep spectral feature extraction, generative models, and classical machine-learning classifiers to identify six pharmaceutical compounds, including amoxicillin, chloramphenicol, ciprofloxacin, tetracycline, ibuprofen, and paracetamol. Raman spectra are converted into spectral images and encoded with several deep neural-network backbones, among which EfficientNet-B3 yields the most effective representation. The resulting 1536-dimensional embeddings are then used to train downstream classifiers, including SVM, KNN, logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, and ANN, using stratified 10-fold cross-validation. The hybrid EfficientNet-B3--SVM configuration achieves the strongest baseline performance, reaching 96.31% accuracy and a macro-F1 score of 96.36%, outperforming the standalone CNN baseline. To address limited-data conditions, a generative model, a DDPM-based feature augmentation, is introduced in a PCA-reduced EfficientNet-B3 latent space. The low-data ablation results show that DDPM augmentation provides selective benefits, particularly for KNN with reduced training fractions, and that its effect remains classifier-dependent. Finally, an application-level Raman Pharmaceutical Analyzer demonstrates the feasibility of embedding the trained model into an interactive Raman analysis workflow. These results suggest that HyMLRaman provides a practical and interpretable route for rapid Raman-based pharmaceutical screening.

</details>


### [16] [The Price of Greenwashing: Algorithmic Verification and Market Discipline using Conformal Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02225)
*Sourav Bose,Taoufik Bouraoui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过融合SEC财务基本面与EPA设施级温室气体登记数据，运用梯度提升与Mondrian保形预测构建企业排放的“物理基线”，提出Conformal-Weighted Continuous Divergence (CWCD)指标量化自报数据与算法基线的偏差；跨期lead-lag计量结果显示该偏差与后续Tobin's Q及ROA显著负相关，证明机构资本会对环境数据欺骗进行定价，为算法化审计基础设施提供量化依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业可持续性监管不断扩展，但金融体系高度依赖企业自报排放数据，导致“漂绿”风险普遍存在。现有文献主要依赖主观ESG评级或文本情感分析，缺乏以客观计量方法量化物理气候现实的研究空白，因此需要解决这类信息不对称问题。

Method: 将美国SEC财务基本面数据与EPA设施级温室气体登记数据进行融合，构建具有数学保证的企业物理排放基线；采用梯度提升（gradient boosting）架构结合Mondrian保形预测（Conformal Prediction），将自报数据与算法基线的缺口量化为CWCD指标；再通过横截面lead-lag计量设计评估该偏差与市场结果的关联。

Result: 算法识别的排放偏差与后续市场估值（Tobin's Q）及经营盈利能力（ROA）呈显著负相关，且关系稳健、统计显著。该结果否定了“市场盲视”假说，表明机构资本已将环境数据欺骗定价为根本性公司治理失当的领先指标，而非单纯的伦理瑕疵。

Conclusion: 研究为资产管理者和监管者大规模部署算法审计基础设施提供了必要的量化依据，说明客观排放基线可用于识别并定价环境数据偏差，从而强化市场纪律。

Abstract: While corporate sustainability mandates are expanding, the systemic reliance on self-reported emissions data exposes financial markets to pervasive greenwashing. Current literature relies heavily on subjective ESG ratings or textual sentiment analysis, leaving a critical econometric gap in objectively quantifying physical climate realities. To resolve this information asymmetry, we fuse U.S. SEC financial fundamentals with facility-level EPA greenhouse gas registries to establish a mathematically guaranteed baseline of physical corporate emissions. Leveraging a gradient boosting architecture and Mondrian Conformal Prediction, we quantify the shortfall between self-reported data and this algorithmic baseline into a novel Conformal-Weighted Continuous Divergence (CWCD) metric. Evaluating this divergence via a cross-sectional lead-lag econometric design, we uncover a robust mechanism of market discipline: algorithmic emissions divergence exhibits a severe, statistically significant negative relationship with subsequent market valuation (Tobin's Q) and operational profitability (ROA). Providing definitive evidence against the market blindness hypothesis, this study proves that institutional capital actively prices environmental deception not merely as an ethical lapse, but as a leading indicator of fundamental corporate mismanagement. Ultimately, these findings provide the quantitative justification necessary for asset managers and regulators to deploy algorithmic auditing infrastructure at scale.

</details>


### [17] [State-Space Unlearning for Non-Stationary Bias in Land Surface Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02248)
*Anidipta Pal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SSU-LSF，首个面向地理科学Mamba状态空间模型的机器遗忘框架，用于消除地表预测模型中吸收的非平稳干扰事件（如灌溉激增、大坝调度变化、传感器重校准），在三个基准上实现0.773–0.859的干扰削减率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行中的地表预测系统基于Mamba族结构化状态空间模型，会把非平稳的混杂事件（未记录的灌溉激增、大坝调度变化、传感器重校准）吸收进状态转移矩阵中；即使物理成因结束后，这些事件仍会长期悄然偏置NDVI、LST和作物物候预测。现有机器遗忘方法并非针对地理科学Mamba型SSM设计，无法有效处理此类时空混淆。

Method: 提出SSU-LSF框架：1）通过闭式矩阵指数梯度，将EKFac影响函数专门化到Mamba状态矩阵；2）用谱半径加权的肘部阈值法定位时间混淆足迹Φ；3）在KL散度信赖域内进行无Hessian投影梯度上升，并加入空间全变差（TV）正则。理论方面，命题1给出残余混淆的上界为O((1-ρ(Ā)^{T_c})/((1-ρ(Ā))μ))，随窗口长度T_c增长。

Result: 在三个异构基准与十一个基线上，SSU-LSF的混淆削减率分别为CropHarvest 0.773、NDVI-LST 0.821、ERA5 0.859；最坏情况下干净域RMSE退化仅4.2%（ERA5）；3–5个epoch收敛，每次遗忘请求的GPU成本比全量重训练低8.4倍。

Conclusion: SSU-LSF是首个面向地理科学Mamba型SSM的机器遗忘框架，能高效、低代价地消除非平稳混淆事件对地表预测的长期偏置，同时保持干净域性能，并提供了残余混淆的理论界。

Abstract: Operational land surface forecasting systems built on Mamba-family Structured State Space Models absorb non-stationary confounding events (unrecorded irrigation booms, dam-operation shifts, sensor recalibrations) into their state-transition matrices, silently biasing NDVI, LST, and crop phenology predictions long after the physical cause ends. This paper introduces SSU-LSF (State-Space Unlearning for Land Surface Forecasting), the first machine-unlearning framework purpose-built for geoscientific Mamba-based SSMs. We develop EKFac influence functions specialized to the Mamba state matrices via a closed-form matrix-exponential gradient, use spectral-radius-weighted elbow thresholding to localize a temporal confounding footprint $Φ$, and apply Hessian-free projected gradient ascent within a KL-divergence trust region augmented by spatial total-variation (TV) regularization. Proposition 1 establishes that residual confounding is bounded by $\mathcal{O}\big((1-ρ(\bar{A})^{T_c})/((1-ρ(\bar{A}))μ)\big)$, which grows with the window length $T_c$. Across three heterogeneous benchmarks and eleven baselines, SSU-LSF achieves confounding reduction rates of $0.773$ (CropHarvest), $0.821$ (NDVI-LST), and $0.859$ (ERA5), with worst-case clean-domain RMSE degradation of $4.2\%$ on ERA5, converging in 3--5 epochs at $8.4\times$ lower GPU-cost per unlearning request than full retraining. Code: https://github.com/Anidipta/SSU-LSF

</details>


### [18] [S$^{2}$-PINN: Stochastic Separable Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03303)
*Zhendong Li,Akwum Onwunta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出S²-PINN，用可分离低秩结构表示随机PDE解，实现高效UQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机PDE的不确定性量化在计算科学与工程中广泛存在，但经典谱方法受维数灾难困扰，现有神经求解器又常忽略使矩估计与校准可处理的随机结构。

Method: S²-PINN将解u(t,x,Z)表示为可学习高斯空间字典、Fourier时间特征与gPC随机基的耦合，并通过低秩CP张量分解核心连接；采用强形式与gPC投影残差混合损失训练。

Result: 理论上证明可分离类在温和条件下于L²稠密，投影残差等价于随机Galerkin约束，小批量投影系数对gPC模态数呈对数依赖，正交惩罚控制空间字典条件数；四个制造基准上均值、方差精度与校准优于九个基线且参数更少，并在非制造Poisson/Darcy、随机Navier-Stokes、高维扩散缩放及两个随机反问题中展现泛化能力。

Conclusion: 结果支持随机可分离性作为物理信息神经UQ的有效设计原则。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) for random partial differential equations (PDEs) is ubiquitous in computational science and engineering. However, classical spectral solvers for this class of problems face the curse of dimensionality, and existing neural solvers often ignore the stochastic structure that makes moments and calibration tractable. We introduce a stochastic separable physics-informed neural network, dubbed S$^{2}$-PINN, that represents the solution $u(t,\mathbf{x},\mathbf{Z})$ of a random PDE with a learnable Gaussian spatial dictionary, Fourier temporal features, and a generalized polynomial chaos (gPC) stochastic basis, coupled by a low-rank Canonical Polyadic (CP) tensor decomposition core. The method is trained with a hybrid strong-form and gPC-projected residual loss. Our theoretical analysis establishes that the separable class is dense in $L^2$ under mild conditions, and the projected residual corresponds exactly to a stochastic Galerkin constraint. Furthermore, we show that mini-batch projection coefficients are logarithmically dependent on the number of gPC modes, and that the orthogonality penalty controls the conditioning of the learned spatial dictionary. Using four manufactured random PDE benchmarks, we show that S$^{2}$-PINN outperforms nine baselines in terms of mean and variance accuracy, as well as calibration, while using significantly fewer parameters. Further evaluations on non-manufactured Poisson and Darcy problems, a stochastic Navier--Stokes problem, a diffusion scaling study of higher random dimensions, and two stochastic inverse problems reveal the generalization capabilities of the proposed structure. Together, these results support stochastic separability as an effective design principle for physics-informed neural UQ. The code for the experiments can be found in https://github.com/DMax1314/s2pinn

</details>


### [19] [Nearest-neighbour baselines for fingerprint prediction from MS/MS spectra under different assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02249)
*Ling Min Serena Khoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了多种最近邻检索变体，分析不同推理假设对MS/MS谱预测分子指纹性能的影响，旨在建立更严格的基线以支持更严谨的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，最近邻检索是MS/MS谱预测分子指纹的强基线，部分变体可媲美甚至超越深度学习模型。但"最近邻"实为一族方法，其在推理阶段假设可得的信息各不相同，这种差异对性能的影响此前缺乏系统考察。

Method: 系统比较若干最近邻检索变体，明确并区分各变体在推理时假设可用的信息条件，评估这些不同假设对预测性能的影响。

Result: 不同最近邻变体因推理假设差异而表现出不同的性能；这些假设显著影响检索方法的表现。

Conclusion: 建立更严格的基线，以实现更严谨的基准测试，更准确地衡量该领域的进展。

Abstract: It has recently been shown that nearest-neighbour retrieval provides a strong baseline for molecular fingerprint prediction from MS/MS spectra, with several variants matching or outperforming current deep learning models (Khoo and Barzilay, 2026; Liu et al., 2026; Gupta et al., 2026). Importantly, "nearest neighbour" encompasses a family of retrieval methods that differ in the information assumed to be available at inference. In this report, we systematically compare several nearest-neighbour variants and show how these differing assumptions affect performance. Our goal is to establish stricter baselines that enable more rigorous benchmarking and better measure progress in this area.

</details>


### [20] [Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling for Diffusion Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02251)
*Marcello Bullo,Yanxiao Liu,Öykü Sıla Güner,Arpan Mukherjee,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RASS（秩感知推测采样），一种基于秩感知列表耦合的扩散推测草稿树验证规则，在多种生成任务上优于 D-GRS，CIFAR-10 上增益约 20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成中，推测采样通过并行验证廉价草稿状态来加速，同时保持目标分布。近期基于树的方法（如 Diffusion Greedy Rejection Sampling，D-GRS）比单链草稿更有效地分配并行计算预算。但 D-GRS 按生成顺序顺序检验各节点上 K 个条件独立候选，未利用候选本身蕴含的有信息排序，而这些排序无需额外目标模型评估即可获得。

Method: 提出 Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling（RASS）：一种针对推测草稿树的秩感知列表耦合验证规则。RASS 沿提议-目标均值位移对草稿候选排序，并按优化权重采样一个秩，以最小化所选提议分布与目标分布之间的全变差；随后将所选候选与目标最大耦合，并通过残差校正保证任意秩权重下的精确采样。

Result: 在高斯混合目标、FFHQ 无条件像素空间生成、CIFAR-10 条件生成，以及使用 COCO2014 提示的 Stable Diffusion 3.5 潜扩散上评估。以标准采样与推测采样的目标模型评估次数之比衡量，RASS 在各设定上均优于 D-GRS，在匹配计算预算下 CIFAR-10 上增益约达 20%。

Conclusion: RASS 通过利用草稿候选的秩信息改进推测采样验证，无需额外目标评估即可提升加速比，并在多种扩散生成任务中一致优于 D-GRS，同时保持精确采样。

Abstract: Speculative sampling accelerates diffusion generation by verifying inexpensive draft states in parallel while preserving the target law. Recent tree-based methods allocate the parallel compute budget more effectively than single-chain drafts, as demonstrated by Diffusion Greedy Rejection Sampling (D-GRS). D-GRS generates $K$ conditionally independent candidates per node, and sequentially tests them in their generation order. Yet the sampled candidates admit an informative ranking without additional target-model evaluations. To exploit this, we introduce Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling (RASS), a verification rule for speculative draft trees based on rank-aware list coupling. RASS orders draft candidates along the proposal-target mean displacement and samples a rank with weights optimized to minimize total variation between the selected-proposal and target laws. Finally, the selected candidate is maximally coupled with the target, with residual correction ensuring exact sampling for any choice of rank weights. We evaluate RASS on a Gaussian-mixture target, unconditional pixel-space generation on FFHQ, conditional generation on CIFAR-10, and latent diffusion with Stable Diffusion 3.5 using COCO2014 prompts. Measured by the ratio of standard to speculative sampling's target-model evaluation counts, RASS improves on D-GRS across the evaluated settings, with gains reaching approximately 20% on CIFAR-10 at matched compute budgets.

</details>


### [21] [Counterfactual Predictions in Scientific Emulators Without Controlled Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02252)
*Dingling Yao,Kahaan Gandhi,Valentin Duruisseaux,Boris Bonev,Francesco Locatello,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReRoute：在无需受控干预数据的情况下，将事实数据与部分机制知识结合，实现目标性科学"what-if"预测的反事实推断框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学问题需要推理从未被观测的情形（若条件、干预或历史不同会如何）。模型在观测数据上预测准确，但当相关输入被独立变动时，在what-if查询上失败。常见补救是添加受控模拟数据显式解耦因素，但这需要模拟器、计算昂贵，并继承模拟器建模假设。

Method: ReRoute将预训练骨干网络的被查询输入固定为参考值，通过已知机制路径重新引入其变化，并在原始事实数据上微调，将下游效应留给学习到的动力学。作者在明确结构假设下给出因果可识别性结果，核心论证在Lean中机器验证。

Result: 在可获精确响应的受控平流-扩散系统中，ReRoute实现高精度反事实预测。在先进气候仿真中，在留出的耦合气候干预上，严重CO2分布漂移下ReRoute将总体气候误差降低18.2-31.8%，同时保持标准条件下技能，成本仅为在额外受控模拟上重训练的一小部分。在基于历史ERA5再分析训练的仿真器上（无反事实参考），ReRoute在固定CO2反事实下保留更多由观测边界条件隐含的地表增温。

Conclusion: ReRoute无需受控干预数据进行适配，即可结合事实数据与部分机制知识实现目标性科学what-if预测，在理论与气候仿真实验中均验证有效且成本低廉。

Abstract: Many scientific questions require reasoning about what was never observed: What if the conditions, interventions, or history had been different? Models can predict accurately on observed data yet fail on such what-if queries when correlated inputs are varied independently. A common remedy is to add controlled simulation data in which these factors are explicitly disentangled, but this requires access to a simulator, can be computationally expensive, and inherits the simulator's modeling assumptions. We introduce ReRoute, a framework for targeted scientific what-if prediction that combines factual data with partial mechanistic knowledge, without requiring controlled intervention data for adaptation. ReRoute fixes the queried input of a pretrained backbone to a reference value, reintroduces its variation through a known mechanistic pathway, and fine-tunes on the original factual data, while leaving downstream effects to the learned dynamics. We provide a causal identification result for this construction under explicit structural assumptions, with the core argument machine-checked in Lean. After showing that ReRoute achieves highly accurate counterfactual predictions in a controlled advection-diffusion system where exact responses are available, we turn to state-of-the-art climate emulation. On held-out coupled-climate interventions, ReRoute reduces aggregate climate error by 18.2-31.8% under severe CO$_2$ distribution shifts while preserving skill under standard conditions, at a small fraction of the cost of retraining on additional controlled simulations, without even accounting for the substantial expense of generating such data. Finally, on an emulator trained from historical ERA5 reanalysis, where no counterfactual reference exists, ReRoute preserves substantially more of the surface warming implied by the observed boundary conditions under a fixed-CO$_2$ counterfactual.

</details>


### [22] [Approximation Property of Dropout Neural Networks: Sobolev Rates and Confidence Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02253)
*Jia-He Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带Dropout的ReLU网络对Sobolev单位球的近似，给出高概率单个采样网络下的上下界，并刻画存活边数、精度与置信度之间的匹配关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用逼近性只说明存在性，未刻画随机实现下达到给定精度所需的网络规模；需研究边的随机保留（Dropout）对近似误差与置信度的影响。

Method: 以概率p独立保留边，误差在输入域上一致度量，要求以至少1-δ概率对单个采样网络成立；构造固定深度、规模为Õ_{n,d}(p^{-9}ε^{-max{d/n,2}}log(1/δ))的网络，结合有界局部子网络、成功近似事件上的局部化及多尺度Taylor分解；下界利用Sobolev容量与固定仿射函数的输出层代价。

Result: 上界给出了具体规模；下界指出存活边数受Sobolev容量限制，固定仿射函数需输出层代价((1-p)/p)ε^{-2}log(1/δ)。对固定p∈(0,1)且δ<min{1/2,1-p}，在固定或对数深度预算下精度指数匹配；当d≤2n时置信度也匹配（忽略精度对数因子）。并将下界推广至W^{n,r}目标与L^s误差，区别于W^{n,∞}的上界。

Conclusion: 在给定条件下，Dropout ReLU网络的近似精度指数上下界匹配，部分情形置信度亦匹配；最优保留概率依赖与对数因子仍待解决。

Abstract: The universal approximation property of dropout neural networks does not by itself describe the network size required for an accurate random realization. In this work, we study approximation of the unit ball of $W^{n,\infty}([0,1]^d)$ by ReLU networks whose edges are retained independently with probability $p$. The approximation error is measured uniformly over the input domain, and the guarantee holds with probability at least $1-δ$ for a single sampled network. We construct networks of constant depth and size $\widetilde O_{n,d}(p^{-9}\varepsilon^{-\max\{d/n,2\}} \log(1/δ))$. The construction combines bounded local subnetworks, localization on a successful approximation event, and a multiscale Taylor decomposition. Conversely, Sobolev capacity imposes a lower bound on the number of surviving edges, while approximation of a fixed affine function requires an output-layer cost of order $((1-p)/p)\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/δ)$ at sufficiently high confidence. For fixed $p\in(0,1)$ and $δ<\min\{1/2,1-p\}$, the upper and lower bounds match in the accuracy exponent under a fixed or logarithmic depth budget. When $d\leq2n$, they also match in confidence up to logarithms of accuracy. We extend the lower bounds to $W^{n,r}$ targets with $L^s$ error, and distinguish this extension from the upper bound for $W^{n,\infty}$. The optimal retention dependence and logarithmic factors remain open.

</details>


### [23] [Overcoming Challenges of Interpretive Structural Modeling with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02254)
*Everett Rush,David J. Icove,Ari Kim,Byung H. Park,Michael A. Langston*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将大语言模型(LLM)与解释结构模型(ISM)结合，用LLM作为"不完美专家"自动发现因果关系图，以替代传统依赖专家反复交互的繁琐建模过程，并比较了四种图发现方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ISM建模需与领域专家反复交互直至达成共识，过程繁琐、人力密集，且难以扩展到包含数百个变量的研究。作者希望借助已有的大规模语言模型因果图发现工作，让LLM充当"不完美专家"，从而克服ISM的可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出集成的LLM-ISM框架，利用LLM自动发现因果关系图。对比评估了四种图发现方法：逐对(pairwise)、k元(k-wise)、逐行(rowwise)以及全图(full graph)发现方法，并以SHD和F1-score作为评价指标。

Result: 实验结果表明，用于ISM的因果图发现方法在逐行方法(SHD=160, F1=0.77)和全图方法(SHD=135, F1=0.73)上表现最佳。

Conclusion: LLM-ISM集成方法可行，其中逐行与全图发现方法效果最优(SHD更低、F1更高)，为ISM在大规模变量研究中的自动化建模提供了途径。

Abstract: Interpretive Structural Modeling (ISM) is a well-known process for multi-criteria decision making. The success of ISM over other methodologies is its ability to model causal relationships, the binary scale of factors, and resulting hierarchical representation. Traditionally, the modeling process is performed by repeated interactions with subject matter experts until consensus is reached. This process is tedious, labor-intense, and most importantly limits the ability of ISM to scale to studies with hundreds of variables. Drawing on existing work of causal graph discovery with large language models (LLM) as imperfect experts, this work explores an integrated LLM-ISM approach for ISM. Pairwise, k-wise, rowwise, and full graph discovery methodologies are compared and evaluated. It is shown that causal graph discovery methods for ISM perform best using rowwise (SHD=160, F1-score=0.77) and full graph methods (SHD=135, F1-score=0.73).

</details>


### [24] [MACTS-EM: Multi-Agent Collaborative Time Series Forecasting with Emergent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02255)
*Ahmad Shahi,Mamehgol Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MACTS-EM框架，通过多智能体协作与涌现记忆实现时间序列预测，在金融、气候、能源及疫情传播等场景中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测在诸多领域仍是关键挑战。现有方法难以应对机制切换（regime shifts）、跨域知识迁移和多模态数据整合等复杂现象。

Method: MACTS-EM架构整合五个组件：(1) 领域专门化预测智能体，负责模式识别、异常检测、因果推断与不确定性量化；(2) 元认知层，用于动态智能体分配；(3) 涌现记忆机制，支持跨域模式迁移；(4) 多模态上下文整合；(5) 对抗鲁棒性组件。

Result: 在金融市场、气候模式、能源消耗和疫情传播上的评估显示，MACTS-EM在多数场景下优于现有方法：预测精度提升8-12%，零样本迁移能力提升22-27%，机制切换期间韧性增强16-21%，分布漂移后恢复速度加快15-18%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，协作式智能体方法在时间序列预测上代表了超越传统架构的有前景方向，尤其适用于需要多分辨率时间理解与上下文适应的复杂现实场景。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains a critical challenge across numerous domains. Despite significant advancements, existing approaches struggle with complex phenomena such as regime shifts, cross-domain knowledge transfer, and multimodal data integration. This paper introduces Multi-Agent Collaborative Time Series Forecasting with Emergent Memory (MACTS-EM), a novel framework where specialised agents collaborate to achieve superior forecasting performance. The MACTS-EM architecture integrates: (1) domain-specialised forecasting agents for pattern recognition, anomaly detection, causal inference, and uncertainty quantification; (2) a meta-cognitive layer for dynamic agent allocation; (3) an emergent memory mechanism enabling cross-domain pattern transfer; (4) multimodal contextual integration; and (5) adversarial robustness components. Evaluation across financial markets, climate patterns, energy consumption, and pandemic propagation demonstrates that MACTS-EM outperforms existing approaches in most scenarios, with 8-12% improvement in forecasting accuracy, 22-27% better zero-shot transfer capability, 16-21% enhanced resilience during regime shifts, and 15-18% faster recovery after distribution shifts. Our findings suggest that collaborative, agentic approaches to time series forecasting represent a promising direction beyond traditional architectures, particularly for complex real-world scenarios requiring multi-resolution temporal understanding and contextual adaptation.

</details>


### [25] [TRACE: A Reproducible Benchmark for Electricity Price Forecasting with Official Operational Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02256)
*Xinyi Yi,Moy Yuan,Ioannis Lestas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE基准，将电价预测与预测时点可得的官方运营文本配对，构建7,300个区域—日样本，验证文本上下文对电价预测的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电价预测（EPF）基准主要聚焦数值输入，预测时点可得的文本上下文对预测的贡献尚未被充分评估，缺乏可复现的评测基准。

Method: 构建TRACE基准，覆盖美国某主要电力市场五个区域，共7,300个区域—日实例，将电价与预测截断点的官方运营文本配对；通过在每个截断点重建官方运营文本以避免截断后信息泄漏；开展语义对齐评估与预测价值评估，并进行受控的跨日文本错配消融实验。

Result: 语义评估显示文本与真实价格的中心走势及两侧尾部风险均对齐，其中上尾价格风险最为一致；预测价值体现在时间序列基础模型上，上尾pinball损失中位数降低7.4%；跨日文本错配消融使增益逆转，甚至低于无文本基线。

Conclusion: 预测时点文本上下文对电价预测具有实质价值，尤其体现在上尾风险预测；TRACE为无信息泄漏的文本增强EPF评测提供了可复现基准。

Abstract: Electricity price forecasting (EPF) supports scheduling, bidding, and risk management in electricity markets, yet existing benchmarks focus mainly on numerical inputs, leaving the forecasting value of forecast-time textual context insufficiently evaluated. We introduce TRACE, a reproducible benchmark of 7,300 zone--day instances pairing prices from five zones in a major U.S. market with official operational text available at the forecast cutoff. TRACE reconstructs official operational text at each cutoff, preventing post-cutoff information leakage. We evaluate TRACE for semantic alignment and forecasting value. Semantic assessments align with central movement and both tail risks in ground-truth prices, most consistently for upper-tail price risk. Forecasting value is reflected in a median 7.4\% reduction in upper-tail pinball loss across time-series foundation models. A controlled cross-day text-mismatch ablation reverses the gains, falling below the no-text baseline.

</details>


### [26] [Parameter-Free Interval-Dynamic Regret under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02258)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究每轮仅一个无偏随机次梯度的在线凸优化，噪声条件p阶矩未知（1<p≤2）。提出一种无需G、σ、p、区间I及路径量的自适应算法，使其在任意长度n区间与比较器路径上的遗憾界由GDn与含√n项、n^{1/p}项的表达式取最小，实现区间自适应与均值梯度/噪声指数的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有在线凸优化成果通常针对固定区间或已知噪声矩，难以在有限条件p阶矩未知、比较器复杂度与观测尺度变化下同时达到最优。本文旨在设计同时适应区间长度与比较器路径复杂度、且不依赖问题参数的学习器。

Method: 每轮使用一个无偏随机次梯度；通过控制校准的期望值与限制观测尺度变化带来的代价，构造基于预测可用专家的非均匀先验的分布比较定理，采用相对熵依赖通用定理；常用先验偏好长窗口与长重启长度；结合改变测度下界，刻画保留全时域最优保证的学习器的对数噪声指数。

Result: 对任意长度n区间I与比较器路径Λ_I=1+P_I/D，期望遗憾≤min(GDn, C[GD√(n(Λ_I+log²(2T))) + σD n^{1/p}(Λ_I+log²(2T))^{(p−1)/p}])，常数通用；结合统计后区间代价为1+log(T/n)，可退化为最优全时域静态率；给出改变测度下界与实际条件。

Conclusion: 结果在无参数先验设置下统一了区间自适应、比较器复杂度与随机噪声指数，并匹配静态与确定性划分情形，说明方法的通用性与最优性界限。

Abstract: We study online convex optimization with one unbiased stochastic subgradient per round and an unknown finite conditional $p$th noise moment, $1<p\le2$. For every fixed interval $I$ of length $n$ and comparator path with $Λ_I=1+P_I/D$, one learner achieves
  \[ E[Regret_I(u)]\le\min(GDn, C[GD\sqrt{n(Λ_I+\log^2(2T))} +σDn^{1/p}(Λ_I+\log^2(2T))^{(p-1)/p}]). \]
  The learner uses none of $G,σ,p,I,P_I$, and the constant is universal. Interval adaptation adds to comparator complexity, preserving the distinct mean-gradient and noise exponents. The analysis controls calibration in expectation and limits the cost of observation-scale changes. Its general theorem compares to distributions over predictably available experts with relative-entropy dependence on a nonuniform prior. A common prior favors long windows and long restart lengths. With the statistics supplied, the interval cost becomes $1+\log(T/n)$, including the optimal full-horizon static rate. A change-of-measure lower bound identifies the noise power of this logarithm for learners retaining a full-horizon optimal guarantee, under explicit conditions. Static comparisons and deterministic partitions follow from the same decisions.

</details>


### [27] [From Mathematical to Executable Certificates for Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02268)
*Ziyu Zhao,Xinyu Wang,Xiaowen Chang,Yixuan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ExecCert发布时认证层，弥合数学保证与实际部署之间的差距：对执行候选关闭原生证书或应用RRV验证保真度；针对冻结表示+可变岭回归头开发增量RRV实现，在四个已发表卸载实现上验证有效性，顺序服务实验中消除错误发布并保持更低认证成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器卸载需在删除请求、过时记录或数据质量问题时移除数据，而从零重训练代价高昂。认证式卸载方法虽提供数学保证，但实际部署系统发布的是软件产生的有限精度具体产物，数学保证与部署产物之间存在鸿沟，需要面向发布时刻的认证机制。

Method: 提出ExecCert发布时认证层，认证拟发布的候选产物：或关闭该方法对执行候选的原生证书，或应用重训练参考发布验证（RRV）认证其与当前保留集重训练的保真度。针对顺序删除中精确参考与存储数值状态分别演化的问题，在冻结表示加可变岭回归头设置下，开发RRV增量实现，跨删除请求维护认证证据而非每次发布重构。

Result: 在四个已发表卸载实现上，ExecCert保持有效证书、改变发布决策、收紧保守界，并识别具体输出所支持的重训练参考保真度。顺序服务实验中，RRV消除由存储方程验证导致的错误发布，同时紧密跟踪实际误差；增量认证在发布检查足够频繁时比全新和持续维护的验证因子方案更便宜。

Conclusion: ExecCert为机器卸载提供了发布时认证层，将数学保证延伸到有限精度执行产物，通过RRV与增量证据维护兼顾保真度验证与效率，适用于顺序删除服务场景。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is needed when data must be removed because of deletion requests, outdated records, or data-quality concerns, while retraining from scratch can be costly. Certified machine unlearning methods provide mathematical guarantees, while deployed systems release concrete finite-precision artifacts produced by software. To bridge the gap between mathematical guarantees and practical deployment, we introduce Executable Release Certification (ExecCert), a release-time layer that certifies the candidate artifact considered for release. ExecCert either closes a method's native certificate for the executed candidate or applies Retraining-Reference Release Verification (RRV) to certify fidelity to current retain-set retraining. Sequential deletion makes the latter nontrivial because the exact retain-set reference and the stored numerical state evolve separately. For frozen representations with a mutable ridge head, we develop an incremental realization of RRV that maintains certified evidence across deletion requests rather than reconstructing it at each release. On four published unlearning implementations, ExecCert preserves valid certificates, changes release decisions, tightens conservative bounds, and identifies the retraining-reference fidelity supported by concrete outputs. In sequential-service experiments, RRV eliminates false releases caused by stored-equation verification while closely tracking realized error, and incremental certification remains cheaper than both fresh and maintained verified-factor alternatives once release checks become sufficiently frequent.

</details>


### [28] [CLEAN: Psychometrically Consistent Incremental Cognitive Diagnosis under Concept-Space Expansion via Architectural Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02278)
*Tao He,Jinxing Xiang,Fan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLEAN是一个支持概念空间扩展的增量认知诊断框架，通过拓扑二分冻结与正交掩码实现历史诊断的严格不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增量认知诊断模型假定概念空间固定，新题引入的新概念会产生梯度覆盖导致灾难性遗忘；且仅靠软约束无法保证增量更新后的诊断不变性（心理测量一致性要求）。

Method: 提出CLEAN框架：（1）严格拓扑二分协议，冻结历史诊断函数并用确定性正交列掩码切断梯度干扰；（2）部署具微方差初始化的可扩展满秩分支以学习新概念；（3）形式化表征漂移（RD）来量化历史特征的扰动，验证结构上即保证不变性。

Result: 在三个大规模教育数据集上，CLEAN实现零RD，旧题指标与静态锚点完全一致，同时在新题上与强持续学习基线相当或更优。

Conclusion: 通过架构隔离实现诊断不变性的结构性保证，兼顾旧题零遗忘与新题性能，为可扩展概念空间的增量认知诊断提供了新范式。

Abstract: Cognitive diagnosis (CD) is a fundamental task in intelligent education that profiles learner proficiency over knowledge concepts. In real-world learning platforms, newly added items continually introduce previously unseen concepts, necessitating dynamic expansion of the underlying concept space. Yet existing incremental CD models assume a fixed concept space, allowing gradients from new items to overwrite historical pathways and induce catastrophic forgetting. More critically, these methods rely solely on soft constraints to preserve historical diagnoses. Such constraints may fail to satisfy the requirement of diagnostic invariance after incremental updates, a requirement known as psychometric consistency in cognitive diagnosis. Therefore, we propose CLEAN (Continual Learning with Expandable and Architecturally Isolated Networks), a novel incremental CD framework supporting concept-space expansion while providing structural guarantees for pointwise invariance of historical diagnoses. Specifically, CLEAN first introduces a strict topological bipartition protocol, freezes historical diagnostic functions and applies deterministic orthogonal column masking to sever gradient interference. Second, to accommodate concept expansion, expandable full-rank branches with micro-variance initialization are deployed to learn novel concepts. Finally, to verify that this architectural design achieves invariance by construction, we formalize Representation Drift (RD) to quantify the perturbation of historical traits. Extensive experiments on three large-scale educational datasets demonstrate that CLEAN achieves zero RD, preserving old-item metrics identically to static anchors through architectural isolation while remaining competitive with or superior to strong continual-learning baselines on new items.

</details>


### [29] [MuLoRA: Spectrally Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02283)
*Junkang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文识别了LoRA持续学习中的"谱可塑性崩溃"问题，并提出MuLoRA，通过历史白化选择任务自适应子空间并对动量更新做近似极正交化，联合控制容量分配与利用，在16项指标中的15项取得最高平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽为持续学习提供参数高效方案，但名义秩会掩盖有效适应能力的丧失：顺序适应中更新能量集中于少数奇异模式，导致低秩空间大量闲置。这说明仅靠避免干扰（保护历史表征）不足以保证剩余适应容量对新任务敏感且被有效利用。

Method: MuLoRA包含两个机制：1）历史白化——识别相对累积历史响应具有强当前任务响应的输入方向，得到任务自适应基，并在训练中保持固定；2）对动量更新做近似极正交化，降低所选空间内的谱集中。二者由正交基连接，将因子更新的谱精确传递到诱导的权重更新。理论上给出精确子空间选择的max-min刻画，并在受控跨步各向异性下推导累积谱界。

Result: 在五个类增量基准、八种增量设置下，MuLoRA在16项报告指标中的15项取得最高平均精度。

Conclusion: MuLoRA表明，持续学习中的参数高效微调需同时管理容量的分配与利用；通过任务自适应子空间选择与谱去集中化，可缓解谱可塑性崩溃，并在广泛增量基准上取得一致优势。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) provides a parameter-efficient approach to continual learning, but its nominal rank can conceal a loss of effective adaptation capacity. We identify \emph{spectral plasticity collapse}: during sequential adaptation, update energy becomes concentrated in a small subset of singular modes, leaving much of the available low-rank space underutilized. This exposes a limitation of interference avoidance alone: protecting historical representations does not ensure that the remaining adaptation capacity is responsive to new tasks or effectively utilized. To address this problem, we propose \texttt{MuLoRA}, which jointly controls capacity allocation and utilization. First, historical whitening identifies input directions with strong current-task response relative to accumulated historical response, yielding a task-adaptive basis that remains fixed during training. Second, approximate polar orthogonalization of momentum updates reduces spectral concentration within theselected space. An orthonormal basis connects these mechanisms by transferring the factor-update spectrum exactly tothe induced weight update. We establish a max--min characterization of exact subspace selection and derive cumulative spectral bounds under controlled cross-step anisotropy. Across five class-incremental benchmarks and eight incremental settings, \texttt{MuLoRA} achieves the highest mean accuracy in 15 of 16 reported metrics.

</details>


### [30] [Effects of interpulse-interval variation on deep-learning classification of bat vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02284)
*Welmoed R. Eversteijn,Burooj Ghani,A. Leonie Baier,Dan Stowell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究通过自然与归一化脉冲间隔（IPI）数据集对比，检验IPI是否提供蝙蝠物种判别信息及Transformer是否比CNN更敏感；结果表明IPI贡献有限，但自然条件下的结果难以完全迁移到归一化条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间上下文可能有助于蝙蝠物种自动分类，但其中具体特征（如脉冲间隔IPI）的贡献尚不明确，且不同架构（Transformer与CNN）对该信息的利用程度未知。

Method: 构建两个匹配数据集：保留原始呼叫时序的自然IPI条件，以及将呼叫起始间隔统一为50 ms的归一化IPI条件。微调EfficientNet-B0与PaSST并在各自条件内评估；另做交叉条件实验（分别训练后在相同自然IPI测试集评估）；最后评估预训练分类器BatDetect2与BAT。

Result: 条件内IPI归一化影响因模型而异：PaSST准确率变化小（71±2.3% vs 70±6.3%），EfficientNet从47±4.7%升至57±3.9%；PaSST在两种条件下均优于EfficientNet。交叉条件评估中，自然IPI训练的模型在自然IPI测试集上更好：EfficientNet从54%降至50%，PaSST从65%降至57%。BatDetect2与BAT在两种条件下差异不大。

Conclusion: 对"自然IPI变异对蝙蝠物种分类贡献显著"及"Transformer比CNN更有效利用该信息"两个假设仅获有限支持；但交叉条件性能下降说明归一化条件下的结果可能无法完全迁移到自然录音。

Abstract: Temporal context may aid automated bat-species classification, but the contribution of specific features remains unclear. We investigated whether variation in the interpulse interval (IPI)-the time between consecutive call onsets-provides species-discriminative information and whether transformer-based models are more sensitive to this information than convolutional neural networks. We created two matched datasets from European bat recordings: a natural-IPI condition retaining the original call timing and a normalized-IPI condition in which call onsets were spaced at 50-ms intervals. EfficientNet-B0 and PaSST were fine-tuned and evaluated within each condition. In an additional experiment, each architecture was trained separately on natural-IPI and normalized-IPI recordings, and evaluated on the same natural-IPI test set. Finally, the pretrained classifiers BatDetect2 and BAT were evaluated on both conditions. Within-condition IPI normalization had model-dependent effects. PaSST accuracy differed little between the natural-IPI ($71 \pm 2.3\%$) and normalized-IPI ($70 \pm 6.3\%$) conditions, whereas EfficientNet accuracy increased from $47 \pm 4.7\%$ to $57 \pm 3.9\%$. PaSST exceeded EfficientNet under both conditions. In the cross-condition evaluation, models trained on natural-IPI recordings outperformed those trained on normalized-IPI recordings on the natural-IPI test set: accuracy decreased from 54% to 50% for EfficientNet and from 65% to 57% for PaSST. BatDetect2 and BAT differed little between IPI conditions. Overall, we found limited support for the hypotheses that natural IPI variation contributes substantially to bat-species classification and that it is used more effectively by transformer-based than CNN-based models. Nevertheless, the cross-condition performance decrease shows that results obtained under normalized conditions may not transfer fully to natural recordings.

</details>


### [31] [Diffusion-Based Synthetic Data Pretraining for Enhancing Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02292)
*E. Riveros,D. Vega-Oliveros,A. Soriano-Vargas,A. Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于CABiGRU模型，利用扩散模型生成合成传感器数据并进行两阶段训练（合成预训练+真实数据微调），在DEO数据集上实现90.6%的平衡准确率，改善了饮食行为识别中少数类欠拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类活动识别（HAR）在医疗、健康与日常监测中日益重要，其中饮食活动（进食、饮水）检测可为饮食习惯与慢性病管理提供可操作洞察。但现有HAR系统在细微且代表性不足的类别上表现不佳，限制了其在真实饮食监测中的实用性，尤其存在类别不平衡导致的少数类欠拟合问题。

Method: 在CABiGRU（结合双向GRU、多头注意力与残差连接的卷积架构，用于处理智能手表加速度计、陀螺仪和磁力计数据）基础上，引入扩散模型生成合成传感器数据窗口，并采用两阶段训练策略：先在合成数据上预训练CABiGRU，再在真实数据上微调，以提升泛化能力并缓解少数类欠拟合。

Result: 在DEO（饮水/进食/其他）数据集上，所提流程达到90.6%的平衡准确率，优于强监督基线，验证了基于扩散的合成预训练对饮食活动识别的有效性，以及在处理类别不平衡方面的进步。

Conclusion: 将扩散生成数据与针对性微调相结合，可增强对饮食行为的鲁棒识别，支持在医疗与营养监测场景中更可靠的部署。

Abstract: Human activity recognition (HAR) is increasingly important for healthcare, well-being, and daily monitoring ap- plications, for which detecting alimentary activities such as eating and drinking can provide actionable insight into dietary habits and chronic disease management. HAR systems, however, often underperform on subtle and underrepresented classes, limiting their utility in real-world dietary monitoring. This work builds upon CABiGRU, a convolutional architecture with Bidirectional GRU layers, multi-head attention, and residual connections, designed to capture discriminative temporal patterns from smart- watch accelerometer, gyroscope, and magnetometer data. To improve CaBiGRU's generalization and reduce underfitting in the minority class, we leverage synthetic sensor data windows using a diffusion model and adopt a two-stage training strategy: pre-training CABiGRU on synthetic data, followed by fine-tuning on the real-world data. On the DEO (drinking/eating/other) dataset, the proposed pipeline achieves a balanced accuracy of 90.6%, improving over a strong supervised baseline and showing the benefits of diffusion-based synthetic pre-training for recognizing alimentary activities and representing a step forward dealing with unbalanced classes. These results suggest that combining diffusion-generated data with targeted fine-tuning enhances robust recognition of dietary behaviors, supporting more reliable deployment in healthcare and nutrition-monitoring settings.

</details>


### [32] [$Ψ$-Resilience: Model-Free Feature Importance from 1D Topological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02299)
*Fabian Galis,Darian Onchis,Pedro Real Jurado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Ψ-Resilience，一种无模型特征重要性方法：通过1D拓扑信号从数据本身导出解释，利用类间密度差异景观的0维持久性构建韧性泛函，得到上下文鲁棒的重要性分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征重要性方法多依赖预测模型（如SHAP），缺乏不依赖模型的、可从数据分布层面严格审计的解释手段。

Method: 估计类条件密度，沿特征轴取逐点绝对差构建"类分歧景观"（1D信号）；对该1D信号计算0维持久性，定义韧性泛函，仅聚合在用户设定鲁棒性尺度内仍存活的拓扑特征，从而得到可审计的鲁棒重要性分数。

Result: 在具有已知真值重要性的合成数据上，特征排序恢复度高，Spearman相关最高达0.8，与SHAP、互信息等方法竞争力相当；在无真值的真实数据集上与这些方法一致，相关性最高达0.9。

Conclusion: Ψ-Resilience是一种稳定的解释方法，无需依赖预测模型即可实现严格、分布层面的特征重要性审计。

Abstract: We introduce $Ψ$-Resilience, a model-free feature importance method that derives explanations directly from the data itself via 1D topological signals. Our method constructs a class-disagreement landscape by estimating class-conditional densities and taking their pointwise absolute difference along the feature axis. Then, the 0-dimensional persistence of this 1D signal defines a resilience functional that aggregates only those topological features that survive perturbations up to a robustness scale which is set by the user. This gives us a context-robust importance score that is inherently auditable via the underlying 1D landscapes and their persistence. We evaluate our method on both synthetic and real datasets. On synthetic generators with specified ground-truth importance, $Ψ$-Resilience recovers the ranking of features with high fidelity, achieving Spearman rank correlations up to 0.8 and performing competitively with multiple feature importance methods, including SHAP and mutual information. On real datasets with no known ground truth, our technique agrees with these methods, with correlations up to 0.9. These results show that $Ψ$-Resilience is a stable explanation method that enables rigorous, distribution-level auditing of feature importance without relying on a predictive model.

</details>


### [33] [Differential Privacy of Gradient Descent on Perturbed Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02716)
*Austin Watkins,Raman Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对正则化经验风险加随机线性项后释放确定性梯度下降第N次迭代所得的有限计算差分隐私性，证明在强凸光滑且Hessian Lipschitz条件下映射为C^1微分同胚，从而给出无显式环境维度因子的隐私轮廓界与泛化风险界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标扰动(objective perturbation)方法通常释放精确最小化点以保证隐私，但实际优化只能得到有限次迭代解，有限迭代与精确最小化之间的隐私与效用差距缺乏精细刻画。作者希望为有限迭代输出建立严格的隐私分析，并消除隐私界中显式的环境维度因子。

Method: 对 F(w;S)+⟨z,w⟩ 做确定性梯度下降，其中 z~N(0,σ²I_d) 仅在最优化前抽样一次，输出第 N 次迭代 w_N。在强凸、光滑、Hessian Lipschitz 的假设下，证明 z↦w_N 在隐私论证所用的有界域上是 C^1 微分同胚，并给出其 Jacobian 最小奇异值的定量下界，从而可对有限迭代直接做变量替换分析。

Result: 对广义线性模型，当迭代条件成立时，所得隐私轮廓(privacy-profile)界不含显式环境维度 d 因子，且有限迭代修正项几何递减；令自由截断参数随 N 缓慢增长，可在极限下恢复精确最小化点的隐私证明。期望超额经验风险被界定为 dσ²/(2μ) 加上几何递减的优化项，并可迁移到总体风险，且主导统计项中不含额外的条件数乘性因子。

Conclusion: 有限次梯度下降迭代的差分隐私与效用可由变量替换严格分析：隐私保证在迭代条件满足时摆脱环境维度显式依赖，有限迭代误差几何收敛并渐近恢复精确最小化证明，效用界保持最优统计率而无需条件数放大。

Abstract: Objective perturbation adds a random linear term to a regularized empirical risk and releases the exact perturbed minimizer. We study the finite computation obtained by releasing the $N$-th iterate of deterministic gradient descent on $w\mapsto F(w;S)+\langle z,w\rangle$, where $z\sim\mathcal N(0,σ^2I_d)$ is drawn once before optimization. For strongly convex and smooth objectives with Lipschitz Hessian, we prove an explicit condition under which the map $z\mapsto w_N$ is a $C^1$-diffeomorphism on the bounded domains used in the privacy argument, with a quantitative lower bound on the smallest singular value of its Jacobian. This permits a direct change-of-variables analysis of the finite iterate. For generalized linear models, the resulting privacy-profile bound has no explicit ambient-dimension factor once the iteration condition holds, and its finite-iteration correction decreases geometrically. By letting the free truncation parameter grow slowly with $N$, we recover the corresponding exact-minimizer certificate in the limit. We also bound the expected excess empirical risk by $dσ^2/(2μ)$ plus a geometrically decreasing optimization term, and transfer the result to population risk without an additional multiplicative condition-number factor in the leading statistical terms.

</details>


### [34] [PowerBench: Measuring Language Model Bias in Power-shifting Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02303)
*Nicolas Martorell,Wendy Brau,Gonzalo A. Heredia,Tomás Pablo Korenblit,Gaspar Labastié,Tomás Gimenez Molina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出PowerBench基准，系统评测语言模型在"权力转移类请求"上的帮助/拒绝行为差异。对24个中美模型在多重实验条件下的测试显示：模型拒绝"夺权"多于"失权"、拒绝"失权"多于"自我赋权"；拒绝率随受影响方规模上升；存在明显国别偏向；当用户为AI智能体时拒绝率升高；语言影响拒绝行为但平均效应互相抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被用于协助与权力相关的请求，因此其在"帮助谁"上的系统性差异可能大规模改变权力分布，或可能被用户利用——通过试探并学习哪些身份较少被拒绝。然而目前缺乏对这一类权力转移请求的系统化测量工具，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 提出PowerBench，将权力转移请求区分为自我赋权、失权、夺权三类，并设置不改变权力的"诱发拒绝"对照请求。构建并开源数据集，变量包括权力领域、情境、受影响方规模、用户原有权力地位。评测24个模型（12个美国、12个中国开发者），在三种实验条件下测试：用户与受影响方国籍互为倒数、AI智能体作为用户、以及8种请求语言。

Result: 模型拒绝夺权多于拒绝失权，拒绝失权多于拒绝自我赋权；对夺权的拒绝率随受影响方规模从个体到社会而上升。模型偏向帮助他方从美国获取权力，反对帮助美国用户从他人处获取权力，但当美国获益且无人受损时则偏向美国。当用户为AI智能体时，对权力转移请求的拒绝率上升，尤以对个体夺权为甚。语言会带来拒绝偏差，但方式因模型而异，平均效应大体相互抵消。

Conclusion: PowerBench为衡量当前及未来模型中此类权力转移不对称性提供了可测量的工具，并已开源发布。

Abstract: Language models increasingly assist people with power-related requests, so systematic differences in whom they help could shift the distribution of power at scale, or be exploited by users who learn which identities are refused less. We introduce PowerBench, an evaluation of power-shifting requests that distinguishes self-empowerment, disempowerment, and power grabbing, plus a control of refusal-inducing requests that shift no power. We build, curate, and open-source a dataset of such requests varying the power domain, the context, the scale of the affected party, and the prior power standing of the user, and evaluate 24 models (12 from US and 12 from Chinese developers) under three experimental conditions: reciprocal nationalities of user and affected party, an AI agent as the user, and 8 request languages. Models refuse power grabbing more than disempowerment, and disempowerment more than self-empowerment. Refusal of power grabbing rises with the scale of the affected party, from an individual to a society. Models are biased toward helping others take power from the US and against helping US users take power from others, but favor the US when it gains power and nobody loses it. When the user is an AI agent, refusal of power-shifting requests increases, especially in power grabbing against an individual. Finally, language biases refusal, but in model-specific ways that largely cancel on average. We release PowerBench to make these asymmetries measurable in current and future models.

</details>


### [35] [Nearly Optimal Fixed-Confidence Best-Arm Identification with 1-Bit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02771)
*Khang Luong,Dinh Thai Son,Hoang Ta,Hung The Tran,Tuan Quang Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究严格1-bit反馈下的固定置信度最优臂识别问题，提出基于随机阈值查询与截断尾部积分恒等式的时间一致1-bit均值估计原语，并将其嵌入候选-挑战者算法，给出固定截断的anytime PAC保证与分阶段自适应截断的间隙自适应样本复杂度，同时证明K臂最坏情况信息论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格1-bit反馈下，每轮仅收到采样奖励是否属于查询集的单个比特，无法直接进行经验均值估计，截断成为不可避免，因此需要新的均值估计原语与最优臂识别算法。

Method: 首先构造基于随机阈值查询和截断尾部积分恒等式的时间一致1-bit均值估计原语；随后将其嵌入候选-挑战者最优臂识别框架，提出固定截断算法（anytime (ε,δ)-PAC保证）和分阶段自适应截断算法（截断水平匹配当前分辨率以获间隙自适应样本复杂度）；并证明K臂最坏情况信息论下界。

Result: 固定截断算法提供简单的anytime (ε,δ)-PAC保证；分阶段自适应截断算法实现间隙自适应样本复杂度；下界表明有限方差1-bit反馈带来的对数惩罚是内在的，且与分阶段算法的主阶依赖在log log低阶因子内匹配。

Conclusion: 在严格1-bit反馈与有限方差分布无关设定下，通过新的均值估计原语与自适应截断策略，可实现接近信息论最优的最优臂识别，1-bit反馈导致的对数惩罚不可消除。

Abstract: We study fixed-confidence best-arm identification under strict 1-bit feedback constraints. At each round, the learner selects an arm and a query set, and receives only a single bit indicating whether the sampled reward belongs to that set. We consider a distribution-free finite-variance setting with arm-wise localization, where direct empirical mean estimation is no longer available and clipping becomes unavoidable. We first formulate a time-uniform 1-bit mean-estimation primitive based on randomized threshold queries and a clipped tail-integral identity. We then embed this primitive into candidate-challenger best-arm identification algorithms. A fixed-clipping algorithm gives a simple anytime $(ε,δ)$-PAC guarantee, while a phased adaptive-clipping algorithm matches the clipping level to the current resolution and yields a gap-adaptive sample complexity. We also prove a $K$-arm worst-case information-theoretic lower bound showing that the logarithmic penalty caused by finite-variance 1-bit feedback is intrinsic. This bound matches the leading dependence of the phased algorithm up to lower-order $\log\log$ factors.

</details>


### [36] [Slow-Fast Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Capability Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02324)
*Xiaofei Yin,Tong Chu,Jiyuan Fu,Jun Lan,Shuheng Zhou,Huijia Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出慢-快多教师在线策略蒸馏（SF-MOPD），通过快模型与慢模型（学生模型的指数滑动平均）的耦合，仅移除会加剧能力干扰的更新分量，在提升多模态专业能力的同时缓解通用能力退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础多模态大模型需覆盖广泛领域能力，而多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）虽能将多个领域专家能力整合到单一学生模型，但其训练过程使学生在参数空间上逐渐偏离初始化模型；偏离越大，通用能力退化越严重，形成"能力干扰"。直接约束学生靠近初始化模型虽可缓解干扰，却同时抑制了专业知识的获取，故需在两者间寻求平衡。

Method: SF-MOPD 采用快-慢双模型结构：快模型为直接由各教师更新的当前学生；慢模型为学生模型的指数滑动平均（EMA），逐步吸收学习信号，作为融合通用基础与已确认领域专长的"移动能力参考"。对每个教师，在 log-probability 空间中计算教师诱导的更新量，仅剔除使快模型进一步远离慢模型的分量，保留与参考方向对齐及正交的分量。

Result: 在多种模型规模上的实验表明，SF-MOPD 有效缓解能力干扰，增强多模态专业能力，并降低通用能力基准上的平均退化程度，一致优于原始 MOPD。

Conclusion: 通过慢模型作为动态参考进行更新分量的方向性筛选，SF-MOPD 在保留通用基础与吸收领域专长之间取得更优权衡，为多教师蒸馏中的能力干扰问题提供了有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Foundation multimodal large language models are designed to support a broad spectrum of capabilities across diverse domains. Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) provides an effective framework for consolidating domain-specific expertise into a single student model. However, MOPD training gradually drives the student away from its initialization model, and general capabilities decline as the displacement grows, resulting in capability interference. A direct remedy is constraining the student toward its initialization, but this suppresses the acquisition of domain expertise as well. We propose Slow-Fast Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (SF-MOPD), which couples a fast model, the current student updated directly by each teacher, with a slow model, an exponential moving average of the student. The slow model absorbs the learning signal gradually, serving as a moving capability reference that fuses the general foundation with confirmed domain expertise. For each teacher, SF-MOPD computes the teacher-induced update in log-probability space and removes only the component that pushes the fast model further away from the slow model, while retaining aligned and orthogonal components. Experiments across multiple model scales demonstrate that SF-MOPD effectively mitigates capability interference, enhances specialized multimodal capabilities, and reduces the average degradation on general-capability benchmarks, consistently outperforming vanilla MOPD.

</details>


### [37] [Muon Learns Facts Better: Understanding the Role of Spectral Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02798)
*Xuheng Li,Qiwei Di,Yuan Cao,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过可解的事实回忆模型，理论分析了Muon优化器中的谱正交化如何重塑特征学习动态：谱梯度流将GF的主语-关系学习时间比从Õ(√(S/R))降至Õ(1)，并使误差以指数速度衰减，同时保持正交等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器对矩阵值更新施加谱正交化，在大规模神经网络训练中表现优异，但这种变换在特征学习中的机制尚不清楚。作者希望在可控的理论框架下揭示谱正交化对特征学习动态的本质影响。

Method: 构建可解的事实回忆（factual-recall）模型：将事实映射为主语-关系对到答案，用线性Transformer学习恢复映射所需的主语相关与关系相关信息。以梯度流（GF）、谱梯度流（Spectral GF）和Sign GF分别作为梯度下降、Muon与Adam的连续时间极限进行优化。以主语相关与关系相关预测分量达到目标准确率的学习时间刻画特征分离现象，并分析固定S、R下误差随训练时间T的衰减速率，以及不同优化器在token嵌入正交变换下的等变性。

Result: 在S个主语、R个关系时，GF的学习时间比为Õ(√(S/R))，而谱梯度流将该比值降至Õ(1)。固定S与R时，GF下主语相关和关系相关误差随训练时间T按1/(T log T)衰减，而在谱梯度流下按exp(−poly(T))衰减。此外，GF与谱梯度流在token嵌入的正交变换下等变，Sign GF则不等变：不同正交归一嵌入可能产生无特征分离、较大特征分离阶段甚至反转的学习顺序。

Conclusion: 研究给出了谱正交化如何从根本上重塑特征学习动态的机制性视角：Muon的谱正交化显著加速并均衡了不同特征分量的学习，并带来等变性等结构性质。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer applies spectral orthogonalization to matrix-valued updates and has shown strong performance in large-scale neural network training, yet the mechanisms of this transformation in feature learning remain poorly understood. In this work, we investigate this question through a tractable factual-recall model, where a fact maps each subject-relation pair to an answer, and a linear transformer learns the subject- and relation-dependent information required to recover this mapping. The transformer is optimized with gradient flow (GF), spectral GF, or Sign GF, which are continuous-time limits of gradient descent, Muon, and Adam, respectively. Prior studies (Nichani et al., 2025) have shown that when the number of subjects exceeds the number of relations, GF learns relation-dependent information before subject-dependent information, producing a feature-separation phase during training. We characterize this separation with the learning times when the subject- and relation-dependent components of the prediction reach a target accuracy. With $S$ subjects and $R$ relations, GF has a learning-time ratio of $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{S/R})$, whereas Spectral GF reduces this ratio to $\widetildeΘ(1)$. In addition, for fixed $S$ and $R$, the subject- and relation-dependent errors decay as $1/(T\log T)$ in training time $T$ under GF, but as $\exp(-\mathrm{poly}(T))$ under spectral GF. Finally, we show that GF and spectral GF are equivariant under orthogonal transformations of the token embeddings, whereas Sign GF is not: Different orthonormal embeddings can potentially produce no feature separation, a large feature-separation phase, or even a reversed learning order. These results provide a mechanistic view of how spectral orthogonalization can fundamentally reshape feature-learning dynamics.

</details>


### [38] [Drive vs. Decay: On the Training Dynamics of Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02344)
*José Lucas De Melo Costa,Seong Woo Ahn,Fabrice Popineau,Arpad Rimmel,Bich-Liên Doan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出JEPA早期训练稳定性理论，将表示坍缩避免的经验启发式统一为驱动-衰减竞争机制，并据此设计了ResidualPred预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA易发生表示坍缩，现有缓解手段多为经验性启发式，缺乏统一的理论解释，也缺乏理论指导的稳定化设计原则。

Method: 在平凡不动点附近对JEPA耦合梯度流线性化，得到驱动力γ与衰减效应σ两种竞争作用；在近似谱解耦下，将逐模态稳定比μ_i=γ_i/σ_i分解为数据侧与预测器侧独立项，不稳定模态数对应可涌现表示的秩，并预测相边界。基于该分析提出ResidualPred——初始化时注意力偏向恒等映射的Transformer预测器。

Result: 相边界在800余组Tabular-JEPA配置上得到实证验证；框架统一解释预测器缩放、掩码比例与EMA作为调节μ的不同机制。ResidualPred在表格基准上提升有效秩与下游准确率，并在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、STL-10和ImageNet的I-JEPA预训练中取得改进。

Conclusion: 该框架将经验性的坍缩规避启发式与显式动力学图景相连接，为JEPA提供了理论驱动的稳定化手段，代码已开源。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) are prone to representation collapse, typically mitigated through empirical heuristics. We develop an early-training stability theory that unifies these heuristics. Linearising the coupled JEPA gradient flow around the trivial fixed point reveals two competing effects: a driving force ($γ$) and a decay effect ($σ$). Under approximate spectral decoupling, a per-mode stability ratio $μ_i = γ_i / σ_i$ factorises into independent data-side and predictor-side terms and the count of unstable modes tracks the rank of representations that can emerge. The framework predicts a phase boundary, which we confirm empirically across more than 800 Tabular-JEPA configurations. It also unifies predictor scaling, masking ratio, and EMA as distinct mechanisms for shifting $μ$. Guided by this analysis, we introduce ResidualPred, a transformer predictor whose attention is biased toward the identity at initialisation; it improves both effective rank and downstream accuracy on tabular benchmarks and in I-JEPA pretraining on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, STL-10, and ImageNet. Our framework connects empirical collapse-avoidance heuristics to an explicit dynamical picture, yielding theory-driven stabilizers. Code is available at https://github.com/jose-melo/drive-vs-decay.

</details>


### [39] [Mitigating Convergence Collapse in Fixed-Target Anomaly Detectors via Kernel-Anchored Locality Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02345)
*José Lucas De Melo Costa,Fabrice Popineau,Arpad Rimmel,Bich-Liên Doan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出固定目标神经表格异常检测器存在“收敛崩塌”：优化越充分，残差信号越弱。作者提出以核回归桥接定位约束，给出闭式免训练的KCM方法，并提出KAR正则项缓解崩塌、提升AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一类表格异常检测器（收缩匹配、一步rectified flow、重构自编码器）在平方误差损失下让神经映射逼近固定目标，并用测试残差打分。这类方法在收敛时映射即使在分布外也紧跟目标，导致异常与正常数据的残差同时消失，只能靠早停或容量限制等隐式不收敛来保留信号。作者认为这是结构性问题：有效异常检测需要阻止无约束外推的局部性约束。

Method: 作者首先形式化固定目标检测器与核回归的联系，证明其核回归类比是有限带宽的Nadaraya-Watson平滑器，并提出Kernel Contraction Matching（KCM）。KCM具有闭式解、无需训练、CPU高效。在此基础上提出Kernel-Anchored Regularizer（KAR），通过惩罚神经预测偏离训练目标的核加权平均来施加局部性约束，从而缓解收敛崩塌。

Result: KCM在ADBench上匹配已建立的神经基线，且闭式、免训练、CPU高效。在易崩塌的ADBench数据集与三种骨干网络上，KAR缓解了收敛崩塌，并在长时间训练下提升AUROC。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了固定目标神经异常检测器的收敛崩塌是结构性问题，需显式局部性约束；通过核回归桥接传统检测器与神经检测器，提出KCM与KAR，前者提供高效免训练基线，后者以核锚定正则改善神经检测器的训练稳定性与检测性能。

Abstract: A family of tabular anomaly detectors trains a neural map toward a fixed target under squared-error loss and scores anomalies by the test-time residual; contraction matching, one-step rectified flow, and reconstruction autoencoders all fit this template. We characterize a convergence collapse: better optimization makes the detector worse. At convergence, the learned map tracks the target even off-distribution, so the residual signal vanishes on anomalies as well as on normal data. These detectors therefore rely on implicit non-convergence (early stopping, capacity caps) to retain signal. We argue this is structural: effective anomaly detection requires a locality constraint that blocks unconstrained extrapolation. Classical detectors (kNN, KDE, isolation forests, LOF) enforce locality explicitly; fixed-target neural detectors do not. We formalize the connection by showing that the kernel-regression analog of a fixed-target detector is a finite-bandwidth Nadaraya-Watson smoother, which we call Kernel Contraction Matching (KCM). KCM is closed-form, training-free, and CPU-efficient, yet matches established neural baselines on ADBench. Building on this bridge, we introduce the Kernel-Anchored Regularizer (KAR), which penalizes deviation of the neural prediction from a kernel-weighted average of training targets. Across collapse-prone ADBench datasets and three backbones, KAR mitigates collapse and improves AUROC under prolonged training.

</details>


### [40] [Below what training size do deep tabular generators stop beating trivial baselines? A preregistered benchmark on a size ladder of clinical and standard datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03500)
*Shivam Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该预注册基准研究通过大小为200至20,000行的规模阶梯实验，发现深度表格生成模型在中小规模数据上从未超过最佳平凡基线，且其小样本排序其实比预期更稳定，但原生小临床数据集与子采样大数据集的排名一致性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度表格生成模型通常在数万行的数据集上进行评测，而临床数据集往往只有数百行，现有基准与真实应用场景之间存在明显的规模鸿沟，作者希望系统性地定位两种规模区间在模型表现上的分歧点。

Method: 采用预注册的规模阶梯基准：选取8个公开数据集，将训练集子采样为200到20,000行，纳入七种生成器（独立边缘分布、高斯Copula、SMOTE、无条件下SMOTE、CTGAN、TVAE、TabDDPM），统一使用20次试验的调参预算与5个评估种子，另加4个真实规模的原生小临床数据集，合计2,220次固定运行；主指标为在合成数据上训练、在真实数据上测试的固定分类器的AUROC。

Result: 在24个（数据集, 深度模型）组合中有23个，深度模型在任何训练规模下都未超过最佳平凡基线一个种子噪声以上；最佳基线在49个（数据集, 规模）单元中胜出40个。预注册假设被证伪：5,000行以下相邻阶梯间的平均Kendall tau为0.806，高于0.8阈值，且稳定性在最小规模处最高而非最低。主要限制是81%的单元中前两名方法的差距小于种子间波动。原生小临床数据集与子采样大数据集的方法排名仅中度一致（平均tau为0.57至0.64）。

Conclusion: 在中小规模表格生成任务上，深度模型的优势并不成立，平凡基线反而更稳健；小样本下深度模型的排序稳定性被证伪性地发现较高，因此不能把“小数据排序不稳定”当作忽视评估的理由。更关键的是，子采样大数据集难以可靠替代原生小数据集，评测小样本生成模型时应尽可能使用真实的小规模数据而非子采样方案。研究公开了全部2,220个结果文件、预注册及其哈希与可复现代码，为后续基准工作提供了透明基础。

Abstract: Deep tabular generative models are benchmarked on datasets with tens of thousands of rows; clinical datasets have hundreds. We preregistered and ran a size-ladder benchmark to find where the two regimes diverge: 8 public datasets subsampled from 200 to 20,000 training rows, seven generators (independent marginals, Gaussian copula, SMOTE, unconditional SMOTE, CTGAN, TVAE, TabDDPM) with a fixed 20-trial tuning budget and 5 evaluation seeds, plus 4 natively small clinical datasets at true size, for 2,220 committed runs in total. The primary metric is the AUROC of fixed classifiers trained on synthetic and tested on real data. In 23 of 24 (dataset, deep model) pairs no deep model ever beats the best trivial baseline by more than seed noise, at any training size we measured. The best baseline wins 40 of 49 (dataset, size) cells. Our preregistered prediction that the deep models' ranking would be unstable at small sizes is falsified: mean Kendall tau between adjacent rungs below 5,000 rows is 0.806, above our 0.8 threshold, and stability is highest at the smallest sizes rather than lowest. One caveat bounds all of this: in 81% of cells the gap between the top two methods is smaller than the variation between seeds. Finally, method rankings on natively small clinical datasets agree only moderately with rankings on subsampled large ones (mean tau 0.57 to 0.64), which questions whether a subsampled large dataset can stand in for a small one. All 2,220 result files, the preregistration and its hash, and the code that regenerates every figure and number from those files are public.

</details>

*Mohamed Bouadi,Nassim Bouarour,Shivam Dubey,Aditya Tanna,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据归因旨在识别哪些训练样本塑造了模型行为，但因果性的训练影响难以观测，导致归因声明难以验证。作者主张通过可控的合成预训练环境，使归因可被实验检验。

Method: 提出O'PRIOR，一个面向表格基础模型的、具有丰富谱系的合成任务生成器，使每个预训练任务在结构机制、缺失性、混杂、捷径和分布偏移上带有显式谱系。结合行为条件归因、反事实重训练以及谱系感知干预，测试任务级忠实性与机制级一致性。

Result: 在留出真实任务上，移除归因排名前5%的合成任务使平均ROC-AUC下降0.013，而随机移除仅下降0.002±0.004；移除归因排名靠后的任务使性能提升0.003。在含捷径谱系的任务中，定向移除效应为0.043，匹配随机移除为0.016。谱系判别能力较弱，排名AUROC仅0.55–0.62，尽管top-k富集显著，说明谱系关联与干预忠实性并不必然一致。

Conclusion: 研究确立了合成谱系作为可验证贡献性归因的可控实验环境，并揭示归因的关联性与因果忠实性可能分离。

Abstract: Training-data attribution aims to identify which training examples shape model behavior, yet validating such claims is difficult because causal training influence is rarely observable. We argue that controlled synthetic pretraining makes attribution experimentally testable. Using O'PRIOR, a provenance-rich synthetic task generator for tabular foundation models, we construct a testbed in which every pretraining task carries explicit lineage over structural mechanisms, missingness, confounding, shortcuts, and distribution shift. We combine behavior-conditioned attribution with counterfactual retraining and provenance-aware interventions to test both task-level faithfulness and mechanism-level consistency. On held-out real tasks, removing the top-attributed 5% of synthetic tasks decreases mean ROC-AUC by 0.013, compared with 0.002$\pm$0.004 under random removal, while removing bottom-attributed tasks improves performance by 0.003. Within shortcut-provenance tasks, targeted removal yields an effect of 0.043 versus 0.016 for matched random removal. Provenance discrimination is more modest by ranking AUROC (0.55-0.62), despite substantial top-k enrichment, revealing that provenance association and interventional faithfulness need not coincide. Our results establish synthetic provenance as a controlled setting for verifiable contributive attribution

</details>


### [42] [Broken scale symmetries in undercomplete linear autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03640)
*Farhad Pashakhanloo,Jacob A. Zavatone-Veth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究欠完备线性自编码器中SGD对尺度对称性的破坏，发现SGD在PCA解流形上偏好大解码器权重的定向尺度漂移，其动力学可解析刻画，最终受限于有限步长稳定性边界，导致解更"尖锐"但不同锐度度量可能相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络损失景观存在多种对称性（如齐次网络的层间尺度对称），梯度流保持这些对称性，但有限步长SGD会破坏它们。已有工作记录了SGD通过平衡梯度噪声或最小化波动来打破尺度对称的案例，但欠完备线性自编码器中解几何是否以及如何选择特定符号的尺度漂移尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以欠完备线性自编码器为研究对象，分析其在PCA解流形上的SGD动力学。通过解析推导，给出尺度漂移的有效描述，刻画其在慢时间尺度上的定向运动，并分析其终止条件——有限步长稳定性边界，同时用损失Hessian最大特征值等锐度度量评估最终解。

Result: 在PCA解流形上，SGD偏好大解码器权重，呈现符号确定的定向尺度漂移；该漂移发生在慢时间尺度上，且其动力学具有可解析处理的有效描述；尺度不能无限增长，最终被有限步长稳定性边界阻止；所得解在损失Hessian最大特征值意义下比平衡基线更尖锐，但不同锐度度量可能朝相反方向变化。

Conclusion: 欠完备自编码器提供了一个具体例证，说明损失几何如何将残余梯度噪声转化为沿功能等价解流形的定向运动。这一发现揭示了SGD打破对称性的新机制——由解流形几何选择漂移方向，而非仅由噪声平衡驱动，并表明锐度评价具有度量依赖性。

Abstract: Neural network loss landscapes have many symmetries, which are preserved by gradient flow but broken by finite-stepsize stochastic gradient descent (SGD). A canonical example of such a symmetry is scale in homogeneous networks: one can scale up the parameters in one layer and down in the next without changing the network output. Previous work has documented cases in which SGD breaks this symmetry in favor of balancing gradient noise or minimizing fluctuations. Here, we show that the solution geometry of undercomplete linear autoencoders instead selects a preferred sign for scale drift: on the PCA solution manifold, SGD favors large decoder weights. This directed scale drift occurs on a slow timescale, and its dynamics admit an analytically-tractable effective description. However, it cannot continue indefinitely: increasing scale eventually drives the dynamics towards a finite-stepsize stability boundary. The resulting solutions are sharper than a balanced baseline in the sense of the maximum eigenvalue of the loss Hessian, but different sharpness measures can move in opposing directions. Thus, undercomplete autoencoders give a concrete illustration of how loss geometry can convert residual gradient noise into directed motion along a manifold of functionally-equivalent solutions.

</details>
