We address this challenge by complementing limited high-fidelity (HF) target-domain data with abundant, cheap, but biased low-fidelity (LF) data, e.g., from a simplified simulator. Most existing methods directly optimize biased objectives based on LF data. In contrast, the recently introduced multi-fidelity policy gradient (MFPG) framework uses LF data solely to construct a control variate that reduces variance and improves HF data efficiency without biasing the policy gradient estimator. However, published work on MFPG is limited to REINFORCE on small-scale simulation tasks. We develop MFPG for modern actor-critic learning in GPU-parallel simulation and on a physical robot. Our analysis and experiments show that naive extensions to proximal policy optimization (PPO) can lose cross-fidelity correlation or inflate variance. Our MFPG-PPO addresses these failures by redesigning the sampling, advantage estimation, and control variate construction to preserve cross-fidelity correlation, and by monitoring estimator uncertainty to prevent variance inflation. We also introduce a budget-aware MFPG-PPO to divide a fixed sampling budget among high- and low-fidelity data sources. Across simulated robot locomotion tasks of varying LF-to-HF transfer difficulty and HF data budgets, MFPG-PPO improves upon PPO trained on HF data alone in nearly all settings, and consistently matches the performance of PPO trained with 16x more HF data on the hardest task at the smallest HF budgets. In contrast, most baselines that use LF data perform well only where direct LF-to-HF transfer succeeds. MFPG-PPO enables stable learning on a physical Franka arm using only 4 real-robot episodes per update and no human demonstrations.

</details>


### [78] [Post-Training Quantization of Autoregressive Weather Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02511)
*Ananyo Bhattacharya,Swastik Bhattacharya,Christiane Jablonowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究将训练后量化（PTQ）应用于两个全球尺度天气预测AI模拟器（DLWP与FourCastNet），系统评估其在短期预报时效内对推理的影响，结果表明模拟量化配置可产生定性上有意义的短期预报，为自回归天气模拟器的PTQ提供了首个基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率数值天气预报（NWP）与资料同化（DA）的进步推动了模拟大气动力学的深度学习架构发展。此类AI天气模拟器预报质量可与物理模式相媲美，且依靠硬件加速的矩阵乘法进行自回归推理，显著降低计算时间与资源消耗。在GPU上优化矩阵乘法为进一步扩展至高分辨率提供了机会，而训练后量化（PTQ）已被证明能在多种深度学习架构中加速推理、提升单位时间计算量并降低功耗，支持边缘硬件部署。然而PTQ对已预训练的全球尺度天气模拟器的影响尚缺乏系统研究，因此本文对此展开探究。

Method: 在预训练的Deep Learning Weather Prediction（DLWP）与FourCastNet（FCN）模型中实现并植入PTQ算法，作为地球物理流体动力学应用的可行性验证。系统考察PTQ对模拟器短期预报时效内推理的影响，并使用模拟量化（simulated quantization）配置对量化效果进行评估。

Result: 基于模拟量化的PTQ配置评估显示，在短时间时效内可获得定性上有意义的预报结果。

Conclusion: 本研究给出了自回归天气模拟器PTQ的首个基准，为面向动力系统的深度学习模型基于量化的优化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Advancements in high-resolution numerical weather prediction (NWP) and data assimilation (DA) have shaped the developments in deep learning (DL) architectures emulating atmospheric dynamics. Emulators for weather forecasting exhibit forecast quality comparable to physics based models at forecast horizon scaling from few days to subseasonal time scales. The emulators are driven by hardware-accelerated matrix multiplication in autoregressive inferences, significantly reducing the computation time and resources required for NWP. Optimization of the matrix multiplication processes in GPU architectures provides opportunities to scale towards high-resolution domain, and offers implementation of out of the box solutions. Post-training quantization (PTQ) has been demonstrated across multiple DL architectures to accelerate and increase the number of computations in unit time while consuming less power, enabling applications on edge hardware. In this study, we investigate the effect of PTQ on pre-trained AI emulators for global-scale weather forecasting. We implement PTQ algorithms in Deep Learning Weather Prediction (DLWP) and FourCastNet (FCN) models as a proof of concept for geophysical fluid dynamics applications. We systematically investigate the effect of PTQ on emulator inferences over short-range forecast horizons. Evaluation of PTQ configurations using simulated quantization hints at qualitatively meaningful forecasts over short-time horizons. These results provide a first benchmark of PTQ for autoregressive weather emulators and a basis for quantization-based optimization of DL models for dynamical systems.

</details>


### [79] [Student-Guided Teacher Distillation for Efficient LLM Task Routing: Positioning Against Jev-Style System-1 Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02516)
*Haifeng Wu,Srinivasan Manoharan,Jian Wan,Fangbo Tu,Junhua Zhao,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究一种学生引导的教师蒸馏流水线：用紧凑的ModernBERT一次前向预测60类任务分布并检索top-k候选，再由DeBERTa-v3零样本NLI教师仅对候选重排，教师标签迭代提升学生。最佳学生checkpoint达到77.5%教师一致率，Coverage@16为91-100%；并指出截断top-k教师分数不能当作完整60类软目标进行KL蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本分类器可用于将用户请求路由到专门的LLM任务，但对大规模候选集逐一评分成本高：NLI分类器需为每个标签评估一个premise-hypothesis对，开销随分类体系规模线性增长。因此需要降低候选评分成本，同时保留教师的决策信息。

Method: 针对固定60类LLM任务分类体系，构建学生引导的教师蒸馏流水线：紧凑的ModernBERT分类器单次前向预测全类别分布，并检索小规模top-k候选集；较大的DeBERTa-v3零样本NLI分类器仅对这k个候选重排，而非全部60个标签；教师产生的标签迭代改进学生，学生再为下一轮生成更精准的候选。与通用嵌入检索或极端多标签分类中的聚类短名单不同，该候选生成器在目标分类体系上端到端训练，且与生产流量服务模型一致。

Result: 最佳学生checkpoint在200例评估集上达到77.5%的教师一致率；初步覆盖率测量显示Coverage@16为91-100%，表明top-k集合保留了教师大部分决策相关信息。此外，将截断的top-k教师分数用作完整60类软目标进行KL蒸馏会破坏软标签蒸馏依赖的dark knowledge，引入系统性偏差，而非无害的稀疏近似。

Conclusion: 学生引导的top-k候选加教师重排可在保持较高教师一致性与覆盖率的同时降低评分成本；截断top-k分数不宜作为完整软目标用于KL蒸馏。完整评估（多k值覆盖率、嵌入检索基线、更大规模人工审核测试集）仍在进行中。

Abstract: Zero-shot classifiers are useful for routing user requests to specialized LLM tasks, but scoring every request against a large candidate set is expensive: a zero-shot NLI classifier must evaluate one premise-hypothesis pair per label, so cost scales linearly with taxonomy size. We study a student-guided teacher distillation pipeline for a fixed taxonomy of 60 LLM task categories: a compact ModernBERT classifier predicts the full category distribution in one forward pass and retrieves a small top-k candidate set, and a larger DeBERTa-v3 zero-shot NLI classifier reranks only those candidates rather than all 60 labels; the resulting teacher labels iteratively improve the student, which produces sharper candidates for the next round. Unlike generic embedding retrieval or clustering-derived shortlists used in extreme multi-label classification, our candidate generator is trained end-to-end on the target taxonomy and is the same model serving production traffic, distinguishing it from LLM-routing work that routes between candidate models, and from concurrent System-1 encoder-classifier proposals (e.g. TypeSafe AI's Jev and the open-source Laya project) whose training methodology is undocumented or RL-based. Our best student checkpoint reaches 77.5% teacher agreement on a 200-example evaluation set, and preliminary coverage measurements show Coverage@16 of 91-100%, suggesting top-k sets retain most of the teacher's decision-relevant information. We further show truncated top-k teacher scores should not be treated as full 60-class soft targets for KL distillation: zeroing untruncated classes destroys the dark knowledge soft-label distillation depends on, introducing systematic bias rather than a harmless sparse approximation. A complete evaluation, including coverage at multiple k on a held-out set, an embedding-retrieval baseline, and a larger human-reviewed test set, remains in progress.

</details>


### [80] [Learning the Latent Structure: A Feature-Centric Approach to Graph Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02517)
*Yu Song,Zhigang Hua,Yan Xie,Bingheng Li,Jingzhe Liu,Bo Long,Jiliang Tang,Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种特征中心的图数据增强框架 SelfAug，绕过显式结构建模，在嵌入空间通过自监督逆掩码过程恢复未观测结构信号，并引入消息正则化器与自举策略提升鲁棒性，在十个数据集上准确率与效率均优于SOTA，适用于归纳与冷启动场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实图数据因采集与观测限制常不完整，严重制约图学习效果。现有图数据增强（GDA）方法依赖标签、计算开销大且本质为直推式（transductive），难以适用于实际场景。

Method: 提出特征中心的图数据增强框架，直接在嵌入空间操作，避免显式结构建模；通过自监督逆掩码（inverse masking）过程捕获观测图与完整图之间的潜在关联，借助精炼的节点表示恢复未观测结构信号；并引入消息正则化器（message regularizer）与自举策略（bootstrap strategy）以增强噪声和稀疏监督下的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Result: 在跨多个领域的十个图数据集上评估，SelfAug 在准确率与效率两方面均持续优于现有最优方法，并在归纳（inductive）与冷启动（cold-start）设置下均取得领先表现。

Conclusion: SelfAug 可作为面向真实图学习场景的可扩展、可泛化的解决方案，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Graph-structured data plays a pivotal role in modeling complex relationships. However, real-world graphs are often incomplete due to data collection and observational constraints, severely limiting the effectiveness of modern graph learning pipelines. While existing Graph Data Augmentation (GDA) methods attempt to refine graph structures for improved downstream performance, they are typically label-dependent, computationally expensive, and inherently transductive, limiting their applicability in practical scenarios. In this work, we present a novel feature-centric graph data augmentation framework that bypasses explicit structure modeling by operating directly in the embedding space. Through a self-supervised inverse masking process, our method captures latent ties between observed and complete graphs, enabling recovery of unobserved structural signals through refined node representations. To enhance robustness under noisy and sparse supervision, we introduce a message regularizer and a bootstrap strategy for effective training and generalization. Evaluated on ten graph datasets spanning multiple domains, our approach, SelfAug, consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both accuracy and efficiency across inductive and cold-start settings, highlighting its potential as a scalable and generalizable solution for real-world graph learning scenarios.

</details>


### [81] [Instance-Dependent Regret for CMDPs with Step-Wise Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02520)
*Qian Zuo,Francesco Emanuele Stradi,Leyang Xue,Sattar Vakili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带逐步安全约束的情节式表格约束MDP在线学习，提出SVAE算法，通过学习候选安全子图并在其中进行方差自适应乐观规划，实现了具有方差自适应、安全子图结构依赖的累积 regret 与约束违反界，并给出下界证明其依赖不可避免。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带逐步安全约束的约束MDP中，约束诱导出一个安全子图，该子图决定了可行策略下累积奖励的方差，进而影响学习难度。然而，利用这一结构需要同时学习哪些动作是安全的并控制约束违反，现有方法未能充分捕捉这种安全子图与方差结构带来的学习复杂度。

Method: 提出Safe Variance-Adaptive Exploration（SVAE）算法：学习候选安全子图，并在其中执行方差自适应的乐观规划；通过控制约束违反来逐步识别安全动作集合。

Result: 以高概率达到累积regret为Õ(√(SAH·min{V_Σ, K·Var*}) + S√(AH³·min{K,C}) + S²AH²)；逐步约束违反为Õ(H√(SAK)+S²AH²)，并具有gap-dependent且关于K为polylog的违反界。同时建立下界，表明对这些实例特定量的依赖不可避免。

Conclusion: SVAE在带逐步安全约束的表格约束MDP在线学习中同时实现了方差自适应regret与约束违反最优或近最优界；下界证明安全子图结构与方差相关实例量对学习复杂度的依赖是本质的。

Abstract: We study online learning in episodic tabular constrained Markov decision processes with step-wise safety constraints. In such a setting, the constraints induce a safe subgraph that shapes the variance of cumulative rewards under feasible policies and, consequently, the difficulty of learning. Exploiting this structure, however, requires learning which actions are safe while controlling constraint violations. We propose Safe Variance-Adaptive Exploration (SVAE), an efficient algorithm that learns candidate safe subgraphs and performs variance-adaptive optimistic planning within them. With high probability, SVAE achieves cumulative regret of order $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{SAH\min\{\mathbb{V}_Σ,K\mathrm{Var}^{\star}\}}+S\sqrt{AH^3\min\{K,\mathcal{C}\}}+S^2AH^2)$ over $K$ episodes, where $H$ is the horizon of a single episode, while $S$ and $A$ are the numbers of states and actions, respectively. Here, $\mathrm{Var}^{\star}$ is the maximum return variance among safe policies, $\mathbb{V}_Σ$ is the variance accumulated before the first unsafe action is encountered, and $\mathcal{C}$ captures the statistical complexity of eliminating actions incorrectly considered potentially safe. SVAE additionally attains $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(H\sqrt{SAK}+S^2AH^2)$ step-wise constraint violation and a gap-dependent violation bound that is polylogarithmic in $K$. Finally, we establish a lower bound showing that dependence on these instance-specific quantities is unavoidable.

</details>


### [82] [BaCP: Backbone Contrastive Pruning for Preserving Representations in Extremely Sparse Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02524)
*Mohammad Haroon Khawaja,Muhammad Haseeb,Mohammad Fatim Shoaib,Muhammad Tahir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Backbone Contrastive Pruning (BaCP)，通过对齐稀疏网络与预训练、微调及历史快照模型的嵌入空间，缓解极端稀疏下的表示坍塌问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端稀疏度的非结构化剪枝常导致表示坍塌（representational collapse），准确率急剧下降。

Method: BaCP：用对比正则化约束稀疏网络的嵌入空间，使其对齐预训练模型、微调模型和历史快照模型；基于CAP框架（Xu et al., 2022）的对比分解，进行匹配预算（matched-budget）的理论刻画，并覆盖多种剪枝准则。

Result: 在90个设置下评估，BaCP在标准剪枝失效的极端稀疏区间显著提升准确率；在表示未受损时接近基线。

Conclusion: BaCP有效缓解极端稀疏下的表示坍塌，是一种稳健且理论可刻画的剪枝正则化方法。

Abstract: Unstructured pruning at extreme sparsity often suffers from representational collapse, causing sharp drops in accuracy. To address this, we study Backbone Contrastive Pruning (BaCP), which regularizes the sparse network's embedding space by aligning it with pretrained, fine-tuned, and historical snapshot models. Building on the contrastive decomposition of the CAP framework (Xu et al., 2022), we provide a rigorous matched-budget characterization of this approach across multiple pruning criteria. Evaluated across 90 settings, BaCP improves accuracy substantially in extreme sparsity regimes where standard pruning fails, and is close to baseline where representations remain intact.

</details>


### [83] [Reward Inflation: A Healthy Stimulus for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02545)
*Ganghun Lee,Minji Kim,Minsu Lee,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“奖励膨胀”（reward inflation），即在训练过程中逐渐放大奖励幅度，理论证明其可产生隐式近因加权并抑制休眠神经元，实验表明适度膨胀有益，并提出自适应变体Fed。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中奖励通常作为主要学习信号，但其幅度在整个训练过程中一般保持固定，奖励的时间调制（temporal modulation）研究不足。作者希望探索对奖励幅度进行动态缩放是否能作为有益的训练刺激。

Method: 提出reward inflation，即随训练进程逐步缩放奖励幅度。理论上分析其对策略更新的影响：产生隐式近因加权（upweight近期transition），并在策略饱和时维持梯度信号。进一步提出自适应变体Fed，可在训练中在线调整膨胀水平。

Result: 在ALE游戏和MuJoCo任务上的实验验证了理论发现：适度的奖励膨胀对广泛任务有益；Fed自适应变体通常优于固定膨胀。

Conclusion: 奖励膨胀是一种健康的学习刺激，能加速适应、抑制休眠神经元并保持可塑性；自适应调整膨胀水平的Fed进一步提升效果。

Abstract: Reward serves as the primary learning signal in reinforcement learning (RL). However, while reward magnitudes are typically held fixed throughout training, their temporal modulation remains underexplored. In this paper, we propose reward inflation, a gradual scaling of rewards over the course of training, and show that it can act as a healthy stimulus for RL. Theoretically, reward inflation induces an implicit recency weighting that upweights recent transitions during policy updates, enabling faster adaptation. We further show that, by sustaining gradient signals as the policy saturates, reward inflation suppresses the emergence of dormant neurons and helps preserve plasticity. Empirical results on ALE games and MuJoCo tasks corroborate these findings, showing that an appropriate level of reward inflation benefits a broad range of tasks. Finally, we introduce Fed, an adaptive variant that adjusts the inflation level on the fly, and find that it often improves upon fixed inflation.

</details>


### [84] [Test-time Multi-agent Coordination by Decomposed Value Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02554)
*Dongsu Lee,Haoran Xu,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCOUT：首个通过测试时动作精炼结合生成基础模型与价值函数的离线MARL框架，用流匹配行为先验与分解价值函数解耦训练，测试时以SVGD将行为样本传输至高价值区域，传输步数替代固定正则系数实现自适应缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线多智能体强化学习存在持续权衡：表达性生成策略能刻画数据中的多模态协调，却无法区分高价值区域；价值优化策略可利用学到的Q函数，却会把多模态塌缩为单一主导模式。单智能体的模式塌缩会破坏联合协调，多智能体同时漂移会将联合策略推向动作空间的未见区域。

Method: SCOUT解耦训练两个组件：流匹配（flow-matching）行为先验与分解价值函数。测试时通过Stein变分梯度下降（SVGD）将行为样本传输至高价值区域；传输步数控制自适应测试时缩放，替代固定的正则化系数。在IGM原则下给出联合软价值差距的单项KL上界证明。

Result: 在离散与连续离线MARL基准上取得最佳平均性能，并在所有离线到在线（offline-to-online）配置中均获得性能提升。

Conclusion: 理论上，联合软价值差距的单项KL界随传输收敛而趋于零，并存在与IGM违背程度成正比、不可约的加性残差；实证上，SCOUT在离线MARL及离线到在线迁移中均优于基线，验证了生成先验与价值优化经测试时精炼统一的有效性。

Abstract: Offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) faces a persistent trade-off. Expressive generative policies can represent multi-modal coordination in the data, but cannot distinguish high-value regions, while value-optimized policies exploit the learned Q-function but collapse the multi-modal into a single dominant mode. A single agent's mode collapse can break joint coordination, and simultaneous drift across agents can push the joint policy into unseen regions of the action space. We propose scalable coordination via optimal unified transport (SCOUT), the first offline MARL framework to combine a generative foundation model with a learned value function through test-time action refinement. SCOUT trains two decoupled components: a flow-matching behavioral prior and a decomposed value function. At test-time, it transports behavioral samples toward high-value regions via Stein variational gradient descent. The number of transport steps controls adaptive test-time scaling, replacing a fixed regularization coefficient. Under the individual-global-max (IGM) principle, we prove a single-term KL bound on the joint soft-value gap that vanishes as transport converges, with an irreducible additive residual proportional to the IGM violation. Empirically, SCOUT achieves the best average performance across discrete and continuous offline MARL benchmarks and yields performance improvements in all offline-to-online configurations.

</details>


### [85] [Neuron merging via inverse-activation regression for post-training compression of sigmoid neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02559)
*Ao Kuniya,Jun Ohkubo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究用于已训练神经网络压缩的聚类式神经元合并方法，提出将神经元响应经逆激活函数映射回预激活空间并用最小二乘法估计代表神经元权重与偏置，同时比较数据辅助与无数据策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络规模不断增大，在有限算力下高效推理需要模型压缩。结构化剪枝虽能移除不重要的神经元或通道，但被移除单元仍可能含有有用信息。从已训练网络粗粒化的角度看，当多个神经元自由度被合并时，值得研究应保留哪些信息。

Method: (1) 回顾并讨论面向已训练网络压缩的基于聚类的合并方法；(2) 提出一种无数据的贡献加权平均方法；(3) 提出神经元合并方法：通过逆激活函数将神经元响应映射回预激活空间，并用最小二乘法估计每个代表神经元的权重和偏置；(4) 考察两种策略——使用真实训练输入的数据辅助策略，以及使用随机生成输入的无数据策略。

Result: 在测试的 sigmoid 网络上获得经验证据：权重信息对聚类特别有用，而激活信息对合并过程中的代表神经元重建有用。

Conclusion: 聚类式神经元合并可作为结构化剪枝的补充或替代思路，通过区分权重信息与激活信息的作用，可为已训练网络的压缩提供有效指导。

Abstract: As neural networks continue to grow in scale, model compression is becoming increasingly important for efficient inference under limited computational resources. Structured pruning methods remove neurons or channels that are estimated to be less important, but the removed units may still contain useful information. From the viewpoint of coarse-graining a trained network, it is valuable to ask which information should be retained when multiple neuronal degrees of freedom are consolidated. In this paper, we discuss cluster-based merging methods for compression of trained neural networks. In addition to a data-free contribution-weighted averaging method, we propose neuron-merging methods in which neuron responses are mapped back to the pre-activation space via the inverse activation function, and the weights and biases of each representative neuron are estimated using the least-squares method. We also examine both a data-assisted strategy with actual training inputs and a data-free strategy using randomly generated inputs. The comparisons provide empirical evidence, in the tested sigmoid networks, that weight information is particularly useful for clustering whereas activation information is useful for representative-neuron reconstruction in the merging process.

</details>


### [86] [OpenGameEval: Benchmarking Agentic Programming and Exploration in a Stateful Game Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02563)
*Eray Turkel,Mengsha Sun,Kartik Ayyar,Sean Dunigan,Jack Lu,Vlad Shcherban,Hsiang-Shun Shih,Xin Wang,Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpenGameEval，一个在 Roblox Studio 内评估智能体游戏开发的基准与评测框架。它将观测工具与编辑工具分离，可直接度量探索行为，并在 84 个任务、13 个前沿模型上评测。最佳模型单次通过率 51.7%，且探索行为显著预测任务成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体编码基准大多需要探索，却只以最终任务成功与否作为评分标准，无法直接衡量探索过程。作者希望构建一个可复现、有状态、能同时评估最终结果与探索行为的游戏开发评测框架，以更细致地刻画模型能力与行为。

Method: 提出 OpenGameEval：在 Roblox Studio 中以可复现的有状态游戏引擎会话运行语言模型智能体；用可执行检查分别在被编辑场景和模拟游玩会话中打分。其八工具动作空间将观测工具与编辑工具分离，从而直接度量探索。在 84 个人工筛选的核心任务上评测 13 个前沿模型，每任务尝试 16 次，并分析通过率与探索行为的关系。

Result: 任务对当前模型较难：最佳模型单次尝试解决 51.7% 的任务，五次全部通过率 39.4%，且有 6 个任务无任何模型解决。前沿模型通过率相近但解决的任务不同；按任务类型拆分后，前五名在脚本编写任务上相差 5.0 个百分点，在场景修改任务上相差 12.5 个百分点。控制任务与模型后，若某次运行在行动前检查了参考解涉及的全部对象，其在仅场景任务上的通过率高出 13.4 个百分点，在仅脚本任务上高出 9.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: 探索行为是运行成功的重要预测因子，说明过程性指标对智能体评测具有独立价值。作者以 MIT 许可证发布了任务套件、place 文件、逐任务标注、在 Roblox Studio 内运行任务的插件及更新后的排行榜，地址为 https://github.com/Roblox/open-game-eval。

Abstract: We present OpenGameEval, a benchmark and evaluation framework for agentic game development inside Roblox Studio. It runs language models as agents in reproducible, stateful game-engine sessions and scores each run with executable checks, both on the edited scene and in a simulated play session. Most agentic coding benchmarks require exploration but score only final task success. OpenGameEval separates observation tools from editing tools in its eight-tool action space, so exploration can be measured directly. We measure the pass rates and exploration behavior of 13 frontier models on 84 human-curated core tasks, with 16 attempts per task.
  The tasks are hard for current models. The best model solves 51.7% of tasks on a single attempt and 39.4% five times out of five, and no tested model solves six of the tasks. Models at the frontier reach similar pass rates by solving different tasks: splitting tasks by the kind of work they require spreads the top five by 5.0pp on script-authoring tasks and 12.5pp on scene-change tasks.
  Exploration behavior predicts whether a run succeeds. Holding task and model fixed, a run that inspects every object a reference solution touches before acting on it passes 13.4pp more often than a run that inspects none of them on scene-only tasks, and 9.8pp more often on script-only tasks.
  We release the task suite, its place files, the per-task annotations, a plugin that runs the tasks inside Roblox Studio, and an updated leaderboard under the MIT license at https://github.com/Roblox/open-game-eval.

</details>


### [87] [DISSOLVR: An Interpretable and Fast Framework for Aqueous and Organic Solubility Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02574)
*Vansh Ramani,Har Ashish Arora,Dhairya Kuchhal,Sayan Ranu,Tarak Karmakar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出透明框架DISSOLVR用于分子溶解度预测，在无需深度学习黑箱架构的前提下达到当前最优性能，接近实验不确定性的随机极限，并通过物理描述符实现OOD泛化，同时构建LLM辅助的事后解释流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真溶解度预测对药物开发和环境分配至关重要，需要将分子结构与热力学行为在不同化学环境中耦合建模。然而近期研究由深度学习架构主导，往往以牺牲物理可解释性换取预测能力。本文旨在挑战这一趋势，证明当前最优性能并不必然依赖非透明架构。

Method: 提出透明框架DISSOLVR进行分子溶解度预测；开展全面的文献综述与多方法基准测试；通过将分子映射到物理上有依据的描述符以实现结构不变性；构建LLM辅助的事后解释流程，将符号化模型产物与化学上有根据的叙述相连接；并对22位专家化学家进行问卷调查与比较基准评估。

Result: DISSOLVR接近实验不确定性的随机极限（aleatoric limit），并通过结构不变性实现OOD泛化；LLM辅助解释流程有效连接了符号模型产物与化学叙事；22位专家化学家的比较基准调查显示专家评估者提供了深入见解。

Conclusion: 溶解度预测的当前最优性能无需依赖非透明深度学习架构即可实现；透明、物理有依据的建模在达到高性能的同时兼顾可解释性与OOD泛化，LLM辅助解释与专家评估进一步增强了模型的化学可理解性。

Abstract: High-fidelity solubility prediction is fundamental to pharmaceutical development and environmental partitioning, where accurate modeling must couple molecular structure with thermodynamic behavior across diverse chemical environments. However, recent advancements have been dominated by deep learning architectures that often sacrifice physical interpretability for predictive power. We challenge this trend by showing that state-of-the-art performance does not require such non-transparent architectures. To address this, we introduce DISSOLVR, a transparent framework for molecular solubility prediction. In addition, we perform a comprehensive literature review and a benchmarking study against various methods. We show that DISSOLVR approaches the aleatoric limit of experimental uncertainty and achieves OOD generalization through structural invariance, derived by mapping molecules to physically-grounded descriptors. Then, we present an LLM-assisted post-hoc explanation pipeline that bridges the gap between symbolic model artifacts and chemically grounded narratives. Finally, a comparative benchmark of a survey involving 22 expert chemists reveals that expert evaluators provide deep insights.

</details>


### [88] [Dense Mixture-of-Experts as a Reparameterized Wide FFN: A Granularity Sweep at Fixed Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02584)
*Vu Quang Hoang,Nghia Hieu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在固定总FFN宽度下，用稠密MoE类比（K个全激活SwiGLU专家+softmax门控）隔离动态专家组合的贡献，发现验证损失随K非单调变化，K=2最优，并揭示该架构等价于带token依赖单纯形约束组缩放的稠密SwiGLU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE模型将学习到的路由与大规模专家池中的选择相结合，但其性能提升究竟来自"动态专家组合"本身，还是来自"更大的专家池/离散选择"，尚不清楚。作者希望隔离动态专家组合这一单一因素的贡献。

Method: 构建稠密类比：在固定总FFN宽度下使用K个SwiGLU专家，所有专家对每个token都激活，并由softmax门控加权组合。此时没有更大的专家池、也没有离散选择，K=1即对应稠密基线。通过对比K=1、2、4、6的验证损失进行消融，并对路由分布（soft/hard、专家使用均衡性）进行诊断，还通过强制门控均匀化测试其功能重要性。此外进行理论分析，证明该架构的函数形式。

Result: 验证损失随K非单调变化：K=2比基线降低0.0048，而K=4和K=6分别升高0.0053和0.0197。路由总体上保持soft且专家使用均衡，唯独K=4模型第一层路由接近one-hot。对该门控强制均匀会使诊断子集上的损失增加2.4 nats，说明其集中路由具有功能重要性。

Conclusion: 该架构精确等价于具有token依赖、单纯形约束组缩放的稠密SwiGLU，且其函数类包含稠密基线。单次运行结果表明，在固定宽度下，always-active、softly gated的FFN存在一个粒度"甜点"（sweet spot），在所测配置中K=2表现最佳。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models combine learned routing with selection from a large expert pool. We isolate the contribution of dynamic expert combination using a dense analogue: $K$ SwiGLU experts, all active for every token and combined by a softmax gate, at fixed total FFN width. With no larger pool or discrete selection, the dense baseline is the $K=1$ case. Validation loss varies non-monotonically with $K$: $K=2$ improves over the baseline by $0.0048$, whereas $K=4$ and $K=6$ worsen it by $0.0053$ and $0.0197$, respectively. Routing generally remains soft and expert usage balanced, except in the first layer of the $K=4$ model, where routing is nearly one-hot. Forcing this gate to uniform increases loss by $2.4$ nats on a diagnostic subset, indicating that its concentrated routing is functionally important. We further show that the architecture is exactly a dense SwiGLU with token-dependent, simplex-constrained group scaling and contains the dense baseline in its function class. These single-run results suggest a granularity sweet spot for always-active, softly gated FFNs at fixed width, with $K=2$ performing best among the configurations tested.

</details>


### [89] [Fisher-Guided Submodular Data Selection for Continual Pre-Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02593)
*Zhenghao Zhao,Gaowen Liu,Zhiling Lan,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出Fisher感知的持续预训练数据选择器，通过分解梯度为锚定分量与前沿分量并以对数行列式次模目标优化，在医疗域CPT中同时提升目标域质量、抑制遗忘，且token效率比回放策略高10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续预训练中，数据选择不仅是噪声与预算问题，更是遗忘控制问题：选得不当的目标域语料会覆盖预训练检查点中的能力。现有做法要么用困惑度等参数无关标量评分，要么耗费大量通用域回放token来缓解遗忘，二者都未直接考察"在候选数据上训练将如何移动模型参数"。

Method: 作者先揭示机制：基于损失的选择会使CPT后的Fisher对角在预训练模型已承诺的高Fisher坐标上下降，而低Fisher坐标几乎不受影响，这种不对称构成参数空间层面的灾难性遗忘机制。据此提出Fisher感知选择器，将每个候选样本的梯度分解为锚定分量（沿已承诺参数方向的扰动）与前沿分量（在未约束的低Fisher子空间中的更新容量），用对数行列式次模目标聚合，并通过可扩展的流式数据选择管线单遍优化。

Result: 在TinyLlama-1.1B与Llama-3.1-8B上针对医疗数据的CPT实验中，选择器在提升目标域质量的同时将留出预训练基准上的遗忘控制在有限范围。1B选中token在适应性与遗忘两项指标上均优于用10B token训练的回放策略，取得10倍token效率优势。

Conclusion: 以参数空间（Fisher信息）视角进行CPT数据选择，可同时兼顾目标域适应与预训练能力保持；相比遗忘感知回放，该方法在同等甚至更优效果下大幅降低token开销，为高效持续预训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Data selection is already a central bottleneck in large-language-model training, where web-scale corpora are noisy and token budgets are finite. In continual pre-training (CPT), it becomes a forgetting-control problem: a poorly chosen target-domain corpus can overwrite capabilities encoded in the pretrained checkpoint. Existing CPT practice either scores candidates with parameter-agnostic scalars such as perplexity, or mitigates forgetting by spending many extra general-domain replay tokens. Neither strategy directly asks how training on a candidate will move the model parameters. We show that loss-based selection causes the post-CPT Fisher diagonal to drift downward on exactly the high-Fisher coordinates the pretrained model had committed to, while leaving low-Fisher coordinates largely untouched. This asymmetry exposes a parameter-space mechanism for catastrophic forgetting. Motivated by this observation, we propose a Fisher-aware CPT selector that decomposes each candidate's gradient into an anchor component, which measures perturbation along committed parameter directions, and a frontier component, which measures update capacity in unconstrained low-Fisher subspaces. We aggregate these signals with a log-determinant submodular objective and optimize it in a single pass using a scalable streaming data selection pipeline. On TinyLlama-1.1B and Llama-3.1-8B CPT over medical data, our selector improves target-domain quality while bounding forgetting on held-out pretraining benchmarks. Most importantly, it is substantially more token-efficient than forgetting-aware replay. 1B selected tokens already outperform the replay strategy trained with 10B tokens on both adaptation and forgetting, giving a 10x token-efficiency advantage.

</details>


### [90] [How Causality Bridges the Semantic Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02594)
*Shuhao Zhang,Xuran Zhou,Han Guo,Pengtao Xie,Yujia Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构约束的语义对齐任务及CausalBridge框架：从观测量中发现含隐变量的因果图，在因果依赖关系约束下求解未命名变量的嵌入（以少量已知名为锚点），再经语言模型输出名称。在5个问卷和3个机器人场景（遮蔽20%-90%变量名）上，其恢复观测与隐变量语义的准确率优于基于关联的现有方法，且在文档越少时优势越大；发现图与文档图命名准确率相当，命名新系统只需数分钟且成本远低于采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值测量刻画系统行为，但变量的含义常未标明：部分变量被测量却无名，部分根本未被测量（潜变量）。现有方法借助通用人类知识为其赋义，既继承了知识存在处的偏见，又在知识缺失处无能为力。作者希望用不含人类知识偏见的因果结构，从变量对其他变量的作用方式读出其语义。

Method: 形式化为"结构约束的语义对齐"：在因果图蕴含的依赖关系下求解每个未命名变量的嵌入，并以少数已知名称的嵌入作为锚点。实现为CausalBridge框架，包含三步——(1) 仅从测量数据中发现因果图（含隐变量）；(2) 在这些依赖关系约束下求解变量嵌入；(3) 通过语言模型将嵌入表达为名称。

Result: 在5个问卷与3个机器人场景上（遮蔽20%–90%变量名）评测：恢复观测变量与潜变量语义的准确率高于依赖关联的现有方法，且系统文档越少其领先幅度越大；所发现因果图与文档化因果图的命名准确率相当；命名一个新系统仅需数分钟，成本远低于采样类方法。

Conclusion: 因果结构反映生成测量的机制且仅从数据本身恢复，可能成为不受人类知识偏见影响的唯一信息源。一旦语义鸿沟被忠实弥合，机器即可理解世界并以因果方式采取行动。

Abstract: Numerical measurements capture how a system behaves, but often leave the meanings of its variables unspecified. Some variables are measured but never labeled, and others are never measured at all. Existing methods assign semantics to such variables by consulting general human knowledge, but this inherits its biases where that knowledge exists and offers nothing where it does not. We bridge this gap between measurements and their meanings with causal structure instead, reading a variable's semantics from how it acts on other variables. We formalize this as structure-constrained semantic alignment, in which the embedding of each unnamed variable is solved under the dependence relations implied by the causal graph, with the embeddings of a few known names as anchors. Accordingly, we build CausalBridge, a framework that discovers the causal graph from the measurements, latent variables included, solves for the embeddings under those relations, and expresses them as names through a language model. The causal structure reflects the mechanism that generated the measurements and is recovered from the measurements alone, which may make it the one source of information free of bias from human knowledge. We evaluate CausalBridge on five questionnaires and three robotics scenarios, with 20 to 90% of the variable names masked. It recovers the semantics of observed and latent variables more accurately than existing methods that rely on association, and its lead widens as less of the system is documented. The graph it discovers names variables as accurately as the documented one, and a new system is named in minutes and at a fraction of the cost of sampling methods. Once the semantic gap is bridged faithfully, machines can understand the world and take actions causally.

</details>


### [91] [Activation Sparsity with Weight Approximation for Faster LLM Decoding on Offloaded Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02598)
*JuneHyung Kim,Sankeerth Durvasula,Nandita Vijaykumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpAx提出将激活稀疏性中"读取/不读取权重"的二元选择扩展为三种选择（完整保留、压缩近似、完全省略），在消费级GPU内存不足场景下显著加速LLM解码，同时控制模型质量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费级GPU上部署LLM时，显存不足以容纳全部权重，解码过程需反复从系统RAM或闪存将卸载的权重传输到GPU，带宽远低于本地显存访问，导致推理极其缓慢。激活稀疏性可通过跳过与零/近零激活相关的权重来减少传输，但随着省略的激活贡献增多，模型质量会急剧下降，表明小幅激活对应的权重对质量有尖锐的集体影响，因此需要在质量与性能间取得更好折中。

Method: 核心思想是把"是否读取权重"的二元选择替换为三种选项：完全保留、用压缩权重表示近似、或完全省略。SpAx对激活最接近零的权重直接省略，对幅度较小的激活读取近似权重，对幅度最大的激活读取原始权重。小幅激活会衰减近似权重引入的误差，而压缩权重表示所需传输字节更少。

Result: 权重卸载到CPU内存时，SpAx使解码平均加速3.86倍（最高5.57倍）于16位权重，2.06倍（最高2.74倍）于4位权重，WikiText-2困惑度增加最多10%。权重卸载到闪存时，加速分别为平均3.31倍（最高4.81倍）和1.54倍（最高2.03倍）。

Conclusion: SpAx通过三选项的激活稀疏策略，在权重卸载场景下有效改善了模型质量与解码性能的权衡。

Abstract: Deploying LLMs on consumer-grade GPUs with insufficient memory to hold their weights can result in prohibitively slow inference, because decoding repeatedly transfers offloaded weights from system RAM or flash storage into GPU at much lower bandwidth than local GPU-memory access. Activation sparsity reduces these transfers by skipping weights associated with zero or near-zero activations. However, as more activation contributions are omitted, model quality eventually degrades rapidly, indicating that weights associated with small-magnitude activations collectively influence model quality sharply. In this work, we improve the trade-off between model quality and decoding performance when exploiting activation sparsity. Our key idea is to replace the binary choice of whether or not to read a weight with three options: fully retain it, approximate it using a compressed weight representation, or omit it entirely. SpAx skips weights associated with activations closest to zero, reads approximate weights for smaller-magnitude activations, and reads original weights for the largest-magnitude activations. Smaller-magnitude activations attenuate the errors introduced by approximate weights, while compressed weight representations require fewer bytes to be transferred. With weights offloaded to CPU memory, SpAx speeds up decoding by 3.86X on average (up to 5.57X) with 16-bit weights and 2.06X (up to 2.74X) with 4-bit weights, at a WikiText-2 perplexity increase of at most 10%. With weights offloaded to flash storage, the speedups are 3.31X on average (up to 4.81X) and 1.54X (up to 2.03X).

</details>


### [92] [Seer: Maximum Likelihood Regression for Learning-Speed Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02610)
*Carl Myers Kadie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Seer 系统，通过经验观测与统计建模来刻画和预测分类学习性能，包括预测达到目标精度所需训练样本数及无限样本下的极限精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习性能通常只能通过反复实验观测，缺乏可预测的定量模型。作者希望建立能刻画分类学习性能的极大似然统计模型，从而在有限实验基础上预测学习行为，减少试错成本。

Method: Seer 先产生分类学习性能的经验观测，再用这些观测拟合统计模型。模型设计上引入适合分类学习的最优约束和有用参数；将模型选择建模为非线性规划，并利用问题约束将其约简为可用高效迭代算法求解的非线性回归任务，从而高效获得极大似然模型。

Result: 在 soybean disease、heart disease 与 audiological problems 三个领域的近 100 个实验中，Seer 对学习性能的刻画表现优异，其预测学习性能的能力似乎已达到尽可能好的水平。

Conclusion: Seer 能有效建模并预测分类学习性能，其模型可估计达到目标精度所需训练样本数及无限样本下的最大精度。论文最后给出了针对具体情境选择回归模型的建议，并指出后续研究方向。

Abstract: The research presented here focuses on modeling machine-learning performance. The thesis introduces Seer, a system that generates empirical observations of classification-learning performance and then uses those observations to create statistical models. The models can be used to predict the number of training examples needed to achieve a desired level and the maximum accuracy possible given an unlimited number of training examples. Seer advances the state of the art with 1) models that embody the best constraints for classification learning and most useful parameters, 2) algorithms that efficiently find maximum-likelihood models, and 3) a demonstration on real-world data from three domains of a practicable application of such modeling.
  The first part of the thesis gives an overview of the requirements for a good maximum-likelihood model of classification-learning performance. Next, reasonable design choices for such models are explored. Selection among such models is a task of nonlinear programming, but by exploiting appropriate problem constraints, the task is reduced to a nonlinear regression task that can be solved with an efficient iterative algorithm. The latter part of the thesis describes almost 100 experiments in the domains of soybean disease, heart disease, and audiological problems. The tests show that Seer is excellent at characterizing learning-performance and that it seems to be as good as possible at predicting learning performance. Finally, recommendations for choosing a regression model for a particular situation are made and directions for further research are identified.

</details>


### [93] [Scale-Recursive Rectified Flows for Few-Step Precipitation Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02611)
*Shunya Nagashima,Takumi Bannai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出尺度递归修正流（scale-recursive rectified flow），通过比较不同空间尺度上集合变异与预测误差来引导采样步数分配，改善卫星降水降尺度中减少采样步数导致的集合变异不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率降水估计对洪水风险评估与水资源管理很重要，但粗分辨率卫星产品无法解析格点内部降水。生成模型可产生多条合理的高分辨率降水场来刻画这种不确定性，但减少采样步数虽能加速生成，却会使集合成员过于相似、低估不确定性。

Method: 提出尺度递归修正流：先生成大尺度降水格局，再生成局部细节；通过比较各空间尺度上集合变异与预测误差来指导采样步数分配，并利用验证分数与降水功率谱约束该分配，避免过度放大。在美国本土的卫星到雷达降尺度任务上进行了验证。

Result: 分析表明，在采样预算受限时，大尺度降水格局是集合变异不足的主要来源。将更多步数分配给粗尺度流，在固定架构与计算成本下、跨训练种子均提升了概率精度与降水检测能力。所提模型还以更短采样时间取得了优于使用更多步数的非递归流的概率精度。

Conclusion: 尺度递归的步数分配策略能在不增加计算成本的前提下提升集合变异与概率精度，是缓解少步采样下不确定性低估的有效途径。

Abstract: Fine-resolution precipitation estimates support flood risk assessment and water management, but coarse satellite products cannot resolve rainfall within each grid cell. Generative models address this ambiguity by producing ensembles of plausible high-resolution rainfall fields. Among these models, rectified flows generate samples by iteratively transforming random noise into rainfall fields. Reducing the number of sampling steps accelerates generation but can make ensemble members too similar, understating uncertainty. We propose a scale-recursive rectified flow that generates broad patterns before local details and guides sampling-step allocation by comparing ensemble variability with prediction error across spatial scales. Validation scores and rainfall power spectra constrain the allocation to avoid excessive amplification. In satellite-to-radar downscaling over the contiguous United States, our analysis identified broad rainfall patterns as the main source of insufficient ensemble variability under reduced sampling budgets. Allocating more steps to the coarse flow improved probabilistic accuracy and rain detection across training seeds at fixed architecture and computational cost. The proposed model also achieved better probabilistic accuracy with shorter sampling time than a nonrecursive flow using more steps.

</details>


### [94] [What Is Lost in Post-Training? Default Collapse and the Loss of In-Context Steerability Across Diverse Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02614)
*Jessica Dierking,Itai Shapira,Niclas Boehmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 后训练不仅收窄LLM默认观点，还损害其情境化转向未被训练偏好视角的能力；作者通过微调与检查点评估验证该退化，并将其刻画为"单一价值优先"与"服务多元用户的技术能力"之间的张力，进而提出以规定观点分布为约束、最大化奖励的目标及stance-distribution matching实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI需面向异质人群、按用户与情境采取合适立场，但既有研究只关注后训练对默认行为的影响，忽视了模型对情境信息进行适应（in-context steering）的能力，因此需考察后训练是否削弱这种可转向性。

Method: 在受控实验中，将模型朝文化价值分歧中的某一方进行微调，并在训练全程评测多个检查点，观察被训练立场的支配程度与对相反立场识别及忠实表达能力的动态变化；随后提出并分析一种替代目标：在规定观点分布约束下最大化奖励，并以stance-distribution matching作为工程实现。

Result: 随训练推进，被训练一方在常规使用中日益占主导，而模型识别并忠实执行对立视角的能力持续下降，表明后训练会降低情境化转向能力。

Conclusion: 优先固化单一价值集合与保持服务多元相关方的技术能力之间存在张力；采用观点分布约束下的奖励最大化（如stance-distribution matching）可作为缓解路径。

Abstract: AI models serving a heterogeneous population must act on the principles appropriate to each user and context. While post-training has been shown to narrow the views large language models express, prior work has focused on default behavior rather than the ability to adapt to in-context information. We show that post-training also degrades a model's ability to be steered in-context toward perspectives it was not trained to favor. In controlled experiments, we fine-tune models toward one side of cultural-value disagreements and evaluate checkpoints throughout training. The trained side becomes increasingly dominant in ordinary use, while the ability to recognize and faithfully enact the opposing view declines. These findings point to a tension between prioritizing a single set of values and preserving the technical capacity needed to serve diverse stakeholders. Finally, we propose and analyze an alternative objective that maximizes reward subject to a prescribed distribution over expressed perspectives, and present stance-distribution matching as a practical implementation.

</details>


### [95] [Quantifying the Value of Constructive Induction, Knowledge, and Noise Filtering on Inductive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02615)
*Carl M. Kadie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"有效维度"(effective dimension)这一新的学习度量，用于将学习问题属性与平均情况学习性能联系起来，兼具VC维的线性关系特性且可经验估计和进行平均情况预测，并在包括反向传播在内的多个学习系统上验证，还精确预测了FRINGE特征构造系统的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习研究的核心目标之一是度量、建模并理解学习问题属性如何影响平均情况下的学习性能，例如量化构造性归纳、噪声过滤和背景知识的价值。现有度量（如VC维）虽与问题属性呈简单线性关系，但难以经验估计，也无法进行平均情况预测，限制了其在机器学习和人类学习研究中的广泛应用。

Method: 提出新的学习度量——有效维度(effective dimension)，其与问题属性通常呈简单线性关系（类似VC维），但与VC维不同之处在于可经验估计并能给出平均情况预测。随后在多个学习系统（包括反向传播Backpropagation）上对该度量进行演示，并将其用于预测FRINGE特征构造系统的收益。

Result: 有效维度在包括反向传播在内的多个学习系统上得到验证；使用该度量精确预测了FRINGE特征构造系统的收益，并发现该收益随着目标概念复杂度的增加而下降。

Conclusion: 有效维度是一种比VC维适用性更广的学习度量，能够连接学习问题属性与学习性能，可经验估计并做出平均情况预测，因而更广泛地适用于机器学习和人类学习研究。

Abstract: Learning research, as one of its central goals, tries to measure, model, and understand how learning-problem properties affect average-case learning performance. For example, we would like to quantify the value of constructive induction, noise filtering, and background knowledge. This paper describes the effective dimension, a new learning measure that helps link problem properties to learning performance. Like the Vapnik-Chervonenkis (VC) dimension, the effective dimension is often in a simple linear relation with problem properties. Unlike the VC dimension, the effective dimension can be estimated empirically and makes average-case predictions. It is therefore more widely applicable to machine and human learning research. The measure is demonstrated on several learning systems including Backpropagation. Finally, the measure is used to precisely predict the benefit of using FRINGE, a feature construction system. The benefit is found to decrease as the complexity of the target concept increases.

</details>


### [96] [Online Verification of Language Model Responses Under Cost Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02632)
*Erfan Hajihashemi,Yanning Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 OMVV 算法，通过维护多个弱验证器池并在线自适应路由，实现低成本、高准确率的 LLM 多步推理输出验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型多步推理输出验证需查询昂贵的真值预言机，在线逐步调用不现实；现有方法仅用单一固定弱验证器决定是否启用强验证器，但单一弱验证器难以应对查询主题与难度随时间变化，易导致成本过高或验证不准。

Method: OMVV 维护含 K 个不同成本与性能的候选弱验证器池，每轮通过在线分数组合器与指数权重路由策略自适应选择验证器进行决策，并给出假接受率与假拒绝率的无分布有限时间保证，同时在成本与一致性联合目标下对最优固定验证器实现次线性遗憾。

Result: 在推理数据集基准上，OMVV 在多种运行预算下相比任意单一固定验证器均实现更高准确率与更低验证成本。

Conclusion: OMVV 通过多验证器在线自适应路由，兼顾验证准确性与成本效率，优于单一固定验证器方案。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed for multi-step reasoning, verifying the correctness of their outputs has become essential for maintaining reliability at scale. Verifying the correctness of large language model outputs is often done by querying a costly ground-truth oracle, which is impractical to invoke at every step in an online setting. Prior work addresses this by querying a single weak verifier on every step, and using its score to decide whether the costly strong verifier needs to be queried as well, reserving strong verification for only a small fraction of the steps. However, a single fixed weak verifier may not perform consistently well as the subject matter or difficulty of incoming queries changes over time, and committing to one in advance risks either overly costly or inaccurate verification. We introduce OMVV (Online Multi-Verifier Verification), an algorithm that maintains a pool of $K$ candidate weak verifiers with differing cost and verification performance, and adaptively routes each round's decision to a verifier selected via an online score combiner and an exponential-weights routing policy. OMVV provides a distribution-free, finite-time guarantee on false-accept and false-reject rates across the full pool of verifiers, and further achieves sublinear regret against the best fixed verifier in hindsight under a combined cost and consistency objective. Experiments on reasoning dataset benchmarks show that OMVV achieves higher accuracy at lower verification cost than any single fixed verifier, across a range of operating budgets.

</details>


### [97] [Context-Tower Conversion Preserves Generation While Freezing Retains Knowledge: Low-Budget AR-to-Diffusion Conversion of MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02657)
*Wentao Lu,Jesse Clark,Tianyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在固定语料、监督令牌预算、可训练参数集与评测框架的条件下，对比同一30B MoE父模型的两类扩散语言模型转换：就地更新型与冻结塔式。1B令牌下，冻结塔式HumanEval pass@10为71.60，就地型仅6.19，提升11.6倍，并保留父模型95% GSM8K与99% MMLU-Pro性能。理论分析表明两类转换在硬注意力掩码与逐轮左到右承诺下均含AR父模型的精确采样器；冻结切断了经上下文状态的梯度。评测协议对已发表500B令牌转换的分数影响显著，而父模型波动小于3分，故需统一协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练自回归（AR）模型转换为扩散语言模型（dLLM）可实现并行生成而无需重新预训练，但已发表的转换方法在训练数据量上相差约三个数量级，且从未在统一协议下比较，导致难以判断各设计的真实优劣。本文旨在固定关键变量（语料、监督令牌预算、可训练参数集、评测框架）开展受控对比。

Method: 以同一30B MoE父模型为对象，比较两类转换，各自沿用其原训练配方：1）就地（in-place）模型，用去噪损失与表征对齐损失更新父模型部分权重；2）冻结塔（frozen-tower）模型，通过交叉注意力条件化于父模型的冻结因果副本。另做稠密父模型实验以验证结论可复现，并在约500M令牌下比较冻结/训练上下文塔。理论层面分析两类转换在硬注意力掩码与每轮左到右承诺一个位置下的精确采样性质，并在共享损失下分析冻结对梯度的影响。同时考察评测协议对已发表500B令牌转换结果的影响。

Result: 1B训练令牌预算下，冻结塔式HumanEval pass@10达71.60，就地型仅6.19，提升11.6倍；同一预算下冻结塔式保留父模型95%的GSM8K与99%的MMLU-Pro。稠密父模型实验复现了HumanEval上的分离。两塔设计中约500M令牌时，冻结上下文塔比训练它保留显著更多MMLU-Pro性能，两者观测到的HumanEval相近。理论证明两类转换均含AR父模型在硬注意力掩码与逐轮左到右承诺下的精确采样器；共享损失下冻结消除经上下文状态的梯度贡献。评测协议使已发表500B令牌转换在各任务上双向显著变化，而其AR父模型分数波动小于3分。

Conclusion: 在测试的低预算区间，冻结塔配置比就地转换显著保留更多父模型的生成性能。由于评测协议能大幅影响dLLM分数（而AR父模型相对稳定），比较不同dLLM必须在统一协议下进行。

Abstract: Converting a pretrained autoregressive (AR) model to a diffusion language model (dLLM) enables parallel generation without pretraining a new model. Published conversion methods differ by roughly three orders of magnitude in training data and have not been compared under a common protocol. We compare two conversions of the same 30B Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) parent, holding the corpus, supervised-token budget, trainable parameter set and evaluation harness fixed, each under its own training recipe. The in-place model updates a subset of the parent's weights using denoising and representation-alignment losses; the frozen-tower model instead conditions through cross-attention on a frozen causal copy of the parent. With 1B training tokens, the frozen-tower model scores 71.60 on HumanEval pass@10 against 6.19 for the in-place model, an 11.6x improvement. At the same budget it also keeps 95% of the parent's GSM8K score and 99% of its MMLU-Pro score. A dense-parent experiment reproduces the HumanEval separation. Within the two-tower design at about 500M tokens, freezing the context tower retains substantially more MMLU-Pro performance than training it, while both give similar observed HumanEval scores. Our theoretical analysis establishes that both conversion classes contain an exact sampler for the AR parent under a hard attention mask and left-to-right commitment of one position per round. Under a shared loss, freezing removes the gradient contribution through the context states. Furthermore, evaluation protocol substantially affects a published 500B-token conversion's scores in both directions across tasks, while its AR parent's scores vary by less than three points, so comparing dLLMs needs a common protocol. These results show that, in the tested low-budget regime, the frozen-tower configuration retains substantially more of the parent's generation performance than in-place conversion.

</details>


### [98] [AIGS: Adaptive Incremental Gating System for Online Representation Learning in Non-Stationary Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02661)
*SiRui He,Kai Liang Lew,Chui Zi Ong,Chean Khim Toa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应增量门控系统（AIGS），通过内源残差反馈的Shock Ratio驱动连续可塑性控制器，在稳定性-可塑性间平滑权衡，实现O(k·d)每步复杂度的在线表示学习，适用于边缘设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web of Things与边缘计算中的实时数据流存在潜在的状态转变（regime changes）。在严格算力约束下进行在线表示学习，核心难题是稳定性-可塑性困境：既要保留有用的历史知识，又要快速响应概念漂移。现有方法采用固定更新计划或滚动窗口，在突变时出现参数僵化，而在流平稳时又浪费计算资源。

Method: 提出自适应增量门控系统（AIGS），一个轻量级闭环状态感知适应框架。核心是Shock Ratio——一种内源残差反馈机制，用近期变异对当前重构误差进行归一化；该信号驱动连续可塑性控制器，在学习可塑性与记忆保持之间平滑插值。将表示学习视为闭环控制机制。

Result: 在真实智慧城市动态流（交通网络、气象系统、工业基础设施）上实验，呈现明显的领域依赖优势：在Electricity Transformer Temperature数据集上，渐进退化场景下实现预防性预警提前量8.31（ETTm1）与9.88（ETTm2）步；在Performance Measurement System交通数据集上，突变后恢复显著更快；在高噪声Weather数据集上，提升异常召回率并抵抗随机噪声过拟合。

Conclusion: AIGS避免了灾难性遗忘，保持严格线性的O(k·d)每步复杂度，适合延迟敏感的边端设备，是资源受限边缘监控系统中实用的即插即用适配器。

Abstract: Real-time data streams in Web of Things (WoT) and edge computing environments often evolve through latent regime changes. For online representation learning under strict computational constraints, the central problem is resolving the stability-plasticity dilemma: keeping useful historical knowledge while rapidly reacting to concept drift. Existing methods employ fixed update schedules or rolling windows. However, they suffer from parameter ossification during sudden shifts and waste computational resources when the stream remains stable. This paper proposes the Adaptive Incremental Gating System (AIGS), a lightweight closed-loop state-aware adaptation framework. AIGS introduces the Shock Ratio, an endogenous residual feedback mechanism that normalizes current reconstruction error against recent variation. This signal drives a Continuous Plasticity Controller that smoothly interpolates between learning plasticity and memory retention. By treating representation learning as a closed-loop control mechanism, AIGS avoids catastrophic forgetting and maintains a strictly linear $\mathcal{O}\left(k\cdot d\right)$ per-step complexity suitable for latency-sensitive edge devices. Experiments on real-world smart city dynamic streams-spanning traffic networks, meteorological systems, and industrial infrastructure-demonstrate distinct domain-dependent advantages. On Electricity Transformer Temperature datasets, AIGS achieves preventative early-warning lead times of 8.31 (ETTm1) and 9.88 (ETTm2) steps under gradual degradation. On Performance Measurement System traffic datasets, it shows significantly faster post-shift recovery after abrupt mutations. On the highly noisy Weather dataset, it improves anomaly recall while resisting stochastic noise overfitting. These findings establish AIGS as a practical, plug-and-play adapter for resource-constrained edge monitoring systems.

</details>


### [99] [Mind the Refinement Gap: When Safe High-Level Robot Plans Produce Unsafe Executions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02662)
*Stabak Das,Priyesh Ranjan,Xiangfang Li,Lijun Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审计语言驱动机器人系统中"轨迹完备性"假设，发现监视器所检查的高层动作序列未必反映执行期真实的导航与隐式动作效应，并提出基于图的轨迹细化作为轻量级缓解与诊断手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言使能的机器人系统日益将语义图规划与时间逻辑安全监视器结合。此类系统隐含一个轨迹完备性假设：监视器检查的高层动作序列应能代表执行过程中产生的导航与隐式动作效应。若该假设不成立，安全监视器的判定将失效，因此需要对该假设进行审计。

Method: 在RoboGuard中审计该假设：在同一LTL规范下，比较监视器对"表层计划"（surface plan）的判定与对"图细化轨迹"（graph-refined trace）的判定。评估包含28个受控案例，覆盖五个动作抽象族，以及14个端到端案例——其中SPINE [1] 从自然语言指令生成计划，RoboGuard生成场景落地的安全规范。

Result: 在受控评估中，全部12个目标抽象案例均表现出预测的表层与细化轨迹之间的判定差异，而全部16个对照案例均符合预期。端到端案例进一步验证了该现象在自然语言到安全规范的真实流程中的存在。

Conclusion: 高层动作序列存在轨迹完备性缺口，监视器的表层判定可能遗漏执行期的隐式效应。基于图的轨迹细化可作为轻量级缓解措施，同时也是面向物理AI安全监视器的有效诊断工具。

Abstract: Language-enabled robot systems increasingly combine semantic-graph planning with temporal-logic safety monitors. We investigate a trace-completeness assumption in these systems: whether the high-level action sequence checked by a monitor represents the navigation and implicit action effects induced during execution. We audit this assumption in RoboGuard by comparing its verdict on a surface plan with its verdict on a graph-refined trace under the same Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) specification. Our evaluation comprises 28 controlled cases spanning five action-abstraction families and 14 end-to-end cases in which SPINE [1] generates plans from natural-language instructions while RoboGuard generates scene-grounded safety specifications. In the controlled evaluation, all 12 targeted abstraction cases exhibit the predicted surface-versus-refined discrepancy while all 16 controls behave as expected, motivating graph-based trace refinement as a lightweight mitigation and a diagnostic tool for physical-AI safety monitors.

</details>


### [100] [LEAP: Learning Efficient Action Proposals For LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02670)
*Zhen Xu,Qizheng Zhang,Gerry Wan,Shang Zhu,Ce Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动作推测（action speculation）延迟框架，揭示端到端加速的决定因素，并据此设计 LEAP：用小规模 drafter 在目标动作序列上训练，实现最高 60% 端到端加速且任务成功率不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 逐 步 rollout 速度慢：每步须先推理再选动作，串行依赖使下一步无法提前开始。已有工作将推测解码思想迁移到动作阶段，但多用现成大模型做 drafter——大 drafter 匹配率高却耗时，小模型快却匹配率低。作者因此追问更本质的问题：究竟是什么决定了动作推测的端到端加速？

Method: 建立动作推测轮次（speculative round）的延迟框架，对比一轮的收益与代价：收益取决于 drafter 对目标模型的预测匹配度及任务在结束前可执行的步数；代价来自 drafting、等待目标验证以及工具执行。基于该框架提出 LEAP，保持 drafter 规模小（0.6B），通过在目标动作序列上训练提升其准确度，并支持无需预采集轨迹的在线训练。

Result: 使用 0.6B 小模型时，LEAP 在多数决策上与目标一致，使 agent 端到端 wall clock 时间最高加速 60%，且任务成功率无系统性变化。在多种数据集、目标模型与 draft 模型上，该框架能解释大部分实测加速。在线训练可达到与离线训练相当的表现，且无需事先收集轨迹。

Conclusion: 动作推测的加速由收益—代价权衡决定，而非单纯依赖 drafter 规模；通过让小型 drafter 在目标动作上训练，可在保持成功率的同时显著降低端到端延迟，且在线训练使其易于实际部署。

Abstract: LLM agents are known to be slow in rollouts. An agent completes a task one step at a time. At each step, it reasons and then chooses an action to execute. The next step and action cannot start until the previous one has finished. Speculative decoding accelerates the rollouts at the reason phase by drafting and verifying the inference tokens. Recent works have also started to apply similar ideas at the action phase. These works use off-the-shelf models, usually large, to draft action proposals for target model to verify. Large drafters match the target more often but take longer to propose, while small off-the-shelf models are fast but rarely make the same decision as the target. We ask a more general question: what determines the end-to-end speedup of action speculation? To answer it, we develop a latency framework for the speculative round. The framework compares what a round gains with what it costs. The gain depends on how well the drafter predicts the target and on how many steps the task can take before it ends. The cost comes from drafting, from waiting for target verification and from executing tools. Guided by the framework, we introduce LEAP (Learning Efficient Action Proposals) which keeps the drafter small and makes it accurate by training it on the target actions sequences. With a small 0.6B model, LEAP agrees with the target on most decisions and makes agents up to 60% faster in end-to-end wall clock time, with no systematic change in task success. Across various datasets, target models and draft models, the framework accounts for most of the measured speedups. We also show the draft model can be online trained with no prior trace collection and match the performance of offline training, making LEAP practical to deploy in the real world.

</details>


### [101] [RAOA: Alternating-Operator Neural Computation with Programmable Radio Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02683)
*Toshiaki Koike-Akino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RAOA——一种在持久隐状态上交替执行"问题更新"与"混合更新"的循环计算架构，将可编程无线电传播从通信信道重新定位为计算深度，并在离散优化、可编程传播仿真与预训练模型适配三类任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可编程无线电传播多被视为通信信道或一次性模拟变换，作者追问其能否承担"计算深度"的角色；同时希望探索将交替算子计算、可编程射频传播与神经网络适配统一到同一循环框架中的可能性。

Method: 提出RAOA（Radio Alternating Operator Ansatz）：在持久隐状态上交替进行能量导出的问题更新与混合更新，每次混叠后重算问题场，使重复迭代具有组合性（即使各层复用同一组学习控制参数）。验证途径包括：精确离散优化、带约束的可编程传播仿真、被动式相位型自由空间模型近似所需算子，以及将RAOA作为零初始化残差适配器接入预训练语言模型。

Result: 离散目标上，重复执行可在不增加学习控制参数量的前提下提升解质量，且同一形式可直接处理高阶交互；被动相位型自由空间模型表明所需算子可被可编程传播近似，在存在实现误差时仍保留有用下游行为；作为零初始化残差适配器时，RAOA在三个模型家族上适配预训练语言模型的WikiText性能接近同等规模浅层MLP，但推理任务迁移效果依赖具体模型。

Conclusion: 该工作将交替算子计算、可编程射频传播与神经适配统一于一个循环框架；射频实现证据基于仿真而非硬件演示，硬件可行性仍待验证。

Abstract: Can programmable radio propagation serve as computational depth rather than only as a communication channel or one-shot analog transform? We introduce the Radio Alternating Operator Ansatz (RAOA), a recurrent computing architecture that alternates an energy-derived problem update with a mixing update over a persistent latent state. Recomputing the problem field after each mix makes repeated passes compositional even when the same learned controls are reused across depth. We evaluate this idea through exact discrete optimization, constrained programmable-propagation simulation, and pretrained-model adaptation. On discrete objectives, repeated execution can improve solution quality without increasing the learned-control count, and the same formulation handles higher-order interactions directly. A passive phase-only free-space model further shows that the required operators can be approximated by programmable propagation while retaining useful downstream behavior despite realization error. When inserted as a zero-initialized residual adapter, RAOA adapts pretrained language models with WikiText performance close to a matched shallow MLP across three model families, while reasoning-task transfer remains model-dependent. Together, these results connect alternating-operator computation, programmable radio propagation, and neural adaptation within one recurrent framework. The RF realization evidence is simulation-based rather than a hardware demonstration.

</details>


### [102] [Test-time Calibration Learning for Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02695)
*Zizhuo Zhang,Xiong Peng,Jingwei Sun,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无标签的测试时校准学习框架 TTCL，利用多响应自监督信号同时适配推理准确率与言语化置信度，在多个基准上显著提升准确率并降低 ECE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠 LLM 需既准确又使言语化置信度忠实反映正确概率。已有将校准学习融入强化学习的方法依赖真值正确性监督，在测试时无标签、且需适应新目标任务时适用性受限。

Method: TTCL 为无标签框架，直接在无标注目标任务数据上联合适配推理准确率与言语化置信度；从多个模型自生成响应中提取正确性与校准的自监督信号，实现测试时校准学习，并通过理论分析证明其为理想校准目标的有界代理。

Result: 在数学推理与事实问答任务上，跨模型与任务持续同时提升准确率与校准。基座模型在八个基准上平均相对准确率提升 +40.13%、ECE 降低 +70.80%；域偏移下（math-to-factQA）相对准确率提升 +20.35%、ECE 降低 +53.83%。

Conclusion: TTCL 以无标签自监督方式实现测试时联合校准与准确率适配，可提升未校准及已校准模型在域偏移下的表现，尤其适用于源域校准迁移差的目标任务。

Abstract: Reliable large language models (LLMs) must not only produce accurate answers but also express confidence that faithfully reflects their probability of being correct. Such calibration is essential for identifying uncertain predictions and supporting reliable decision-making in real-world deployment. Recent studies incorporate calibration learning into reinforcement learning (RL), jointly optimizing answer correctness and verbalized confidence using ground-truth correctness supervision. However, their reliance on labeled data limits their applicability in practical test-time settings, where ground-truth labels are unavailable and calibration may need to adapt to newly encountered target tasks. To address this challenge, we propose Test-Time Calibration Learning (TTCL), a label-free framework that jointly adapts reasoning accuracy and verbalized confidence directly on unlabeled target-task data. Specifically, TTCL derives self-supervision signals for both correctness and calibration from multiple model-generated responses, enabling calibration learning at test time without ground-truth labels. Theoretical analysis further establishes TTCL as a bounded surrogate for the ideal calibration objective. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and factual question answering demonstrate that TTCL consistently improves both accuracy and calibration across diverse models and tasks. On base models, TTCL achieves an average relative accuracy improvement of +40.13% and an ECE reduction of +70.80% across eight benchmarks. Moreover, TTCL can further improve both accuracy and calibration for already calibrated models under domain shift, particularly when source-domain calibration transfers poorly to target tasks. In the math-to-factQA setting, TTCL achieves an average relative accuracy gain of +20.35% and reduces ECE by +53.83%. The source code is released at https://github.com/tmlr-group/TTCL.

</details>


### [103] [Learning from Evolving Errors: Adaptive Iterative Repair for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02700)
*Rui Li,Liyang He,Zheng Zhang,Zhenya Huang,Linbo Zhu,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIR-OPD：面向同策略自蒸馏的自适应迭代修复框架，用"错误→修复"引导提供对齐监督，数学推理平均分最高提升3.6分且不损OOD性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）虽能在学生自身采样轨迹上提供密集的token级反馈，优于强化学习的结果级奖励，但其教师以完整参考解为条件，而学生无法获得该参考解。参考解只指明目标，未说明如何从学生当前错误走向目标，因而存在"以解为条件的捷径"风险。

Method: 提出AIR-OPD自适应迭代修复框架：给定失败回答，引导生成器针对当前错误合成修复引导；学生据此进行同策略重试，若仍错误，则为新错误生成新引导，迭代进行。每轮中固定教师以引导作为特权上下文，在最新失败回答中与错误对齐的区域上监督学生。并采用结果感知的阶段加权，偏向早期修复阶段，并对即时重试通过验证的阶段给予奖励。引导来源包括学生自身策略的自引导与更大模型的外部引导。

Result: 在DAPO-Math-17K上训练，于AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25评测，并做MMLU-Pro与GPQA的分布外测试。对Qwen3-4B与Qwen3-8B，AIR-OPD均取得最佳数学推理平均成绩，较最强基线最高提升3.6分，同时在分布外基准上保持基座模型性能。

Conclusion: 以"错误→修复"引导替代单纯的解条件监督，可有效缓解OPSD的捷径问题，在提升数学推理能力的同时保持通用能力不受损。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) supplies dense token-level feedback on trajectories sampled from the student's own policy, a richer training signal than the outcome-level rewards of reinforcement learning. This feedback comes from a teacher conditioned on a full reference solution unavailable to the student. The reference solution specifies the target but not how to move from the student's current error toward it, creating a solution-conditioned shortcut risk. We introduce AIR-OPD, an adaptive iterative repair framework for on-policy distillation that provides error-to-repair supervision. Given a failed response, a guidance generator synthesizes repair guidance for the current error. The student samples an on-policy retry with this guidance. If the retry remains incorrect, the generator produces new repair guidance for the newly observed error. At each round, a fixed teacher receives the guidance as privileged context and supervises the student on an error-aligned region of its latest failed response. Outcome-aware stage weighting favors early repair stages and credits stages whose immediate retry passes verification. We train AIR-OPD on the DAPO-Math-17K dataset and evaluate on AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25, alongside out-of-distribution tests on MMLU-Pro and GPQA. We examine two guidance sources, self-guidance from the current student policy and external guidance from a larger model. For both Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, AIR-OPD attains the best mathematical-reasoning averages, improving over the strongest baseline by up to 3.6 points, while preserving base-model performance on the out-of-distribution benchmarks.

</details>


### [104] [Conditional Capacity and Routing in Mixture-of-Experts Particle Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02701)
*Kaushik Pendiyala,Haris Zia,Trevin Lee,Timothy Legge,Alejandro J. De Leon,Zihan Zhao,Aaron Wang,Abhijith Gandrakota,Jennifer Ngadiuba,Richard Cavanaugh,Javier Duarte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在188类JetClass-II任务上对比稠密与MoE粒子Transformer，发现避免token丢弃时top-1 MoE以近乎不变的标称前向计算量超越稠密基线，而继续增加存储专家数收益甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE可在不按比例增加激活计算量的前提下提升参数容量，但该权衡关系在粒子物理Transformer中尚不明确，需要系统刻画存储容量、激活计算、路由容量与路由组织各自的作用。

Method: 在JetClass-II（188类）上训练稠密与MoE粒子Transformer，系统变化专家数量、路由容量、top-K及辅助损失，并进行路由分配与粒子身份、运动学关联性的分析。

Result: 避免token丢弃时，top-1 MoE在标称前向计算量近乎不变的情况下优于稠密基线；进一步增加存储专家数带来的精度增益很小；每token激活多个专家可提升预测性能但计算成本更高。路由分析显示部分配置下专家分配与粒子身份和运动学关联更强，但这种结构并不随分类性能单调增强。

Conclusion: 评估面向喷注分类的稀疏专家模型时，需区分存储参数容量、激活计算量、路由容量与路由组织；top-1路由是低成本提升性能的有效方式，而容量扩展与路由结构化程度并非性能的单调驱动因素。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models can increase parameter capacity without proportionally increasing active computation, but it is unclear how this trade-off behaves in particle-physics transformers. We study dense and MoE Particle Transformers on 188-class JetClass-II, varying expert count, routing capacity, top-K, and auxiliary loss. We find that, when token dropping is avoided, top-1 MoE models improve over the dense baseline at nearly unchanged nominal forward compute, while further increasing the number of stored experts produces little additional accuracy gain. Activating multiple experts per token yields additional predictive improvements at higher computational cost. Routing analyses show that expert assignments become more strongly associated with particle identity and kinematics in some configurations, but this structure does not increase monotonically with classification performance. These results highlight the need to distinguish stored parameter capacity, active computation, routing capacity, and routing organization when evaluating sparse expert models for jet classification. Code and experiment configurations are available at https://github.com/kpendiyala/MPT.

</details>


### [105] [MuonIO: Principled Norm-Aware Descent for Embedding Tables and Language Model Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02705)
*Linkai Ma,Xinyu Luo,Mengbo Wang,Ananth Grama,Petros Drineas,Brian Bullins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MuonIO为嵌入表和语言模型头提供统一的Muon式更新：分别基于1→2和2→∞算子范数，实现列/行归一化的同一规则，减少I/O优化器状态内存50%、更新FLOPs约46%，并提升验证困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Muon通过求解谱范数惩罚下损失局部线性化来导出隐藏线性层的更新规则，但输入（嵌入表）和输出（语言模型头）层被排除在外，仍沿用AdamW，缺乏统一的原理化处理。

Method: 对语言模型头L∈R^{V×d}，基于softmax输出几何的Lipschitz连续性采用2→∞算子范数；对嵌入表E∈R^{d×V}，基于one-hot输入几何采用1→2算子范数。利用恒等式‖L‖_{2→∞}=‖L^T‖_{1→2}，使两者处于同一词汇导向几何，从而应用单一的归一化向量规则：E为列归一化，L为行归一化。

Result: 在1B LLaMA的C4预训练中，相比Muon，MuonIO将I/O优化器状态内存减少50%，I/O更新FLOPs减少约46%，同时改善验证困惑度。

Conclusion: MuonIO统一了嵌入与输出层的更新规则，在降低内存与计算开销的同时提升模型性能，为Muon的完整原理化实现提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer derives its update rule for hidden linear layers by solving a local linearization of the loss penalized by the spectral norm, motivated by an RMS-stability argument for dense linear layers. Standard Muon implementations, however, exclude the input (embedding table) and output (language model head) layers from this principled treatment, for which they use AdamW instead. We present MuonIO, a single Muon-style update for both of these layers. For the language model head $\mathbf{L} \in \mathbb{R}^{V \times d}$, we motivate the use of the $2\to\infty$ operator norm, due to the Lipschitz continuity of the softmax output geometry, while for the embedding table $\mathbf{E} \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times V}$, we draw on the $1 \to 2$ operator norm, based on the one-hot input geometry identified by Bernstein & Newhouse (2025). The identity $\lVert\mathbf{L}\rVert_{2\to\infty}=\lVert\mathbf{L}^\top\rVert_{1\to2}$ then puts both matrices in the same vocabulary-oriented geometry: MuonIO applies a single normalized-vector rule, which appears as column normalization for $\mathbf{E}$ and row normalization for $\mathbf{L}$. Empirical evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, with MuonIO reducing I/O optimizer state memory by 50% and I/O update FLOPs by $\sim$46% compared to Muon for 1B LLaMA pretraining on C4, while also improving validation perplexity.

</details>


### [106] [Structural-Functional Brain Connectivity Generation via Multimodal Hypergraph-based Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02722)
*Chyong Yi Poh,Hwa Hui Tew,Junn Yong Loo,Raphaël C. -W. Phan,Fuad Noman,Pew-Thian Yap,Chee-Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MHG-FM框架，用超图与流匹配联合生成并互译SC-FC脑连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模配对SC-FC数据稀缺，而现有生成方法多用成对图、仅建模二元交互，且独立生成SC与FC，难以保持高阶结构-功能关系。

Method: 构建模态特定超图，用超图神经网络编码器学习高阶表征，通过双重交叉注意力进行双向跨模态融合；以变分自编码器将融合表征映射到紧凑隐空间，在隐空间用条件流匹配实现连接合成与多模态翻译（隐空间传输）。

Result: 在HCP-YA数据集上，重建质量、拓扑保持、分布相似性及SC-FC耦合均优于多个SOTA基线，采样速度约为同等扩散骨干的8倍。

Conclusion: 超图高阶建模结合隐空间流匹配可有效缓解SC-FC配对数据稀缺问题，实现高质量联合生成与跨模态翻译，并显著提升采样效率。

Abstract: Structural connectivity (SC) and functional connectivity (FC) provide complementary information on interactions between brain regions and are widely used in neuroimaging studies of neuropsychiatric disorders. Generative modelling can alleviate the scarcity of large-scale paired SC-FC data, but existing approaches typically use pairwise graphs that capture only dyadic interactions and often generate SC and FC independently, limiting preservation of higher-order structure-function relationships. We propose a Multimodal Hypergraph Flow Matching (MHG-FM) framework for joint SC-FC connectivity generation and cross-modal translation. MHG-FM constructs modality-specific hypergraphs, learns higher-order representations with Hypergraph Neural Network (HGNN) encoders, and performs bidirectional cross-modal fusion using Dual Cross-Attention (DCA). A variational autoencoder maps the fused representations to a compact latent space, where conditional flow matching enables connectivity synthesis and multimodal translation via latent transport. Experiments on the Human Connectome Project Young Adult (HCP-YA) dataset show that MHG-FM outperforms several state-of-the-art baselines in reconstruction quality, topology preservation, distributional similarity, and SC-FC coupling, while achieving approximately 8x faster sampling than a matched diffusion backbone.

</details>


### [107] [Bellman Error Minimization Via Linear Programming Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02730)
*Haining Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的函数逼近方法，将深度神经网络与线性规划近似算法结合，用于降低高维动态规划与强化学习中的贝尔曼误差，并以收益管理中的网络容量控制问题为动机示例，仿真显示其性能可与基准方法竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维动态规划与强化学习问题中贝尔曼误差较大，传统近似方法难以有效求解，需要新的函数逼近途径来提升近似精度与可行性。

Method: 提出一种新的函数逼近框架，将深度神经网络与线性规划近似算法相结合，并以经典动态规划问题——收益管理中的网络容量控制——作为动机示例进行建模与求解。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的近似算法在与基准方法对比时取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络与线性规划近似算法的结合可为动态规划问题提供有效近似解，并有助于降低贝尔曼误差；该思路在网络容量控制等场景中具备应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new functional approximation approach to reduce Bellman error in high-dimensional dynamic programming and Reinforcement Learning problems. Using a classic dynamic programming problem (network capacity control in revenue management) as the motivational example, the paper illustrates that deep neural networks and linear programming approximation algorithms can be combined to derive approximate solutions to dynamic programming problems. Simulation results show the proposed approximation algorithms achieves competitive performance when compared with benchmark.

</details>


### [108] [Inner Momentum for Differentially Private Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02738)
*Bishnu Bhusal,Minh Vu,Ben Southworth,Geigh Zollicoffer,Rohit Chadha,Manish Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DP-Muon-IM：通过将每个样本的Muon梯度在当前模型与近期模型历史上做平均后再裁剪，抑制差分隐私裁剪对奇异向量几何的扭曲。理论上将裁剪后批量矩阵分解为公共缩放与协方差残差R（||R||_F ≤ σ_λσ_G），并证明有限步Newton-Schulz迭代在此谱条件下保持极分解因子。在ε∈{1,2,4,8}的私有GPT-2微调中，E2E与DART上BLEU、ROUGE-L在所有同种子对比中均优于DP-Muon，非隐私诊断显示裁剪前极误差降低2–4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私训练对每个样本梯度做径向裁剪再加噪，但不同样本的裁剪因子不等，会扭曲样本梯度平均后的相对奇异向量几何。Muon的更新近似为极分解因子UV^T，仅依赖奇异向量，因此对裁剪导致的奇异向量偏移尤为敏感，性能易退化。作者旨在缓解这一裁剪诱导的几何失真。

Method: 在裁剪前，将每个采样样本的Muon梯度在当前模型与近期若干模型的短历史上做平均（IM），再执行每样本裁剪。由此裁剪后批量矩阵可分离为公共缩放部分与协方差残差R，R反映采样梯度与裁剪值之间的协方差，并用||R||_F ≤ σ_λσ_G对裁剪诱导失真给出直接界。进一步分析有限步Newton-Schulz正交化：在给定谱条件下保持输入的极分解因子，说明该校正能通过正交化步骤而不被破坏。

Result: 在E2E与DART数据集上以ε∈{1,2,4,8}做私有GPT-2微调，DP-Muon-IM在每一个同种子配对的比较中都提升了BLEU与ROUGE-L。非隐私诊断显示裁剪前极误差（pre-noise polar error）降低2–4%。

Conclusion: 在裁剪前对Muon梯度做模型历史平均，可有效控制差分隐私裁剪对奇异向量几何的失真，并能被有限步Newton-Schulz正交化保持；该方法在私有微调中一致优于DP-Muon。

Abstract: Differentially private training clips each per-example gradient before adding noise. This clipping is radial for each example, yet unequal clipping factors can distort the relative singular-vector geometry of their average. Muon is particularly exposed to this effect, since its update is an approximate polar factor UV^T that depends only on the singular vectors that clipping can shift. To curb this degradation, we propose averaging each sampled example's Muon gradient over the current model and a short history of recent models before clipping. The clipped batch matrix then separates into a common rescaling and a covariance residual R between sampled gradients and clipping values, with ||R||_F <= sigma_lambda sigma_G, bounding the clipping-induced distortion directly. We further show that a finite Newton-Schulz iteration preserves the polar factor of its input under these spectral conditions, confirming that our correction survives orthogonalization. In private GPT-2 fine-tuning on E2E and DART at epsilon in {1, 2, 4, 8}, DP-Muon-IM improves BLEU and ROUGE-L over DP-Muon in every seed-matched comparison, and non-private diagnostics show 2-4% lower pre-noise polar error.

</details>


### [109] [Prospective Hindsight: Self-Calibrating Reinforcement Learning via Prediction-Reality Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02740)
*Jiaxin Zhang,Xiangyu Peng,Qinglin Chen,Yu Li,Hiroaki Hayashi,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Prospective Hindsight (PH)训练原则，利用行动时前瞻预测与反馈后回顾评估之差（surprise）自校准地为梯度加权，在单轮与多轮任务上同时提升性能与校准度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野智能体仅依赖纯回顾式训练信号：信用分配只在观察到环境后果后进行，行动时刻的信念不可见于梯度，导致智能体自模型失准（miscalibration）且无法被优化直接修正。

Method: PH为任意回顾式基方法附加一个信号：每轮轨迹中，计算智能体前瞻预测（反馈前）与回顾评估（反馈后）之间的差距作为surprise；该surprise识别自模型最不准的样本，并通过stop-gradient的surprise加权优势放大其梯度贡献。前瞻预测器与策略共享参数，二者协同演化，逐步聚焦剩余盲点。理论上将其联系到特权信息差距，证明最小化surprise残差为智能体失准率提供下降路径，使校准成为优化副产物而非额外目标。

Result: 在单轮可验证任务及多轮个人智能体任务上，结合GRPO、on-policy蒸馏及其组合，PH同时提升任务性能与校准度，且在不同模型规模上增益一致。值得注意的是，主导失准模式在不同场景间发生结构性转变——单轮表现为过度自信的失败，多轮表现为信心不足的成功——而同一训练原则对两者均有效。

Conclusion: PH通过前瞻-回顾差距实现自校准训练，将校准内生于优化过程，无需附加目标即可跨任务类型与模型规模一致提升性能与校准，并能应对机制不同的失准模式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for long-horizon agents relies on purely retrospective training signals: credit is assigned only after observing environmental consequences, leaving the agent's belief at action time invisible to the gradient. We introduce Prospective Hindsight (PH), a self-calibrating training principle that augments any retrospective base method with a signal derived from the gap between the agent's prospective prediction (before feedback) and the retrospective evaluation (after feedback). This per-rollout surprise identifies samples where the agent's self-model is most inaccurate and amplifies their gradient contribution through a stop-gradient surprise-weighted advantage. Since the prospective predictor shares parameters with the policy, the two co-evolve, progressively shifting focus to the agent's remaining blind spots. We connect this principle to a privileged-information gap and show that minimizing the surprise residual provides a descent pathway on the agent's miscalibration rate; calibration thus emerges as a byproduct of optimization rather than from an added objective. On single-turn verifiable tasks and a multi-turn personal-agent task (under GRPO, on-policy distillation, and their combination), PH improves both task performance and calibration, with consistent gains across model scales. Notably, the dominant miscalibration mode shifts structurally between regimes, overconfident failures in single-turn, underconfident successes in multi-turn, yet the same training principle addresses both successfully.

</details>


### [110] [Jumping up and down: Denoiser diffusion models for discrete ordinal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02754)
*Yair Shenfeld,Ricardo Baptista,Stefano Peluchetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JUD——首个面向离散序数数据的去噪器型扩散模型家族，支持双向（升/降）扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续空间扩散模型在图像、视频领域已高度成熟，离散扩散模型在类别型数据（尤其语言）也取得重大进展，但面向离散整数值（序数）数据的扩散模型发展不足，而这类数据广泛存在于图像、音乐、基因计数等领域。

Method: JUD（Jumping Up and Down）以训练去噪器为核心，构建面向序数数据的扩散模型家族；其训练目标简洁，并允许对数据进行双向（向上与向下）扰动。

Result: 凭借简洁的训练目标与双向扰动的灵活性，JUD在多种数据模态上取得具有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: JUD填补了序数数据扩散建模的空白，验证了去噪器框架结合双向扰动在离散序数数据上的有效性，具备跨模态通用性。

Abstract: Diffusion models are highly developed in continuous spaces for image and video domains. Recently, major advances have been made for discrete diffusion models for categorical data, specifically in the language domain. In contrast, diffusion models for discrete integer-valued data are less developed, despite the prevalence of this modality, ranging from images and music to gene counts. We introduce Jumping Up and Down (JUD)---a new family of denoiser-based diffusion models for discrete ordinal data. This is the first family of diffusion models for ordinal data which centers around training denoisers, which at the same time allows for bi-directional (up and down) perturbations of the data. The simplicity of the training objective, combined with the flexibility of bi-directional perturbations, leads us to obtain competitive results across different data modalities.

</details>


### [111] [A Two-Stage Cascade for Near-Real-Time Forest Anomaly Detection from Sentinel-1 SAR Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02763)
*Pann Thinzar Seint,Subas Chhatkuli,Bryan Atwood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段"统计-编码器级联"方法，利用Sentinel-1时序数据实现热带森林损失的近实时异常检测，通过稳健统计z检验与卷积自编码器SSIM确认门双重把关，降低云层与季节性后向散射干扰带来的误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带森林监测对全球气候稳定与生物多样性保护至关重要，而非法采伐干预、供应链透明、土地利用治理和碳市场标准都亟需快速可靠的森林损失检测。现有遥感监测面临两大挑战：光学监测受云层覆盖限制，SAR系统则易将季节性湿度变化误判为森林损失。

Method: 采用两阶段统计-编码器级联架构：第一阶段在同配准的Sentinel-1 VH后向散射上，结合同季节历史基线进行自适应稳健统计z-score检验；第二阶段使用在稳定森林斑块上训练的卷积自编码器，基于潜空间结构相似性（SSIM）构建学习式确认门。仅当两阶段一致时，候选扰动才被确认为警报。系统还根据重复发生历史分配置信度分数和低/中/高风险等级。

Result: 系统输出每条警报可审计的置信度分数和以公顷计的面积估计，可直接兼容监测、报告与核查（MRV）工作流、可持续林业管理、现场核查及环境风险评估。模型具备灵活性，可扩展至选择性采伐、大规模农业侵占制图、洪水制图等环境应用。

Conclusion: 该两阶段级联方法有效克服了云层限制和季节性后向散射变化两大遥感难题，实现了高保真、近实时的热带森林损失检测，并提供可审计的置信度与面积估计，具备向多种环境监测任务推广的潜力。

Abstract: Tropical forest monitoring is essential for global climate stability and biodiversity preservation. To address the urgent need for rapid, reliable detection of forest loss which is essential for timely intervention against illegal logging, supply chain transparency, land-use governance and carbon market standards, we introduce a two-stage statistics-encoder cascade for near-real-time anomaly detection using Sentinel-1 time series. Our system is designed to overcome two fundamental challenges in remote sensing: the cloud-cover limitations that restrict optical monitoring and seasonal backscatter variation that causes SAR systems to mistake natural moisture changes for forest loss. The architecture integrates two distinct analytical engines to ensure high-fidelity detection: (1) an adaptive, robust-statistics z-score test on co-registered Sentinel-1 VH backscatter, same-season historical baseline and (2) a learned confirmation gate based on the latent-space structural similarity (SSIM) of a convolutional autoencoder trained on stable-forest patches. A candidate disturbance is confirmed as an alert only when both stages agree, and is assigned a confidence score and a Low/Medium/High risk tier from its repeat-occurrence history. The system produces per-alert auditable confidence scores and area-in-hectares estimates directly compatible with Monitoring, Reporting and Verification (MRV) workflows, sustainable forestry management, operational field checks and environmental risk assessments. Beyond its primary application, the model's flexibility allows for critical environmental applications ranging from selective logging to large-scale agricultural encroachment mapping, flood mapping and so on.

</details>


### [112] [A Controlled Audit of Personal AI Memory for Rating Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02764)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过"用户内历史评分置换"对照实验，检验结构化评分预测中个人AI究竟依赖历史物品-评分关联，还是主要依赖用户评分倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
