### [217] [Linear stability of electroconvection in a viscoelastic electrolyte: polymeric-work pathways and eigenmode selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02474)
*Minyoung Kim*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过线性稳定性、特征谱与机械功分析（辅以渐近路径排序），研究了粘弹性电解质中离子选择性表面附近的电对流不稳定性，揭示其随Weissenberg数Wi呈非单调依赖，极大增长率与极小临界电压出现在Wi~O(1)附近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电对流不稳定性在离子选择性界面处的电化学传输中起关键作用，牛顿流体中的该不稳定性已被广泛研究，但粘弹性对失稳机制的调控仍缺乏理解，因此需要厘清聚合物应力如何改变失稳阈值与增长率。

Method: 采用线性稳定性分析、特征谱分析与机械功（能量）平衡分析，并结合渐近路径排序。机械功平衡将贡献分解为电场功、溶剂粘性功与聚合物功，并依据本构来源进一步将聚合物功拆分为法向应力差路径与剪切应力路径。

Result: 增长率与临界电压对Wi呈非单调依赖，最优失稳在Wi~O(1)。Wi<<1时聚合物应力在首阶上退化为牛顿粘性耗散；Wi→1时牛顿型聚合物路径的相位效率显著降低，负聚合物功塌缩、不稳定性增强。中等Wi下低波数处出现临界电压的附加极小值，特征谱分析将其归因于一个缓慢传播且保持显著负聚合物功的模态选择。大Wi时该模态不再决定失稳阈值，低波数极小值消失，法向应力差功与剪切应力功近乎抵消，伴随更强的ESC局域剪切变形与以溶剂粘性为主的耗散。

Conclusion: 该非单调响应由应力—变形相位关系、溶剂耗散与特征模态选择共同支配，而非由应力幅值或单一聚合物贡献决定。

Abstract: Electroconvective (EC) instability near ion-selective surfaces plays a key role in electro-chemical transport beyond the diffusion-limited regime. While EC instability in Newtonian electrolytes has been extensively investigated, the influence of viscoelasticity on the instability mechanism remains less understood. In the present study, we investigate the viscoelastic modulation of EC instability using linear stability, eigenspectrum, and mechanical-work analyses, supported by asymptotic pathway ordering. The analysis reveals a non-monotonic dependence on the Weissenberg number Wi, with the maximum growth rate and minimum critical voltage occurring near Wi ~ O(1). The mechanical-work balance separates electric, solvent-viscous, and polymeric contributions, and further decomposes the polymeric work into normal-stress-difference and shear-stress pathways according to their constitutive origins. For Wi << 1, the polymer stress recovers Newtonian viscous dissipation at leading order. As Wi approaches unity, the phase efficiencies of the Newtonian-like polymeric pathways decrease substantially, causing the negative polymeric work to collapse and the instability to intensify. At intermediate Wi, an additional minimum in the critical voltage appears at low wavenumbers. Eigenspectrum analysis attributes this feature to the selection of a slowly propagating mode that retains substantial negative polymeric work. At large Wi, this mode no longer determines the instability threshold, and the low-wavenumber minimum disappears. The normal-stress-difference and shear-stress work then nearly cancel, accompanied by stronger ESC-localized shear deformation and predominantly solvent-viscous dissipation. Thus, the non-monotonic response is governed by stress-deformation phase relations, solvent dissipation, and eigenmode selection rather than stress magnitude or a single polymeric contribution.

</details>


### [218] [Radiometer effect on an infinitely thin circular disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02896)
*Takuma Tomita,Satoshi Taguchi,Tetsuro Tsuji*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文用线性化BGK模型与特征型数值格式求解稀薄气体中无限薄圆盘的辐射计效应，系统给出辐射计力、终端速度及流场/应力场随Knudsen数的变化规律，并用线性化Boltzmann方程的对称关系独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射计效应是圆盘两侧温差驱动的自热泳现象，其力主要来源于锐利边缘局部的应力集中；在近连续区准确解析边缘处速度分布函数的间断对捕捉该力至关重要，而现有研究缺乏系统的动理学参考解。

Method: 针对静止气体中自由平动至终端速度的无限薄圆盘，采用线性化BGK模型结合漫反射边界条件建模；用基于特征线的数值格式显式处理锐利边缘产生的速度分布函数间断，并施加无界域上的远场渐近条件。

Result: 在宽范围内获得辐射计力、终端速度及流动与应力场：无量纲辐射计力随Knudsen数单调增加，连续极限下按Kn^{3/2}标度，自由分子极限下趋于常数；终端速度呈非单调依赖，在中等Knudsen数处略超自由分子极限值；连续极限下法向应力差边缘局部变化的幅值似乎保持有限，其特征宽度为分子平均自由程量级。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了锐边缘动理学问题的参考数值解，揭示辐射计力与终端速度的标度及非单调特性，并表明边缘局域应力在连续极限下幅值有限、宽度趋于平均自由程尺度；计算结果通过线性化Boltzmann方程对称关系获得独立验证。

Abstract: The radiometer effect is a self-thermophoretic phenomenon in which a thin body with a temperature difference between its two sides experiences a force in a rarefied gas. We numerically investigate this effect for an infinitely thin circular disk freely translating at its terminal velocity in an otherwise quiescent gas. The problem is formulated using the linearized Bhatnagar--Gross--Krook model of the Boltzmann equation with the diffuse reflection boundary condition. A major difficulty arises from the sharp disk edge, which generates discontinuities in the velocity distribution function that propagate into the gas. Accurate resolution of these discontinuities is essential for capturing the edge-localized stress associated with the radiometric force in the near-continuum regime. The discontinuities are explicitly accounted for using a characteristic-based numerical scheme, together with the appropriate far-field asymptotics for the unbounded domain. The radiometric force, the terminal velocity, and the flow and stress fields are obtained over a wide range of Knudsen numbers. The dimensionless radiometric force increases monotonically with the Knudsen number, scaling as $\mathrm{Kn}^{3/2}$ in the continuum limit and approaching a constant in the free-molecular limit. The terminal velocity exhibits a nonmonotonic dependence on the Knudsen number and slightly exceeds its free-molecular-limit value at intermediate Knudsen numbers. In the continuum limit, the magnitude of the edge-localized variation of the normal-stress difference appears to remain finite, whereas its characteristic width is of the order of the molecular mean free path. The resulting numerical data also provide reference solutions for future studies of sharp-edge kinetic problems. Finally, the computed radiometric force is independently validated using the symmetric relation for the linearized Boltzmann equation.

</details>


### [219] [Development of a class of dynamic RANS closures for coarse-grained approaches to turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03067)
*Yves Tessier Urrecha,Giorgio Manzi,Filippo Cancellotti,Andrea Cimarelli*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该工作扩展了Cimarelli等人提出的基于时间滤波Navier-Stokes方程的动态RANS模型新类别，推导了基于k-ω模型的新公式，并通过尺度解析模拟验证其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cimarelli等人[1]提出的新型湍流封闭基于时间滤波Navier-Stokes方程框架，提供了从统计到尺度解析方法统一的粗粒化形式，并给出不同粗化层次湍流应力的精确方程，由此建立了基于动态计算涡黏系数的新型封闭。本文旨在将这一动态RANS模型类别进一步扩展。

Method: 在时间滤波Navier-Stokes方程理论框架下，将动态RANS模型扩展至k-ω模型，推导新的公式；同时给出一个替代版本，以优雅方式解决k和ω输运方程所再现的自由流湍流非物理衰减问题；并开展尺度解析模拟（scale-resolving simulation）活动进行分析。

Result: 尺度解析模拟结果揭示了有趣特征：新的动态k-ω模型对时间分辨率的粗化表现出强鲁棒性，并成功捕捉了复杂流动现象，包括层流到湍流的转捩，以及边界层分离与再附对边界处指定自由流湍流水平的敏感性。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了Cimarelli等人提出的新型湍流封闭的发展，验证了动态k-ω模型在时间分辨率粗化下的鲁棒性及其捕捉转捩、边界层分离与再附等复杂现象的能力。

Abstract: This work represents a crucial advancement in the development of a new class of turbulence closures conceived by Cimarelli et al. [1]. This new class of closures is based on the theoretical framework provided by the temporally filtered Navier-Stokes equations. This framework represents a unifying formalism for coarse-grained approaches to turbulence ranging from the statistical to the scale-resolving ones and provides exact equations relating turbulent stresses at different levels of coarsening. The latter have been used in Cimarelli et al. [1] as the theoretical foundation to derive a new class of closures based on the dynamic computation of the model coefficient appearing in the definition of eddy viscosity. Here, we extend this class of dynamic RANS models by deriving a new formulation based on the k-ω model including an alternative version where the non-physical decay of free-stream turbulence reproduced by the transport equations of k and ω is solved in an elegant way. A scale-resolving simulation campaign is performed and the analysis of the results reveals interesting features. Among others, the new dynamic k-ω model demonstrates strong robustness to a coarsening of the temporal resolution and successfully captures complex flow phenomena, including laminar-to-turbulent transition and the sensitivity of boundary-layer separation and reattachment to free-stream turbulence levels prescribed at the boundaries.

</details>


### [220] [Flow regime determines whether larval settlement hydrodynamics are governed by swimming speed or shape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03596)
*Daniel Gysbers,Mark A. Levenstein,Gabriel Juarez*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究通过水动力与幼体尺度耦合模拟，揭示了游泳速度和体形如何与近表层流动结构相互作用来控制幼虫在脊状基底上的附着。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幼虫扩散与附着决定固着海洋种群的补充和底栖群落组装，但幼虫性状与近表层流动结构如何相互作用以引导附着仍不清楚。

Method: 采用耦合的水动力与幼体尺度模拟，在振荡流条件下、脊状基底上，解析游泳速度与体形（从圆形到细长形）对附着的影响。近表层流动通过差异性调控向基底的输运、近基底滞留及与基底的遭遇，发挥性状筛选作用。

Result: 附着成功率在不同性状组合间差异可达一个数量级。关键性状取决于局部流动结构：在快速流限制进入附着面的区域，游泳速度最重要；在脊间形成回流区，依赖体形的排列取向控制输运与滞留。

Conclusion: 研究将幼虫性状与流动介导的附着机制相联系，为理解复杂底栖生境中补充量的变化提供了物理框架。

Abstract: Larval dispersal and settlement govern the replenishment of sessile marine populations and the assembly of benthic communities, yet how larval traits interact with near-surface flow structures to direct settlement remains unclear. We used coupled hydrodynamic and larval-scale simulations to resolve how swimming speed and body shape, from circular to elongated, influence settlement in oscillatory flow over ridged substrates. Settlement success differed by up to an order of magnitude across trait combinations. Near-surface flow acted as a trait filter by differentially regulating transport to, retention near, and encounters with the substrate. The trait that mattered most depended on the local flow structure. Where rapid flow restricted access to settlement surfaces, swimming speed was most important. Where flow recirculated between substrate ridges, shape-dependent alignment controlled transport and retention. These results link larval traits to flow-mediated settlement mechanisms and provide a physical framework for understanding how recruitment varies across complex benthic habitats.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [On deformations of foliated complex analytic structures II: Functors of Artin rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02277)
*Chunghoon Kim*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在Artin环函子框架下研究由切丛与余切丛的局部自由子层定义的叶状非奇异簇的形变，识别一阶形变与阻碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将叶状结构的形变理论纳入Artin环函子的标准形变框架，以刻画叶状非奇异簇在无穷小形变下叶状结构如何随底空间一起变化。

Method: 使用Artin环函子语言，把叶状结构分别表示为切层的局部自由子层（切向分布）与余切层的局部自由子层，构造相应形变函子，并计算其在一阶（切空间）与二阶（阻碍空间）的取值。

Result: 给出叶状非奇异簇形变函子的一阶形变空间与阻碍空间的识别结果，即用相应子层的上同调群描述无穷小形变与阻碍。

Conclusion: 叶状非奇异簇的形变理论可由Artin环函子统一处理，其一阶形变与阻碍可通过切层、余切层子层的上同调显式刻画。

Abstract: We study deformations of foliated nonsingular varieties defined by locally free subsheaves of tangent sheaves and locally free subshseaves of cotangent sheaves in the language of functors of Artin rings. We identify first-order deformations and obstructions.

</details>


### [222] [On wall-crossing invariant combinations of Welschinger numbers for rational surfaces with a conic bundle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02445)
*Sergey Finashin,Viatcheslav Kharlamov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文将此前在 del Pezzo 曲面上提出的、对实结构变化不敏感的实枚举不变量加以精细化，并将其推广到任意有理曲面；构造依赖于辅助 conic bundle 与 Pin-结构的选取，证明了不变量与这些辅助选择无关，并给出递归计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有实枚举不变量仅定义于 del Pezzo 曲面，适用范围受限；需要一种对实结构变化不敏感且能推广到更一般曲面（任意有理曲面）的实枚举不变量体系。

Method: 在已有实枚举不变量基础上进行精细化；构造过程中引入辅助 conic bundle 及 Pin-结构；证明所得不变量不依赖于辅助 conic bundle 与 Pin-结构的选取；建立用于计算的递归关系。

Result: 成功将精细化后的实枚举不变量从 del Pezzo 曲面推广至任意有理曲面；证明了不变量对辅助选择（conic bundle、Pin-结构）的独立性；得到了不变量满足的递归关系。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了实枚举不变量的定义范围与鲁棒性，为任意有理曲面上实枚举不变量的系统计算提供了理论框架与递归工具。

Abstract: We refine the real enumerative invariants insensitive to changes in the real structure introduced in our previous paper on del Pezzo surfaces, and extend these refined invariants to arbitrary rational surfaces. The construction involves a choice of an auxiliary conic bundle together with a Pin-structure. We prove that the resulting invariants are independent of these auxiliary choices and establish recursive relations for their computation.

</details>


### [223] [The Mumford--Tate group of a very general Lagrangian fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02481)
*Edward Varvak*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文分类了具有全纯 Lagrangian 纤维化的本原辛簇上极一般纤维的可能 Mumford–Tate 群，并得出判别式余维数为 1 等结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究本原辛簇（及超 Kähler 流形）上的 Lagrangian 纤维化，刻画其极一般纤维的 Hodge 结构对称性，并解决 Li–Tosatti 的猜想。

Method: 通过分类极一般纤维的可能 Mumford–Tate 群，结合 Hodge 理论与辛几何分析，应用于判别式、Hodge-generic 性及特殊 Kähler 度量的曲率性质。

Result: 证明判别式在 B 中余维数为 1；当 X 为一般超 Kähler 流形且 b_2(X)≥7 时极一般纤维是 Hodge-generic；并验证了 Li–Tosatti 期望：非等平凡情形下特殊 Kähler 度量在正则值上具有正全纯截面曲率。

Conclusion: 该分类统一给出了 Lagrangian 纤维化的多项结构性结论，并确认了 Li–Tosatti 猜想。

Abstract: For a primitive symplectic variety $X$ admitting a holomorphic Lagrangian fibration $f: X \to B$, we classify the possible Mumford--Tate groups of the very general fiber. We extract as a consequence that the discriminant of $f$ has codimension one in $B$. In the special case when $X$ is a hyper-Kähler manifold which is general among those admitting a Lagrangian fibration, and $b_2(X) \geq 7$, we further show that the very general fiber of $f$ is Hodge-generic. Finally, as an application of our classification, we confirm an expectation of Li--Tosatti that when $f$ is not isotrivial, the special Kähler metric on $B$ has positive holomorphic sectional curvature on the regular values of $f$.

</details>


### [224] [Regularization and algebraic stability of birational automorphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02536)
*Alexandra Kuznetsova*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文比较代数簇上双有理自同构的五类自然集合：射影可正则化、可正则化、射影伪可正则化、伪可正则化，以及容许代数稳定模型的映射，确定它们之间的一般包含关系，并证明对 $\mathbb{P}^k$（$k\geqslant 3$）所有非平凡包含均为严格包含。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双有理自同构的动力学（尤其第一动力学度）需要在合适的几何框架中研究，而不同正则化/稳定化概念提供了不同框架，因此有必要厘清这些类之间的包含关系及其严格性。

Method: 对五类双有理自同构（射影可正则化、可正则化、射影伪可正则化、伪可正则化、容许代数稳定模型）进行一般性比较，确定包含关系；并通过构造显式例子证明 $\mathbb{P}^k$（$k\geqslant 3$）上所有非平凡包含的严格性。

Result: 确定了五类之间的所有一般包含关系；对 $\mathbb{P}^k$ 且 $k\geqslant 3$，证明所有非平凡包含均为严格包含，并由显式例子给出验证。

Conclusion: 这些正则化与稳定化类给出了研究双有理动力学及第一动力学度的层次化几何框架，且在射影空间高维情形下各层次确实互不相同。

Abstract: We compare five natural classes of birational automorphisms of an algebraic variety: projectively regularizable, regularizable, projectively pseudo-regularizable, pseudo-regularizable, and those admitting an algebraically stable model. These classes provide different geometric frameworks in which the dynamics of a birational automorphism, and in particular its first dynamical degree, can be studied. We determine the general inclusion relations among them and show that, for $\mathbb{P}^k$ with $k\geqslant 3$, all the resulting non-trivial inclusions are strict; we prove this by giving explicit examples.

</details>


### [225] [Sharp FRS and singularity thresholds for off-diagonal Gram maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02585)
*Ying Xie*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文确定了h向量非对角Gram映射的FRS（有理奇点约化纤维）平坦性阈值，并计算了零纤维理想I_{M,h}的对数典范阈值lct。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Gram映射的奇点性质：判断其约化纤维是否具有有理奇点（FRS），并刻画其平坦性条件，这对于理解相关模空间的几何性质具有重要意义。

Method: 利用有限局部主理想环上空心对称矩阵的精确加权估计，结合各向同性下界，通过点计数方法处理有理奇点问题。

Result: 证明Gram映射平坦且具有FRS约化纤维当且仅当M > max_{2<=r<=h}(2h - r + 1 - 2h/r)；给出零纤维理想I_{M,h}的lct显式公式；在严格范围内，截断系数映射具有几何整的完全交纤维，并在Z[1/2]上忠实平坦；同时给出奇数剩余特征局部域上Gram映射密度的连续模。

Conclusion: 该工作精确刻画了非对角Gram映射的FRS卷积阈值与lct，推广了点计数方法并给出密度连续性模，对有理奇点理论有贡献。

Abstract: The off-diagonal Gram map for h vectors in an M-dimensional quadratic space is flat with reduced fibers of rational singularities (FRS) in characteristic zero if and only if M > max_{2 <= r <= h} (2h - r + 1 - 2h/r). Equivalently, this gives the exact FRS convolution threshold of x |-> (x_i x_j){i < j}. For its zero-fiber ideal I{M,h}, we also prove
  lct(I_{M,h}) = min{ binom(h,2), min_{0 <= k <= h-2} ( binom(k+1,2) + M(h-k)/2 ) }.
  In the strict range, all truncated coefficient maps have geometrically integral complete-intersection fibers and are faithfully flat over Z[1/2], uniformly over graphs on h vertices. The proof uses a sharp weighted estimate for hollow symmetric matrices over finite local principal ideal rings, paired with isotropic lower bounds. Within the established point-counting approach to rational singularities, this estimate also gives an explicit modulus of continuity for Gram-map densities over every local field of odd residue characteristic.

</details>


### [226] [Pappas's conjecture on ramified unitary local models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02602)
*Yu Luo*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明 ramified unitary group 的 wedge local model 在 p ≥ 2s−1 时是 flat 的，验证了 Pappas 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pappas 猜想预言 ramified unitary group 的 wedge local model 的平坦性，此前的证明尚不完整，本文旨在 signature (r,s)（r>s）且 p≥2s−1 的情形下给出证明。

Method: 第一步将问题归约为确定自伴矩阵某个幂零轨道闭包的定义方程；第二步计算相应 Kempf resolution 的整体截面；计算中借助 Koszul complex 将其归约到 type A 情形。

Result: 在 p ≥ 2s−1（r>s）时证明了 wedge local model 是 flat 的，从而在此情形下证实 Pappas 猜想。

Conclusion: wedge local model 的平坦性归结为幂零轨道闭包定义方程与 Kempf resolution 整体截面的计算，并可通过 Koszul complex 归约到 type A。

Abstract: We prove that the wedge local model for a ramified unitary group of signature $(r,s)$ ($r>s$) at self-dual level is flat when $p\geq 2s-1$, confirming Pappas's conjecture in this case. The proof first reduces the problem to determining the defining equations of a nilpotent orbit closure of self-adjoint matrices, and then to computing the global sections of the corresponding Kempf resolution. The computation is carried out by reducing to the type $A$ case using the Koszul complex.

</details>


### [227] [Sharp Noether-type inequalities for $3$-folds of intermediate Kodaira dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02689)
*Meng Chen,Yong Hu,Chen Jiang,Yang Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对中等Kodaira维数的光滑射影三维簇建立Noether型不等式，将Iitaka体积Ivol(V)与几何亏格p_g(V)联系起来，并刻画等号成立情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望推广经典的Noether不等式到中等Kodaira维数（1、2维）的光滑射影三维簇，寻找Iitaka体积与几何亏格之间的定量关系，并理解取等时的几何结构。

Method: 通过双有理几何（极小模型纲领）方法，分析Kodaira维数为1和2的光滑射影三维簇的Iitaka纤维化与典范/双典范系统，证明Ivol(V)与p_g(V)的不等式，并刻画等号条件。

Result: 当Kodaira维数为2时，Ivol(V) ≥ (3/4)p_g(V) - 5/4；当Kodaira维数为1时，Ivol(V) ≥ p_g(V) - 1。两个不等式均是最优（sharp）的。在Kodaira维数2情形，若p_g(V) ≥ 4，等号成立当且仅当V的双典范系统诱导Iitaka纤维化，且V的典范系统复合于一个曲面束（其一般纤维的Iitaka体积为1/2）。

Conclusion: 论文给出了中等Kodaira维数三维簇的Noether型不等式，Iitaka体积受几何亏格下界控制，并精确刻画了Kodaira维数2时的取等几何结构。

Abstract: For a smooth projective $3$-fold $V$ of intermediate Kodaira dimension, we establish Noether-type inequalities relating the Iitaka volume $\operatorname{Ivol}(V)$ and the geometric genus $p_g(V)$. Specifically, for a smooth projective $3$-fold $V$ of Kodaira dimension $2$, we show that $\operatorname{Ivol}(V)\geq \frac{3}{4}p_g(V)-\frac{5}{4};$ while if the Kodaira dimension of $V$ is $1$, then $\operatorname{Ivol}(V)\geq p_g(V)-1.$ Both inequalities are sharp. In the Kodaira dimension $2$ case, we prove that the equality holds only if the bicanonical system of $V$ induces the Iitaka fibration and the canonical system of $V$ is composed with a pencil of surfaces whose general member has Iitaka volume $\frac12$ provided that $p_g(V)\geq 4$.

</details>


### [228] [Grothendieck ring of varieties and at-the-characteristic numerical invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02690)
*Toni Annala,Matthew Satriano,Evan Sundbo*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在正特征下假设弱形式的奇点消解，证明Hodge数等在特征数值不变量由Grothendieck环的同态给出，并得到两个推论与一个附录结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正特征下数值不变量的函子性，回答de Fernex--Mere关于$\widehat K$-等价簇的Hodge数问题，以及Nicaise--Sebag关于普遍同胚簇在Grothendieck环中类的问题。

Method: 假设正特征下弱形式的奇点消解，构造从Grothendieck簇环出发的同态来刻画Hodge数等at-the-characteristic数值不变量。

Result: (i) $\widehat K$-等价簇具有相同Hodge数；(ii) 普遍同胚簇在Grothendieck环中未必有相同类；附录中无条件证明维数不超过四的双有理Calabi--Yau簇具有相同Hodge数。

Conclusion: 在弱奇点消解假设下建立了Hodge数等不变量的Grothendieck环同态描述，并解决两个公开问题；附录给出低维双有理Calabi--Yau情形的无条件证据。

Abstract: Assuming a weak form of resolution of singularities in positive characteristic, we show several at-the-characteristic numerical invariants, including Hodge numbers, are given by homomorphisms out of the Grothendieck ring of varieties. Under the same assumption, we obtain two consequences: (i) $\widehat K$-equivalent varieties have the same Hodge numbers, answering a question of de Fernex--Mere; and (ii) universally homeomorphic varieties need not have the same class in the Grothendieck ring of varieties, answering a question of Nicaise--Sebag. In the appendix, we provide unconditional evidence for (i) by proving that birational Calabi--Yau varieties of dimension at most four have the same Hodge numbers.

</details>


### [229] [Arakelov inequalities for fibered surfaces in positive characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02760)
*Hao Max Sun,Wan-Yuan Xu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在正特征代数闭域上，对亏格 g≥2 的相对极小半稳定纤维化，若全曲面为 Hodge Witt 且奇异纤维数 s≥2，则证明经典 Arakelov 不等式成立；对底为 P^1 得到更强估计，并构造特征 5 中亏格 2、恰有 4 条奇异纤维的例子说明下界与不等式均可达，且给出应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正特征情形下，经典的 Arakelov 不等式是否成立是一个自然问题，尤其当底曲线为 P^1 时，奇异纤维数目下界与不等式的最优性此前并不清楚。本文旨在研究 Hodge Witt 曲面上半稳定纤维化的 Arakelov 型不等式，并验证相关下界与不等式的尖锐性。

Method: 设 f:S→C 为亏格 g≥2 的相对极小半稳定纤维化，一般纤维光滑且几何连通，Σ 为奇异纤维对应点集，s=|Σ|。在 S 为 Hodge Witt 且 s≥2 的条件下，利用相对对偶层 ω_{S/C} 与 Ω_C^1(logΣ) 的次数关系推导 Arakelov 不等式；对 C=P^1 的情形进一步结合第一 Betti 数 b_1(S) 进行精细估计。同时构造特征 5 中底为 P^1、亏格 2 且恰有 4 条奇异纤维的半稳定纤维化作为例子。

Result: 证明当 S 为 Hodge Witt 且 s≥2 时，deg f_*ω_{S/C} ≤ (g/2) deg Ω_C^1(logΣ)。当 C=P^1 时得到更强估计 deg f_*ω_{S/P^1} ≤ (g/2)(s-2) − (1/2)b_1(S)。构造出特征 5 中亏格 2、底为 P^1、恰有 4 条奇异纤维的半稳定纤维化，其全曲面为 Hodge Witt 且 deg f_*ω_{S/P^1}=2=(g/2)(s-2)，从而说明 Nguyen 的下界 s≥4 与 Hodge–Witt Arakelov 不等式均是尖锐的。作为应用，还推导出典范类不等式及系数关于 g 线性的 Szpiro 型不等式。

Conclusion: 在 Hodge Witt 条件下，正特征半稳定纤维化的 Arakelov 不等式成立，且对 P^1 底可加强；所构造例子表明相关下界与不等式不可改进。由此可进一步得到典范类不等式和 Szpiro 型不等式等应用。

Abstract: Let $f:S\to C$ be a relatively minimal semistable fibration of genus $g\ge2$ over an algebraically closed field of characteristic $p>0$ with smooth and geometrically connected generic fiber. Let $Σ\subset C$ denote the set of points over which the fibers are singular, with $s=|Σ|$. If the total surface $S$ is Hodge Witt and $s\ge2$, we prove the classical Arakelov inequality $$
  \text{deg}\, f_*ω_{S/C}\le \frac g2 \text{deg}\, Ω_C^1(\logΣ), $$ where $ω_{S/C}$ denotes the relative dualizing sheaf. For $C=\mathbb P^1$, we obtain the stronger estimate $$
  \text{deg}\, f_*ω_{S/\mathbb P^1}\le \frac g2(s-2)-\frac12 b_1(S), $$ where $b_1(S)$ is the first Betti number of $S$. We also construct a genus-$2$ semistable fibration over $\mathbb P^1$ with exactly $4$ singular fibers in characteristic $5$. Its total surface is Hodge Witt, and $$
  \text{deg}\, f_*ω_{S/\mathbb P^1}=2=\frac g2(s-2). $$ Thus Nguyen's lower bound $s\ge4$ and the Hodge--Witt Arakelov inequality are both sharp. As applications, we derive a canonical-class inequality and a Szpiro-type inequality with coefficients linear in $g$.

</details>


### [230] [Categorical Dynamics of Abelian Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02807)
*Yu-Wei Fan*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在任意阿贝尔簇的导出范畴上建立了若干范畴动力学的一般性结果：证明了指数与多项式 Gromov–Yomdin 等式、给出了范畴多项式熵的上界 $g^2$、用辛平移数的拉回刻画移位数，并通过移位数集合的有限性刻画了 nef 锥的有理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典阿贝尔簇的动力学/体积增长理论提升到导出范畴的层面，统一并推广已有结果（椭圆曲线的指数等式、阿贝尔曲面与单阿贝尔簇的相应结论、Lin–Oguiso–Zhang 的多项式对数体积增长），并回应 Dimitrov–Haiden–Katzarkov–Kontsevich 关于范畴熵函数代数性的问题。

Method: 研究任意阿贝尔簇导出范畴的自等价（autoequivalence）的范畴动力学：建立自等价下的指数与多项式 Gromov–Yomdin 等式；通过范畴熵与 $g^2$ 上界估计；将移位数与 $\mathrm{Sp}(4g,\mathbb{R})$ 万有覆叠上归一化 Barge–Ghys 辛平移数的拉回相对应；并用 nef 锥的有理性来分析约化移位数集合的有限性。

Result: (1) 对任意阿贝尔簇导出范畴的每个自等价证明了指数与多项式 Gromov–Yomdin 等式；(2) 证明范畴多项式熵上界为 $g^2$，并以此直接恢复 Lin–Oguiso–Zhang 的上界；(3) 将任意自等价的移位数等同于 $\mathrm{Sp}(4g,\mathbb{R})$ 万有覆叠上归一化 Barge–Ghys 辛平移数的拉回；(4) 证明约化移位数集合有限当且仅当 nef 锥为有理多面体；当该集合无限时，存在具有超越移位数的自等价。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了阿贝尔簇导出范畴的范畴动力学，给出了范畴熵的维数上界与移位数的辛解释，并通过 nef 锥的有理性完全刻画了约化移位数的有限性；特别地，对 Dimitrov–Haiden–Katzarkov–Kontsevich 关于范畴熵函数代数性的问题给出了否定回答。

Abstract: We establish several general results on the categorical dynamics of abelian varieties.
  (1) We prove the exponential and polynomial Gromov--Yomdin equalities for every autoequivalence of the derived category of an arbitrary abelian variety. The exponential equality extends earlier results of Kikuta for elliptic curves and of Yoshioka for abelian surfaces and simple abelian varieties.
  (2) We prove the categorical polynomial entropy of every autoequivalence is bounded above by $g^2$, where $g$ is the dimension of the abelian variety. This gives a categorical generalization of the polynomial log-volume growth of Lin--Oguiso--Zhang and recovers their upper bound as a direct consequence.
  (3) We identify the shifting number of every autoequivalence of an abelian variety with the pullback of the normalized Barge--Ghys symplectic translation number on the universal cover of $\text{Sp}(4g,\mathbb{R})$.
  (4) We prove that the set of reduced shifting numbers is finite if and only if the nef cone is rational polyhedral. Moreover, when this set is infinite, there exists an autoequivalence with transcendental shifting number. In particular, this gives a negative answer to a question of Dimitrov--Haiden--Katzarkov--Kontsevich concerning the algebraicity of categorical entropy functions.

</details>


### [231] [Extension of solvable coverings across horizontal divisors over the integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02948)
*Naganori Yamaguchi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究从算术概形中删除水平除子对étale基本群的影响，证明在特定假设下诱导满射的核是拓扑完美的，特别地在极大prosolvable商上给出同构，并应用于射影超平面补与完全局部系数环上的表示延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解删除水平除子如何改变算术概形的étale基本群，并刻画由此产生的核的结构，进而推广经典的超平面补与表示延拓结论。

Method: 设概形沿该除子是正则的、每个素分支含有剩余特征为2的normal crossings点，且边界分支的一般纤维在有限étale基变换后保持几何整。在此假设下分析删除水平除子诱导的基本群满射，证明其核是拓扑完美的。

Result: 诱导满射的核是拓扑完美的；特别地，包含映射在极大prosolvable商上诱导同构。该结果应用于射影超平面补以及完全局部系数环上的表示延拓。

Conclusion: 删除满足所述正则性与边界条件的水平除子不会改变算术概形的极大prosolvable基本群，为超平面补与表示延拓问题提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We study the effect of deleting a horizontal divisor from an arithmetic scheme on its étale fundamental group. Assume that the scheme is regular along the divisor, that each prime component contains a normal crossings point of residue characteristic $2$, and that the generic fibers of the boundary components remain geometrically integral after finite étale base change. We prove that the kernel of the induced surjection on fundamental groups is topologically perfect. In particular, the inclusion induces an isomorphism on maximal prosolvable quotients. We apply this result to projective hyperplane complements and to the extension of representations over complete local coefficient rings.

</details>


### [232] [Canonical extensions of ruled surfaces over elliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03048)
*Francesco Antonio Denisi,Andreas Höring*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在椭圆曲线负次数线丛上的直纹面上，切丛的对称张量环不是有限生成的；并在其典范扩张上构造全纯函数，证明该扩张空间不是Stein的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathbb P(\mathcal O_E \oplus L)$ 是代数几何中最简单的曲面之一，但其切丛结构出乎意料地复杂。作者旨在通过研究切丛的对称张量环与典范扩张的Stein性质，揭示这种复杂性。

Method: 设 $L$ 为椭圆曲线 $E$ 上的负次数线丛，$M = \mathbb P(\mathcal O_E \oplus L)$。先研究 $T_M$ 的对称张量环；随后在 $M$ 的典范扩张 $Z_M$ 上构造一个全纯函数。

Result: 证明了 $T_M$ 的对称张量环不是有限生成的；并借助所构造的全纯函数，证明典范扩张 $Z_M$ 不是 Stein 的。

Conclusion: 即便在最简单的高维几何对象上，其切丛也可能表现出非有限生成等复杂性质，并且其典范扩张未必具有Stein性。

Abstract: Let $L$ be a line bundle of negative degree on an elliptic curve $E$. While the ruled surface $M := \mathbb P(\mathcal O_E \oplus L)$ is one of the simplest varieties in algebraic geometry, its tangent bundle $T_M$ is surprisingly complex: we first show that the ring of symmetric tensors is not finitely generated. We then construct a holomorphic function on the canonical extension $Z_M$ which allows us to show that $Z_M$ is not Stein.

</details>


### [233] [Multiplicativity of the perverse filtration in the $\mathrm{SL}_n$ case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03096)
*Xueqing Wen*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了互素情形下典范 SL_n Hitchin 系统的反常层滤过具有乘性，并给出椭圆轨迹上的比较与推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hitchin 系统的反常层滤过（perverse filtration）及其代数结构是几何表示论与 Langlands 纲领的核心问题，其乘性（multiplicativity）反映了滤过与 Hitchin 纤维化/整体几何的相容性。已有结果多在特定轨迹或特定秩下成立，作者希望在秩与次数互素的完整情形、以及典范模空间中建立此性质。

Method: 1) 设 rank 与 degree 互素，直接证明典范 SL_n Hitchin 系统的反常层滤过是乘性的；2) 在偶数度扭曲 Hitchin 系统的椭圆轨迹（elliptic locus）上，将紧化 Prym 的 Fourier 投影算子与内窥对应（endoscopic correspondence）进行比较；3) 利用变体上同调（variant cohomology）的平方零性质，结合抛物 Higgs 模空间的约化（parabolic Higgs moduli reduction），从椭圆轨迹过渡到完整典范模空间。

Result: 在 rank 与 degree 互素时，典范 SL_n Hitchin 系统的反常层滤过被证明是乘性的；在偶数度扭曲 Hitchin 系统的椭圆轨迹上，紧化 Prym 的 Fourier 投影算子与内窥对应得到比较；这些结果通过平方零性质与抛物约化被提升至完整典范模空间。

Conclusion: 反常层滤过的乘性对互素典范 SL_n Hitchin 系统成立，且椭圆轨迹上的局部比较可借助平方零与抛物 Higgs 约化推广到整体模空间，为 Hitchin 系统的滤过结构与内窥/几何 Langlands 的联系提供了新证据。

Abstract: We prove that the perverse filtration of the canonical $\mathrm{SL}_n$ Hitchin system is multiplicative when the rank and degree are coprime. Over the elliptic locus of an even-degree twisted Hitchin system, we compare the Fourier projectors for compactified Pryms with the endoscopic correspondence. We then use the square-zero property of the variant cohomology and a parabolic Higgs moduli reduction to pass to the full canonical moduli space.

</details>


### [234] [Ample stability implies rigidity for quiver moduli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03302)
*Pieter Belmans,Gianni Petrella*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明ample stability足以对quiver模空间上universal representation的自同态丛施加Teleman量子化，并由此推出其无穷小刚性，回答了此前合作工作中遗留的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前与Brecan、Franzen、Reineke合作研究universal bundles的层上同调时留下未解问题：能否将Teleman量子化应用于quiver模空间上universal representation的自同态丛并得出无穷小刚性。本文旨在给出肯定回答，并将适用条件推广至最广范围。

Method: 在quiver模空间上，对universal representation的自同态丛施加Teleman量子化，所需条件仅为ample stability。

Result: 证明ample stability即足以实现上述Teleman量子化，并由此推出该自同态丛的无穷小刚性；同时指出对于互素维数向量，模空间的Picard秩达到最大。

Conclusion: ample stability是充分条件，使若干近期结果可在最广范围内适用；对互素维数向量可保证模空间Picard秩最大。

Abstract: We show that ample stability suffices to apply Teleman quantisation to the endomorphism bundle of the universal representation on a quiver moduli space, and thus in particular to deduce its infinitesimal rigidity. This answers a question left open in our earlier work with Brecan, Franzen, and Reineke on the sheaf cohomology of universal bundles. This sufficiency makes several recent results applicable in the widest possible range, subject only to ample stability, which for coprime dimension vectors guarantees that the Picard rank of the moduli space is maximal.

</details>


### [235] [Betti Numbers of Odd Biisotropic Symplectic Grassmannians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03309)
*Anton Fonarev,Alexander Kuznetsov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 计算奇数型双迷向辛Grassmannian S_2Gr(k,2n+1)的奇异上同调群，通过构造BB胞腔与Gr(k,2n+2-k)中Schubert胞腔之间保维数的组合双射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辛Grassmannian及其变体的上同调结构是代数几何与组合学的核心问题；奇数型双迷向辛Grassmannian的胞腔结构此前缺乏显式刻画，需给出可计算的上同调描述。

Method: 利用Bialynicki-Birula分解得到S_2Gr(k,2n+1)的BB胞腔，并构造其与Grassmannian Gr(k,2n+2-k)的Schubert胞腔之间的显式保维数组合双射。

Result: 建立了BB胞腔与Schubert胞腔的一一对应（保维数），据此通过胞腔链复形计算出S_2Gr(k,2n+1)的奇异上同调群。

Conclusion: 奇数型双迷向辛Grassmannian的上同调可由普通Grassmannian的组合数据刻画，为相关Schubert演算与枚举问题提供组合基础。

Abstract: We compute the singular cohomology groups of odd biisotropic symplectic Grassmannians $\operatorname{S_2Gr}(k, 2n+1)$ by presenting an explicit dimension-preserving combinatorial bijection between the Bialynicki-Birula cells in $\operatorname{S_2Gr}(k, 2n+1)$ and the Schubert cells in $\operatorname{Gr}(k, 2n+2-k)$.

</details>


### [236] [K-stability of a class of smooth Fano complete intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03311)
*Yijue Hu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文提出通过估计光滑曲线上可约零维子格式处的局部稳定性阈值来研究Fano簇K-稳定性，并证明维数大于1的光滑index-one Fano超曲面（位于二次超曲面中）均为K-稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-稳定性是Fano簇存在Kähler-Einstein度量的代数判据，对一般Fano超曲面等情形验证困难，需要发展降维与局部估计技术。

Method: 沿光滑曲线上的可约零维子格式估计局部稳定性阈值；应用Abban-Zhuang方法构造合适容许旗，将高维稳定性问题归约到低维，并使Abban-Zhuang估计的最后一项化为光滑曲线上多点处的局部稳定性阈值，同时给出该估计的凸几何解释。

Result: 证明了维数大于1时，二次超曲面中的全部光滑index-one Fano超曲面都是K-稳定的。

Conclusion: 该局部稳定性阈值估计与Abban-Zhuang归约相结合，为Fano簇K-稳定性提供了有效工具，并给出二次超曲面中index-one Fano超曲面的K-稳定性判定。

Abstract: We develop an approach to study K-stability of Fano varieties by estimating the local stability threshold along a reducible zero-dimensional subscheme on a smooth curve. As an application, we we show that all smooth index-one Fano hypersurfaces in quadrics are K-stable, when the dimension is higher than one. To prove it, we apply the Abban-Zhuang method to reduce the stability problem to lower dimensions. Specifically, we construct suitable admissible flags such that the last term of the Abban-Zhuang estimate is a local stability threshold along multiple points on a smooth curve, and give a convex geometric interpretation of the estimate.

</details>


### [237] [Mond's conjecture for corank-one maps from $\mathbb C^3$ to $\mathbb C^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03331)
*Richard Rimanyi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了余秩一映射芽的 Mond 猜想：$\mathcal A_e$-余维数不超过像 Milnor 数，拟齐次情形成立等式；给出了依赖于权与次数的 Hilbert 级数闭公式，并归约到 nice dimensions 中关于一般齐次芽的不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mond 猜想断言映射芽的 $\mathcal A_e$-余维数不超过其像 Milnor 数，是奇点理论中的重要问题。对余秩一、来源维数为 3 的映射芽，需要验证该猜想并在拟齐次情形给出等号刻画。

Method: 研究 $\mathcal A$-有限余秩一映射芽 $(\mathbb C^3,0)\to(\mathbb C^4,0)$；对拟齐次芽，用双点曲面、cross-cap 曲线及其正规化刻画像模去导子域（conductor fields）的导子；再对一般芽，证明互素次数的一般齐次余秩一芽在任意源维数下 $\mathcal A$-有限，并应用 Fernández de Bobadilla、Nuño-Ballesteros 与 Peñafort Sanchis 的归约定理。

Result: 对 $\mathcal A$-有限余秩一映射芽 $(\mathbb C^3,0)\to(\mathbb C^4,0)$ 证明 Mond 猜想；拟齐次芽情形成立等式。得到仅依赖权与次数的分次 $\mathcal A_e$-正规空间 Hilbert 级数闭公式，其在 $t=1$ 的值与 Ohmoto 由 Segre–Schwartz–MacPherson Thom 多项式给出的像 Milnor 数公式一致。此外证明互素次数的一般齐次余秩一芽在任意源维数下 $\mathcal A$-有限，并在每对 nice dimensions 中把 Mond 猜想（余秩一情形）归约为一般齐次芽的不等式。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了余秩一、来源维数 3 情形下的 Mond 猜想，并通过归约定理将一般余秩一情形的 Mond 猜想简化为 nice dimensions 中关于一般齐次芽的不等式。

Abstract: We prove Mond's conjecture for $\mathcal A$-finite corank-one map germs $(\mathbb C^3,0)\to(\mathbb C^4,0)$: the $\mathcal A_e$-codimension is at most the image Milnor number, with equality for quasihomogeneous germs. For a quasihomogeneous germ we determine the derivations of its image modulo the conductor fields, in terms of the double-point surface, its cross-cap curve and its normalisation. This yields a closed formula, depending only on the weights and degrees, for the Hilbert series of the graded $\mathcal A_e$-normal space; its value at $t=1$ coincides with Ohmoto's formula for the image Milnor number, which comes from Segre--Schwartz--MacPherson Thom polynomials. For arbitrary germs we show that generic homogeneous corank-one germs of coprime degrees are $\mathcal A$-finite in every source dimension, and apply the reduction theorem of Fernández de Bobadilla, Nuño-Ballesteros and Peñafort Sanchis. In every pair of nice dimensions this reduces Mond's conjecture for corank-one germs to an inequality for generic homogeneous germs.

</details>


### [238] [Super Teichmüller Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03488)
*Nadia Ott*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文给出三个结果：亏格 g>1 超黎曼曲面超模空间的全局商表示；标记超黎曼曲面模叠（超 Teichmüller 空间）是复解析 Deligne–Mumford 超叠；并构造其 étale 图册，其底拓扑空间为 2^{2g} 个可缩单位球的不交并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究亏格 g>1 超黎曼曲面的模空间结构。超模空间缺乏类似经典 Teichmüller 理论的全局描述与图册刻画，作者希望建立其全局商表示并给出叠论与复解析框架下的严格结构。

Method: 采用超几何与叠论方法：构造超模空间的全局商表示；证明标记超黎曼曲面的模叠为复解析 Deligne–Mumford 超叠；并显式构造该超 Teichmüller 空间的 étale 图册。

Result: 得到三项结果：超模空间的全局商表示；标记超黎曼曲面模叠为复解析 Deligne–Mumford 超叠；构造出 étale 图册，其底拓扑空间同胚于 2^{2g} 个可缩单位球的不交并。

Conclusion: 该工作为超黎曼曲面模空间提供了全局商描述与叠论刻画，并通过 étale 图册揭示了超 Teichmüller 空间的拓扑结构（2^{2g} 个可缩分支），为后续超模空间与超 Teichmüller 理论研究奠定基础。

Abstract: The paper contains three results. First, we give a global quotient presentation of the supermoduli space of genus $g>1$ super Riemann surfaces. Second, we prove that the moduli stack of marked super Riemann surfaces, which we call super Teichmüller space, is a complex analytic Deligne Mumford superstack. Third, we construct an étale atlas for super Teichmüller space whose underlying topological space is a disjoint union of $2^{2g}$ contractible unit balls.

</details>


### [239] [Quantum cohomology, Hitchin systems, and Fourier--Mukai comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03520)
*Yunfeng Jiang,Hsian-Hua Tseng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文研究稳定无迹Higgs丛模空间X上的C*-等变量子上积，证明其由X×X中Lagrangian Steinberg cycle的Steinberg对应给出，并给出Γ-不变曲线类的特征分解、rank 2 genus 2情形下的15条内窥线与约化谱判别式，同时证明K(X)与扭K([X/Γ],α)的Fourier-Mukai同构与除子量子上积相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Higgs丛模空间上理解量子上积的几何实现，并借助Steinberg对应与内窥（endoscopic）结构刻画其曲线类与谱数据；同时将PGL_r-Higgs丛的orbifold量子上同调与gerbe扭K理论联系起来，以通过Fourier-Mukai变换实现量子结构的兼容性。

Method: 对固定行列式Λ、gcd(r,degΛ)=1的稳定无迹rank r Higgs丛模空间X，利用2点Kontsevich稳定映射空间到X的约化虚拟基本闭链经赋值映射得到Lagrangian Steinberg cycle，并由此构造Steinberg对应；对Γ=Pic^0(C)[r]给出Γ-不变曲线类或有限轨道和的特征分解；对[X/Γ]采用gerbe扭orbifold量子上同调，使用μ_r-gerbe α的扭K理论及Fourier-Mukai变换证明与除子量子上积的兼容性。

Result: 证明X上除子的C*-等变量子上积由Steinberg对应给出；给出Γ-不变曲线类的特征分解；在rank 2、genus 2时显式确定15条endoscopic lines与约化谱判别式；证明K(X)与扭K([X/Γ],α)同构且由Fourier-Mukai变换给出，并证明该变换与除子量子上积兼容。

Conclusion: Higgs丛模空间的等变量子上积可通过Steinberg对应统一刻画，内窥结构可由Γ-特征分解与谱判别式描述；在PGL_r-Higgs丛情形，gerbe扭K理论与Fourier-Mukai变换提供了与量子上积兼容的同构框架。

Abstract: Let $X$ be the moduli space of stable trace-free rank $r$ Higgs bundles over a smooth projective curve $C$ with fixed determinant $Λ\in\Pic(C)$ and $\gcd(r,\degΛ)=1$. We prove that the $\cc^*$-equivariant quantum product of a divisor on $X$ is given by the Steinberg correspondence of the Lagrangian Steinberg cycle in $X\times X$. The Steinberg cycle is the image of the reduced virtual fundamental cycle of the $2$-pointed Kontsevich stable map space to $X$ under evaluation map. For $Γ=\Pic^0(C)[r]$, we give a character decomposition for $Γ$-invariant curve classes or finite orbit sums. In rank two and genus two we explicitly determine its fifteen endoscopic lines and the reduced spectral discriminant. %The resulting endoscopic support statement is cohomological, not a determination of the entire Chow cycle.
  For $\widehat X=[X/Γ]$, which is the moduli space of stable $\PGL_r$-Higgs bundles over $C$. We consider the orbifold quantum cohomology theory from a gerbe-twisted theory. The complex K-theory $\KU^*(X)$ and twisted K-theory $\KU^*([X/Γ],α)$ by the lifting $μ_r$-gerbe $α$ of the universal projective bundle is an isomorphism, and is given by the Fourier-Mukai transformation. We prove that the Fourier-Mukai transformation is compatible with the quantum product by the divisors.

</details>


### [240] [Simpson's closedness conjecture in arbitrary rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03542)
*Tianzhi Hu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了 Simpson 关于稳定分次 Higgs 丛对应的流束 locus $W_{[(E,θ)]}^1$ 在 de Rham 模空间 $M_{\mathrm{dR}}(X,n)$ 中为 Zariski 闭的猜想，且适用于任意秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Simpson 猜想 $W_{[(E,θ)]}^1$（由具有给定分次 Higgs 丛 $(E,θ)$ 的 Simpson 滤过的平坦丛组成）在 de Rham 模空间中是 Zariski 闭的，但该猜想此前未被证明，本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 基于全纯丛模空间上 cohomology 行列式线丛的 Quillen 几何：对任意平坦丛的全纯族，构造配备典范全纯 determinant 联络的 cohomology 行列式线丛，并导出其曲率显式公式；对位于 $W_{[(E,θ)]}^1$ 中的平坦丛族，证明 determinant 联络形式为 exact。

Result: 证明了 determinant 联络形式的 exactness 迫使相关 determinant frame 在任意单参数退化上延拓为非零处处不消失的 frame，从而控制极限滤过。

Conclusion: 该控制极限滤过的性质最终给出所需的闭性，从而在任意秩下证明了 Simpson 猜想。

Abstract: For a compact Riemann surface $X$, Simpson associated to each stable graded Higgs bundle $(E,θ)$ a locus $W_{[(E,θ)]}^1 \subset M_{\mathrm{dR}}(X,n)$, consisting of flat bundles that admit a Simpson filtration whose associated graded Higgs bundle is $(E,θ)$. He conjectured that $W_{[(E,θ)]}^1$ is Zariski closed in $M_{\mathrm{dR}}(X,n)$. We prove this conjecture in arbitrary rank.
  The proof is based on the Quillen geometry of the determinant-of-cohomology line bundle over the moduli of holomorphic bundles. To any holomorphic family of flat bundles, we associate a determinant-of-cohomology line bundle equipped with a canonical holomorphic determinant connection, and derive explicit formulas for its curvature. For a family of flat bundles in $W_{[(E,θ)]}^1$, we then prove that the determinant connection form is exact. This exactness forces the associated determinant frame to extend as a nowhere-vanishing frame across any one-parameter degeneration, which controls the limiting filtration and yields the desired closedness.

</details>


### [241] [Rationality of varieties and $\mathbb{A}^1$-homotopy theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03680)
*Utsav Choudhury,Biman Roy*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同有理性性质与 $\mathbb{A}^1$-同伦论不变量之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将代数簇的有理性问题置于 $\mathbb{A}^1$-同伦论的框架下理解，建立经典有理性性质与同伦不变量之间的对应。

Method: 采用 $\mathbb{A}^1$-同伦论（Morel–Voevodsky 意义下的动机同伦论）工具，通过比较各类有理性性质与同伦不变量来建立联系。

Result: 给出了若干有理性性质与 $\mathbb{A}^1$-同伦论不变量之间的关联性结果。

Conclusion: $\mathbb{A}^1$-同伦不变量可作为研究有理性性质的有效工具，相关性质可在同伦论层面得到统一刻画。

Abstract: In this article, we present the results relating different rationality properties with $\mathbb{A}^1$-homotopy theoretic invariants.

</details>


### [242] [Mirror symmetry for gCICY threefolds (II): $[3,-1]$ blocks, VGIT, and toric phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03688)
*Atsushi Kanazawa*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究含 [3,-1] 块的广义完全交 Calabi-Yau 三维流形，通过相对 GIT 变相将其约化为普通 Batyrev-Borisov 对偶，从而构造镜像并计算 Hodge 数与 Picard-Fuchs 系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: gCICY 的配置矩阵含负元素，其定义线丛不是 nef，Batyrev-Borisov 构造无法直接应用，需要新的方法处理这类广义相并构造其镜像。

Method: 考察 P^1×B 上带 [3,-1] 块的 gCICY 三维流形，即由 O(3)⊠L 与 O(-1)⊠(L+R) 的截面定义，其中 2L+R=-K_B。引入辅助变量将广义截面转化为相对 GIT 变相，其相反相为 P^2×B 上秩 3 丛的零点轨迹。对一般数据，两相或者同构，或者通过 ∫_B c_1(L)^4 个不相交的 Atiyah flops 相联系。对射影空间乘积上的配置进行分类（共 18 个），并对若干例子显式构造镜像，计算基本周期与低阶 Picard-Fuchs 系统。

Result: 在射影空间乘积上共有 18 个含 [3,-1] 块的 Calabi-Yau 三维流形配置；除伴随论文中的非 Gorenstein 情形外，均具有 Gorenstein 环面 nef 相。由此每个配置的镜像对称可经变相约化为普通 Batyrev-Borisov 对偶，得到广义相自身的镜像 Hodge 数。详细分析了 4 个含 [3,-1] 块的例子及 1 个族外例子，构造显式镜像并计算其基本周期与低阶 Picard-Fuchs 系统；极大单值边界点处的指标代数同构于 A-模型相的理性偶上同调环。

Conclusion: 通过相对 GIT 变相，含 [3,-1] 块的 gCICY 镜像对称可约化为普通 Batyrev-Borisov 对偶，指标代数与 A-模型相偶上同调环的同构为这些广义相提供了有效的镜像描述。

Abstract: The configuration matrices of generalized complete intersection Calabi-Yau (gCICY) threefolds contain negative entries, so their defining line bundles are not nef and the Batyrev-Borisov construction does not apply directly. We study gCICY threefolds in $\mathbb{P}^1\times B$ with a $[3,-1]$ block, that is, cut out by sections of $O(3)\boxtimes L$ and $O(-1)\boxtimes(L+R)$, where $B$ is a smooth projective fourfold and $2L+R=-K_B$. Auxiliary variables turn the generalized section into a relative variation of GIT, whose opposite phase is the zero locus of a rank-3 bundle on $\mathbb{P}^2\times B$; for general data the two phases are isomorphic or related by $\int_B c_1(L)^4$ disjoint Atiyah flops. Over products of projective spaces there are exactly 18 Calabi-Yau threefold configurations with a $[3,-1]$ block. All of them except the non-Gorenstein case studied in the companion paper have Gorenstein toric nef phases. Thus, for every other configuration in this classification, mirror symmetry is reduced after variation of GIT to ordinary Batyrev-Borisov duality, which gives the mirror Hodge numbers of the generalized phase itself. We analyze four examples with a $[3,-1]$ block and one outside this class in detail. For each we construct an explicit mirror and compute its fundamental period and low-order Picard-Fuchs system; the indicial algebras at the maximally unipotent boundary points are isomorphic to the rational even cohomology rings of the A-model phases.

</details>


### [243] [Mirror symmetry for gCICY threefolds (I): a $[3,-1]$ block and a non-Gorenstein toric phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03719)
*Atsushi Kanazawa*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造并分析了广义完全交Calabi-Yau三维流形$X_+$的镜像族，通过辅助变量表示与toric GIT变分跨越wall得到$X_-$，并构建了镜像流形$Y$及其Picard-Fuchs系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义完全交Calabi-Yau三维流形$X_+=[\mathbb{P}^1|3\ {-1};\mathbb{P}^4|2\ 3]$包含负次数截面，且其nef相$X_-$所在的环境空间非Gorenstein，导致Batyrev-Borisov构造不适用，因此需要发展新的镜像构造方法。

Method: 采用辅助变量表示将负次数截面转化为toric GIT的变分；跨越一个wall后flop 16条不相交$(-1,-1)$曲线得到$X_-$；仅使用Hori-Vafa方程选取2参数Laurent族，并构造其光滑射影crepant紧化$Y$。

Result: 构造出$(h^{1,1},h^{2,1})=(46,2)$的镜像流形$Y$，其Mordell-Weil群为$\mathbb{Z}/4\mathbb{Z}$；$Y$的周期满足秩6的Picard-Fuchs系统，具有两个极大unipotent边界点，其indicial代数同构于$X_-$与$X_+$的有理偶上同调环；genre-zero预测与$X_-\to\mathbb{P}^2$上176条竖直直线和100条竖直二次曲线的独立计数一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为Batyrev-Borisov构造不适用的非Gorenstein环境空间中的广义完全交Calabi-Yau流形提供了可行的镜像构造框架，并通过枚举几何计数验证了镜像对称预测的正确性。

Abstract: We construct and analyze a mirror family for the generalized complete intersection Calabi-Yau threefold $X_+=[\mathbb{P}^1\,|\,3\ {-1};\ \mathbb{P}^4\,|\,2\ 3]$, with $(h^{1,1},h^{2,1})=(2,46)$. An auxiliary-variable presentation turns the negative-degree section into a variation of toric GIT. Crossing one wall flops 16 disjoint $(-1,-1)$-curves and yields a Calabi-Yau threefold $X_-$, a nef complete intersection in a toric $\mathbb{Q}$-Fano eightfold, which carries a genus-one fibration of index 4. Because this ambient space is not Gorenstein, the Batyrev-Borisov construction does not apply. We use the Hori-Vafa equations only to select a 2-parameter Laurent family, and construct a smooth projective crepant compactification $Y$ of the Laurent model with $(h^{1,1},h^{2,1})=(46,2)$; for very general parameters the Mordell-Weil group is $\mathbb{Z}/4\mathbb{Z}$. The periods of $Y$ satisfy a rank-6 Picard-Fuchs system with two maximally unipotent boundary points, whose indicial algebras are isomorphic to the rational even cohomology rings of $X_-$ and $X_+$. The resulting genus-zero predictions agree with independent counts of 176 vertical lines and 100 vertical conics on the genus-one fibration $X_-\to\mathbb{P}^2$.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [The geometry of monarch matrix approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02226)
*Shintaro Yoshizawa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将Helmke–Shayman关于秩-r矩阵流形最小二乘的Morse理论推广到Monarch矩阵（置换矩阵与两个块对角因子之积），用转移矩阵不变量统一刻画规范维数、Bruhat型胞腔余维与闭包关系，并给出Monarch-Schur定理、Morse指标公式、焦点集刻画及四元正则化下梯度流的全局收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Helmke与Shayman的经典结果仅适用于秩-r矩阵流形：一般目标下的最小二乘距离是非退化Morse函数，其指标由Bruhat胞腔余维决定。Monarch矩阵作为结构化因子分解层（置换×两块对角）在高效深度学习与结构化矩阵领域日益重要，但其流形的参数化具有规范自由度，缺乏对应的几何与优化理论，尤其缺乏对泛型目标的最小二乘Morse结构、临界点指标以及无正则梯度流是否收敛的系统分析。因此需要建立Monarch情形的类比理论。

Method: 作者对Monarch矩阵的参数化（固定置换矩阵乘以两个块对角因子）进行几何分析：引入转移矩阵（transfer-matrix）不变量统一描述参数化的规范维数、Bruhat型胞腔的余维及闭包关系；在等块情形证明Monarch-Schur定理，刻画最小化规范自由、最大化流形维数的置换，并给出任意分块的维数下界；进而证明泛型二阶非退化性，推导Morse指标公式并显式给出测度为零的焦点集；最后分析无正则梯度流的非强制性（存在无界平坦方向），并引入四元正则项，证明其恢复全局收敛、在存在精确解时选出规范最小范数解，否则给出良定义的最佳逼近像，同时保持Monarch架构。

Result: 建立Monarch矩阵最小二乘距离的类比Morse理论：规范维数、Bruhat型胞腔余维与闭包关系由同一转移矩阵不变量决定，但这些量一般不相等；等块情形下Monarch-Schur定理给出最优置换刻画及任意分块的维数下界；泛型目标下二阶非退化成立，得到Morse指标公式与显式的零测焦点集；无正则梯度流因无界平坦方向缺乏强制性，而四元正则化恢复全局收敛，并在有精确解时选出最小范数精确解，否则给出最佳逼近像，且保持Monarch架构，为结构化因子分解层中的权重衰减提供几何解释。

Conclusion: 该工作将秩-r矩阵的Morse几何理论成功推广至Monarch矩阵流形，揭示了转移矩阵不变量在规范自由度、胞腔结构与优化景观中的统一作用；Monarch-Schur定理与四元正则化共同表明，适当的正则项不仅是优化技巧，更可作为在结构化因子分解中选取规范解与解释权重衰减的几何机制。

Abstract: Helmke and Shayman showed that, for generic targets, least-squares distance to the manifold of rank-r matrices is a nondegenerate Morse function whose indices are governed by Bruhat-cell codimensions. We develop the analogous theory for Monarch matrices, products of a fixed permutation matrix with two block-diagonal factors. The parametrization's gauge dimension, as well as the codimensions and closure relations of the associated Bruhat-type cells, is determined by a common transfer-matrix invariant, although these quantities are generally distinct. In the equal-block case, we prove the Monarch-Schur theorem, characterizing the permutations that minimize gauge freedom and maximize manifold dimension, and derive a general lower bound for arbitrary partitions. We further establish generic second-order nondegeneracy, a Morse-index formula, and an explicit focal set of measure zero. Finally, we show that the unregularized gradient flow lacks coercivity because of unbounded flat directions. A quartic regularizer restores global convergence, selects a canonical minimum-norm exact solution when one exists, and otherwise yields a well-defined best-approximation image. The regularizer preserves the Monarch architecture and provides a geometric interpretation of weight decay in structured factorized layers.

</details>


### [245] [Accelerated Safe Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02433)
*Xuting Gao,Sonia Martínez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出A-SGF：将二阶Nesterov加速流经二阶CBF-QP安全滤波，证明前向不变性与唯一平衡点为约束最优解；在显式增益条件下保持无约束Nesterov收敛速率，实现可行域内随时可行且更快的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强凸约束优化中，如何在保证约束严格可行（安全）的同时保留加速收敛性能，是安全优化与加速梯度方法结合的关键难点。现有安全梯度流多为一阶，缺少二阶加速下的安全性与收敛性保证，因此作者提出二阶加速安全梯度流。

Method: 核心为A-SGF：以标称二阶加速流为输入，通过二阶控制障碍函数（CBF）构成的二次规划（QP）进行安全滤波。作者给出严格可行性与所得向量场局部Lipschitz连续性的条件；证明提升安全集的前向不变性，并证明其唯一平衡点即约束极小点。采用变分Lyapunov（variational Lyapunov）分析，将安全修正项的影响分离，导出使A-SGF保持与无约束Nesterov加速法相同收敛速率所需的障碍增益显式条件。

Result: 证明了A-SGF的严格可行性、向量场局部Lipschitz连续、提升安全集前向不变及唯一平衡点为约束最优解；给出了保持Nesterov收敛速率的障碍增益条件。一个非线性二维算例展示其随时可行性与更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: A-SGF能在满足约束安全的前提下保持Nesterov式加速收敛，为强凸约束优化提供具有理论保证的二阶安全加速方法；数值示例验证了其实时可行性与加速效果。

Abstract: This letter introduces an accelerated safe gradient flow (A-SGF) for
  strongly convex constrained optimization problems. The A-SGF filters
  a nominal second-order accelerated flow through a second-order
  control barrier function quadratic program. We establish conditions
  for strict feasibility and local Lipschitz continuity of the
  resulting vector field, prove forward invariance of a lifted safe
  set, and show that its unique equilibrium is the constrained
  minimizer. A variational Lyapunov analysis isolates the effect of
  the safety correction and gives explicit conditions on the barrier
  gains under which A-SGF retains the same convergence rate as the
  unconstrained Nesterov accelerated method. A nonlinear 2D example
  demonstrate anytime feasibility and faster convergence of the
  algorithm.

</details>


### [246] [Fully Coupled Nonlinear Mean-Field FBSΔEs: Solvability and an LQ Buffer-Adjustment Illustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02467)
*Yingjie Zheng,Qingxin Meng,Maoning Tang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文为全耦合非线性均值场前向-后向随机差分方程（依赖于无条件一阶矩）建立了有限时域可解性的充分条件，并给出唯一平方可积适应解、先验界与系数稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均值场前向-后向随机差分方程在离散时间下缺乏系统的可解性理论，尤其当方程全耦合且依赖一阶矩时，现有支配-单调性方法难以直接推广。作者希望建立不依赖原系数小性限制的可解性判据。

Method: 在前向递推中使用下一后向状态及其与创新乘积的条件投影；在中心坐标与均值坐标下构造确定性矩阵组合；基于支配-单调性方法证明关于同伦参数一致的延拓估计；结合全局Lipschitz连续性与一个活跃的支配-强制方向；对相反单调性方向进行符号变换；活跃参数可归一化而不需原系数的小性假设。

Result: 在全局Lipschitz连续与单个活跃支配-强制方向下，得到唯一平方可积适应解、先验估计及系数稳定性；通过带状态与均值饱和交互的非线性例子验证假设（含退化支配方向）；标量均值场LQ缓冲调整模型中Hamiltonian解刻画唯一开环最优解；有限情景树精确计算与直接二次优化结果一致。

Conclusion: 该文给出全耦合非线性均值场前向-后向随机差分方程有限时域可解性的充分条件，证明了解的唯一性、先验界与系数稳定性，并通过非线性例子与LQ模型及精确情景树计算验证了理论结果。

Abstract: This paper establishes sufficient conditions for finite-horizon solvability of fully coupled nonlinear mean-field forward--backward stochastic difference equations with dependence on unconditional first moments. The forward recursion uses conditional projections of the next backward state and its product with the innovation. Building on existing domination--monotonicity methods, we formulate deterministic matrix combinations in centered and mean coordinates and prove a continuation estimate uniform in the homotopy parameter. Global Lipschitz continuity and one active domination--coercivity direction yield a unique square-integrable adapted solution, an a priori bound, and coefficient stability. The active parameter can be normalized without imposing an additional smallness restriction on the original coefficients. A sign transformation handles the opposite monotonicity orientation. A nonlinear example with saturating state and mean interactions verifies the assumptions, including degenerate domination directions. A scalar mean-field LQ buffer-adjustment model illustrates the theorem: its Hamiltonian solution characterizes the unique open-loop optimizer. An exact finite scenario-tree calculation checks this characterization against direct quadratic optimization.

</details>


### [247] [Mean-variance portfolio selection: connections between mean-field systems and forward-backward stochastic systems under initial-terminal cost coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02485)
*Xun Li,Hongyu Shi,Zhen Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究均值-方差投资组合选择问题中均值场系统与前向-后向随机系统之间的内在联系，通过引入终端财富的条件期望过程将均值场项表示为后向状态，从而导出一类具有初始-终端值耦合的前向-后向随机控制问题，并建立相应的随机最大值原理与LQ理论框架，最终显式求解经典均值-方差模型的高效策略与有效前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典均值-方差投资组合选择问题的目标泛函中含终端财富的期望（均值场项），使得该问题不能直接纳入标准前向-后向随机最优控制框架；同时，耦合初始-终端值的特点使经典方法失效，亟需建立新的随机最大值原理与求解工具。

Method: 引入终端财富的条件期望过程，将均值场项转化为后向状态，构造带有初始-终端值耦合的前向-后向随机控制系统；针对含运行成本与初始-终端耦合的控制问题建立随机最大值原理并给出最优性充分条件；进一步研究允许控制权重不定的广义随机线性二次（LQ）控制问题，利用Riccati方程导出最优反馈表示。

Result: 建立了适用于含运行成本及初始-终端值耦合控制问题的随机最大值原理与最优性充分条件；在控制权重可不定情形下，通过Riccati方程得到了广义LQ问题的最优反馈表示；证明经典均值-方差投资组合模型为特例，并显式给出高效投资策略与有效前沿，同时通过数值模拟展示了结果并考察了参数敏感性及其金融含义。

Conclusion: 均值场系统可由前向-后向随机系统刻画，初始-终端耦合问题可通过所建立的随机最大值原理和Riccati方程方法有效求解；该框架统一并推广了经典均值-方差投资组合选择理论，具有明确的金融解释与应用价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates the connection between mean-field systems and forward-backward stochastic systems arising from the mean-variance portfolio selection problem. By introducing the conditional expectation process of terminal wealth, the mean-field term is represented by a backward state, leading to a class of control problems for forward-backward stochastic systems with coupled initial-terminal values. The coupling between the terminal value of the forward state and the initial value of the backward state in the cost functional prevents classical methods for forward-backward stochastic optimal control from being directly applicable. To overcome this difficulty, we establish a stochastic maximum principle for control problems with running costs and coupled initial-terminal values, and provide sufficient conditions for optimality. Furthermore, we investigate a class of generalized stochastic linear-quadratic control problems with initial-terminal coupling, allowing the control weight in the running cost to be indefinite, and derive the optimal feedback representation via a Riccati equation. The classical mean-variance portfolio selection model is shown to be a special case, for which the corresponding efficient investment strategy and efficient frontier are obtained explicitly, and numerical simulations are presented to illustrate the results and examine parameter sensitivity and its financial implications.

</details>


### [248] [Recursive Stochastic Linear-Quadratic Control with Indefinite Weights for Jump-Diffusion Systems with Random Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02530)
*Xinyu Ma,Yang Liu,Qingxin Meng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时间域内、带递归代价的有限活动跳跃扩散随机线性二次（LQ）控制问题，在不定权重下有界随机系数框架下，通过测度变换与非递归化处理，建立随机Riccati方程、反馈表示、比较与唯一性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典LQ理论多针对非递归代价与定常权重，而递归代价由BSDE定义、且权重不定时，值函数与最优控制的结构尚不清晰；同时跳跃扩散下测度变换会改变控制空间，需要解决物理测度与变换测度之间的相容性与能量有限性问题。

Method: 采用Brownian-Poisson测度变换与积分因子，将递归LQ泛函约化为非递归LQ泛函；在物理与变换平方可积控制空间的交集上，建立L^1二次数据的递归赋值与全变换值相等性；借助变换测度下的一致凸性与无控跳跃映射可逆性，从非递归值核识别随机Riccati方程；用条件能量估计将鞅系数转回物理测度；通过配方法给出反馈表示、比较与唯一性。

Result: 在变换一致凸性与无控跳跃映射可逆条件下，得到随机Riccati方程的识别、反馈最优控制的表示、比较定理与反馈容许类中的唯一性；对每个初始状态对，递归值恰好可达当且仅当变换最优控制具有有限的物理控制能量；并用解析例子展示非零反馈与负控制权重下的一致凸性。

Conclusion: 递归代价、不定权重与跳跃扩散下的有限时间LQ控制可通过测度变换统一处理，物理与变换控制空间相交是保证递归值可达与等价性的关键；所建立的Riccati方程与反馈刻画为后续研究提供框架。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon stochastic linear-quadratic (LQ) control for finite-activity jump diffusions with bounded random coefficients, indefinite weights and a linear recursive cost. The cost is defined by a backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) whose generator has bounded deterministic coefficients multiplying the Brownian and jump integrands. A Brownian-Poisson change of measure and an integrating factor reduce the criterion to a nonrecursive LQ functional. On the intersection of the physical and transformed square-integrable control spaces, we establish recursive evaluation for L^1 quadratic data under the transformed measure and equality with the full transformed value. Under transformed uniform convexity and invertibility of the uncontrolled jump map, we identify the stochastic Riccati equation from the nonrecursive value kernel. Conditional energy estimates justify transferring its martingale coefficients to the physical measure. Completion of squares gives feedback representation, comparison and uniqueness in the feedback-admissible class. For each initial pair, the recursive value is attained exactly when the transformed optimizer has finite physical control energy. Analytical examples illustrate nonzero feedback and uniform convexity with a negative control weight.

</details>


### [249] [Second-Order Mean-Field Schrödinger Bridges with Partial State Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02537)
*Asmaa Eldesoukey,Md Zulfiqur Haider,Italo Napolitano,Yongxin Chen,Abhishek Halder*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出惯性多智能体系统的部分指定薛定谔桥问题：仅给定首末端位置分布、速度自由度未指定，在Vlasov–Fokker–Planck（含非局部相互作用）平均场框架下推导最优性系统，并设计嵌套不动点（Fortet–Sinkhorn推广）算法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制系统中决策常仅依赖状态的部分信息，而经典薛定谔桥通常要求完整的端点分布（含速度），无法刻画只知群体位置快照、速度未知的情形。因此需要为该类部分信息下、含相互作用惯性的多智能体群体控制建立新的最优输运/桥接形式。

Method: 在平均场惯性多智能体框架下，用带非局部相互作用的非线性Vlasov–Fokker–Planck积分-偏微分方程描述集体动力学；仅在有限时域端点给定位置分布（群体快照），速度端条件不定。由此构造部分指定薛定谔桥问题，推导对应最优性系统，刻画控制需通过时间前向与后向传播同时受初始与终端指定信息影响；分析部分端点指定对最优控制与一致性演化带来的特殊条件。随后提出嵌套不动点算法，借鉴经典Fortet–Sinkhorn迭代并扩展以处理非局部相互作用与时间耦合导致的非线性。

Result: 得到部分端点指定下的最优性系统及其一致性条件，说明最优控制不是单侧由初端或末端决定，而由二者经前后向传播共同决定；部分端点指定还会引出区别于完整端点情形的控制与演化条件。所提嵌套不动点算法可迭代求解该最优性系统。

Conclusion: 该工作将薛定谔桥推广至惯性相互作用多智能体系统的部分信息设定，为仅依赖位置快照的群体控制提供理论刻画与可计算算法，并为后续数值与收敛性分析奠定基础。

Abstract: Motivated by scenarios in control systems where decision making is informed by only partial information about the states, we introduce a novel formulation of the Schrödinger bridge problem for inertial interacting-agent systems. Within our setting, the agent ensemble is steered to match partial specifications of the collective configuration. Our analysis pertains to a mean-field framework in which the collective dynamics are described by a nonlinear Vlasov--Fokker--Planck integro-PDE with nonlocal interactions. We assume that only distributions over positions are specified at the endpoints of a finite time horizon, representing aggregate population snapshots, while the corresponding velocity degrees of freedom remain unspecified. We derive the associated optimality system, identifying the optimal controlled evolution as jointly informed by the initial and terminal specifications through forward and backward propagation in time. We also show that the partial endpoint specifications bring about distinct conditions on the optimal control and the consistent evolution. Subsequently, to compute solutions to the optimality system, we develop a nested fixed-point algorithm inspired by the classical Fortet--Sinkhorn iteration, but extended to handle the nonlinearities arising from nonlocal interactions and temporal coupling.

</details>


### [250] [Control of Markov Jump Linear Systems with Uncertain Lumpable Cluster Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02573)
*Carlos A. F. Persiani,Ram Padmanabhan,Melkior Ornik,Marco H. Terra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文研究Markov跳变线性系统在真实模态仅知属于某可观测“模态簇”时的鲁棒控制，将其建模为min-max最优控制问题并化为Riccati递推求解，证明了递推收敛性与闭环稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有Markov跳变线性系统控制方法通常假设当前激活模态精确可知，从而能准确获知系统动态与转移概率。但在实际应用中，真实模态往往无法精确观测，只能确定其所属的模态集合，导致动态和转移概率均存在不确定性，因此需要针对这种“模态簇”观测不确定情形设计鲁棒调节控制器。

Method: 将真实模态仅知属于某观测簇的设定建模为min-max最优控制问题，目标是实现鲁棒调节；借助正则化最小均方优化（regularized least-mean-square optimization）的结果，把问题求解约化为一个Riccati递推，使控制器仅依赖模态所属簇的信息而不依赖精确模态。

Result: 给出了问题到Riccati递推的约化方法；建立了该递推的收敛性以及所得闭环系统的稳定性；通过数值算例验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 在模态仅以簇为单位可观测、动态与转移概率均不确定的情况下，min-max鲁棒控制可通过基于簇信息的Riccati递推有效实现，并具备收敛性与闭环稳定性保证，数值结果支持该方法的实用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the control of Markov jump linear systems when the active system mode is not exactly known. We rather assume that this true mode is only known to belong to an observed cluster of modes, making both the dynamics and the associated transition probabilities uncertain. We construct a min-max optimal control problem in this setting, with the objective of robust regulation. Using results in regularized least mean-square optimization, we describe how the solution of this problem can be reduced to a Riccati recursion, such that the controller depends only on knowledge of which cluster the mode lies in. We subsequently establish convergence of the recursion and stability of the resulting closed-loop system. A numerical example illustrates the effectiveness of the proposed methodology.

</details>


### [251] [Static Output Feedback Control for Multi-Agent Systems Using Nash Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02589)
*Paul Serna-Torre,Patricia Hidalgo-Gonzalez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于半定规划(SDP)的静态输出反馈控制综合方法，用于网络化线性时不变多智能体系统(MAS)，理论上证明可由状态反馈的Nash均衡构造输出反馈控制，保证闭环稳定性与耗散性，并通过三个数值算例与IEEE 24节点电网算例（261状态非线性电磁暂态模型）验证其成本效益、时域性能、可扩展性与实用性，同时开源Python/MATLAB工具箱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS输出反馈控制多依赖全状态信息，且以往研究通常仅在小规模数值模型中验证框架，缺乏在真实大规模网络化系统（如电网电磁暂态模型）中的可扩展性与实用性验证；同时需要兼顾个体最优（Nash均衡）与闭环稳定性、抗扰耗散性。

Method: 1) 建立SDP模型综合静态输出反馈控制；2) 理论证明MAS输出反馈控制可由已有状态反馈控制的Nash均衡构造，Nash均衡刻画各智能体在MAS系统动力学约束下最小化自身代价函数；3) 由该SDP模型导出的控制保证闭环系统稳定性及存在扰动时的耗散性；4) 四个案例：前三个数值算例与先进框架比较成本效益与时域性能，第四个针对改进IEEE 24节点电网设计频率调节方案，将逆变器视为智能体、仅利用相邻逆变器部分状态信息综合分布式控制器，并在261状态高保真非线性电磁暂态模型中实现；5) 在Python与MATLAB中提供开源工具箱。

Result: 所提SDP模型综合的控制能确保闭环稳定性和扰动下的耗散性；前三个数值算例表明其在成本效益与时域性能上可与现有先进框架相比；第四个案例在261状态非线性电磁暂态电网模型中成功实现分布式逆变器频率调节，验证了该SDP的可扩展性与对真实网络化MAS的实用价值，优于以往仅在小规模模型上验证的研究。

Conclusion: 该工作为网络化LTI多智能体系统提供了一种可扩展、实用的静态输出反馈控制综合SDP框架，融合Nash均衡与耗散性保证，并在大规模电网非线性模型中验证；开源Python/MATLAB工具箱便于研究者与工程人员直接应用。

Abstract: This work proposes a semidefinite programming (SDP) model that synthesizes static output control feedback for networked linear time-invariant multi-agent systems (MAS). We theoretically prove that the output feedback control of MAS can be constructed using the Nash equilibrium previously derived for state-feedback control. The Nash equilibrium considers that agents seek to minimize their individual cost functions subject to the system dynamics of the MAS. The resulting control from the pro- posed SDP model ensures the stability of the closed-loop system and dissipativity when a disturbance is present in the MAS. A set of four case studies is implemented. The first three numerical cases serve to validate and compare cost effectiveness and time-domain performance of the proposed control framework with other state-of-the-art frameworks. The fourth case designs a frequency regulation scheme for a modified IEEE 24-bus power grid. Using the proposed framework, distributed controllers are synthesized for the inverters which are considered agents in the grid. Each inverter uses partial state information of the neighboring inverters. The resulting control scheme is implemented in a high-fidelity non-linear model of 261 states that describes the electromagnetic transient dynamics of the power grid. Hence, compared to prior studies that usually validate their frameworks in small-scale numerical models, this fourth case demonstrates a virtue of our pro- posed SDP: its scalability and practical utility in real-world networked MAS. We implement the proposed framework in an open-access toolbox in Python and MATLAB, enabling practitioners and researchers to readily implement it in their applications.

</details>


### [252] [Data-Driven Static Output-Feedback Control for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02623)
*Paul Serna-Torre,Adam Sedlak,Patricia Hidalgo-Gonzalez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的输出反馈控制综合框架，用于线性时不变多智能体系统（MAS）；先用离线状态与输入轨迹样本求解纳什均衡，再通过半定规划计算静态输出反馈控制器，并在含构网/跟网逆变器的非线性多节点电网的电压与频率调节中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动MAS控制工作通常需要较强的可控性假设、依赖在线信息或状态估计；且多在小型数值算例中验证，难以反映真实系统特性。作者希望在更弱假设下实现无需在线信息与状态估计的输出反馈控制，并在高保真非线性MAS上验证其有效性。

Method: 1）提出两种算法，利用离线状态与输入轨迹数据样本确定MAS的纳什均衡（NE）；2）在给定NE解的基础上，构造半定规划以计算各智能体的静态输出反馈控制器；3）将该框架应用于含构网型与跟网型逆变器的非线性多节点电力网络，设计电压与频率调节控制方案。

Result: 相比传统策略（如比例-积分控制），该框架可实现最高约78%的超调量降低与30%的调节时间缩短；在高保真非线性MAS模型中实现成功的电网稳定与逆变器调节服务供给，并揭示小型数值算例难以捕捉的现象。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动框架在较弱可控性假设下，无需在线信息与状态估计即可完成MAS输出反馈控制综合，并能在真实非线性电力网络中有效稳定电网、提供调节服务，性能优于传统PI控制。

Abstract: This work proposes a data-driven framework that synthesizes output-feedback control for linear time-invariant multi-agent systems (MAS). We first develop two algorithms that use offline data samples of state and input trajectories to determine a Nash equilibrium (NE) for MAS. Next, we formulate a semidefinite program that, given the NE solution, computes the static output-feedback controllers for the agents in MAS. Compared to prior work, virtues of our framework include weaker controllability assumptions, the absence of online information and data-driven estimation of states. Next, we use the proposed framework to design control schemes for voltage and frequency regulation for nonlinear multi-nodal electrical networks with grid-forming and grid-following inverters. Thus, compared to prior work that implements their data-driven frameworks in small-scale numerical examples, we validate the performance of our data-driven framework in high-fidelity nonlinear models of MAS. This implementation in realistic MAS provides insights that small-scale numerical examples may fail to capture. Results show successful grid stabilization and provision of regulation services from the inverters in the grid using our proposed framework. It can attain up to 78% less overshoot and 30% faster settling time than traditional strategies such as proportional-integral control.

</details>


### [253] [A hybrid CNN-adjoint optimization framework for reconstruction of viscoelastic tissue properties in magnetic resonance elastography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02634)
*Anwesa Dey,Johann Rudi,Elena Cherkaev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出"CNN初始化+PDE约束伴随优化"的混合框架，用于MRE复剪切模量重建，兼顾快速性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRE中从位移场反演复值剪切模量是严重不适定反问题，噪声与有限边界激励下尤甚；纯PDE约束优化精度高但严重依赖初值，纯CNN反演快但精度有限。

Method: 以修正静态Stokes系统的标量形式为前向模型，建立前向问题适定性、极小元存在性与一阶最优性条件，实现带Armijo线搜索的非线性共轭梯度法；构建二维CNN将复值位移映射为空间变化复剪切模量作为初值；混合方法用CNN结果初始化伴随优化；CNN在单一扰动系数场上训练，在单一及未见的组合构型上测试。

Result: CNN可泛化到更难的组合构型，后续PDE约束优化进一步精化重建系数；混合方法收敛更快、精度更高。

Conclusion: 数据驱动初始化与物理驱动优化结合，可提升MRE重建的效率和准确性，具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance elastography (MRE) is a noninvasive imaging modality for quantifying the viscoelastic properties of soft tissues from shear wave propagation. Recovering the complex-valued shear modulus from measured displacement fields leads to a severely ill-posed inverse problem, particularly in the presence of noise and limited boundary excitations. We investigate adjoint-based optimization, convolutional neural network (CNN) reconstruction, and a hybrid framework combining both approaches. The forward model is based on a scalar form of the modified stationary Stokes system with a complex shear modulus. We establish well-posedness of the forward problem, existence of minimizers, and first-order optimality conditions for the adjoint-based formulation, and implement a nonlinear conjugate-gradient method with Armijo line search. While PDE-constrained optimization can accurately refine coefficient reconstructions, its performance depends strongly on initialization. We therefore construct a two-dimensional CNN that maps complex-valued displacement measurements to spatially varying complex shear modulus fields and provides rapid, informative initial reconstructions. The proposed hybrid method uses the CNN reconstruction to initialize the adjoint-based optimization, yielding faster convergence and improved accuracy. The CNN is trained on coefficient fields containing individual perturbations and tested on both individual and previously unseen combined configurations. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the CNN generalizes to these more challenging configurations, while subsequent PDE-constrained optimization further refines the reconstructed coefficient. These results demonstrate the potential of combining data-driven initialization with physics-based optimization for efficient and accurate MRE reconstruction.

</details>


### [254] [Advances in Continuous-Time Multiparametric Optimal Control: The Role of Time-Varying Active Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02680)
*Lida Lamakani,Efstratios N. Pistikopoulos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出判定多参数最优控制中临界区域边界形状的先验规则：若新约束在所有边界点同时激活且其余约束持续时间一致，则边界为超平面，否则为曲线边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数连续时间最优控制将初始状态空间划分为若干临界区域，相邻区域边界可能是超平面（可由线性方程闭式给出），也可能是必须计算的曲线边界。但计算前缺乏判定边界形状的方法，直接计算效率低且不必要。

Method: 通过分析相邻两区域的时变激活约束序列，证明边界为超平面需同时满足两个条件：新约束在每个边界点同一时刻激活，且其余约束的激活持续时间在各边界点相同。基于激活约束序列读出五条规则，每条规则对应超平面或曲线边界。

Result: 五条规则可预先判定边界形状。在Newell-Lee蒸发器上验证：七个区域划分的八条边界被分类为四条超平面（闭式给出）和四条曲线边界（精确计算）。

Conclusion: 边界形状可由相邻区域的激活约束序列直接确定，无需先验计算；该结果为多参数最优控制的分区计算提供了判定准则，并在Newell-Lee蒸发器案例中得到验证。

Abstract: Multiparametric optimal control in continuous time partitions the space of initial states into critical regions, each with its own control law involving a set of time-varying active constraints. The boundaries between two neighbouring regions are either hyperplanes, which are given in closed form by one linear equation, or curved boundaries, which must be computed. The sequence of time-varying active constraints plays a key role in defining the linear or nonlinear shape of a critical-region boundary. In this paper we show how to determine that shape prior to any computation. In particular, we show that the boundary is a hyperplane under two conditions. First, the new constraint becomes active at the same instant at every point of the boundary. Second, the durations for which the other constraints stay active are the same at every point of the boundary. Otherwise, the boundary is curved. Both conditions can be read from the sequences of active constraints of the two neighbouring regions, which gives five rules, each yielding a hyperplane or a curved boundary. We illustrate the rules on the Newell-Lee evaporator, where they classify the eight boundaries of a seven-region partition into four hyperplanes, given in closed form, and four curved boundaries, computed exactly.

</details>


### [255] [Finding Stationary Points under Higher-Order Smoothness: Trapping Algorithms and First-Order Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02791)
*Huanjian Zhou,Masashi Sugiyama,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在高阶光滑性下，仅用函数值或仅用梯度寻找近似稳定点的查询复杂度，覆盖不同维度、预言机模型、随机化与约束情形，并给出上下界匹配的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高阶光滑目标函数下求近似稳定点的查询复杂度，特别是梯度-only 方法是否存在随机化加速，以及一般维度下的最优复杂度刻画。

Method: 对常数维 \(C^{p,\beta_p}\) 目标，采用确定性函数值梯度陷阱算法；利用高阶线积分实现梯度-only 陷阱；二维 \(C^{1,1}\) 使用随机化积分；一般维度提出“陷阱或下降”框架，惰性复用边界模型；并对高维梯度-only 方法证明随机化下界。

Result: 常数维 \(d\)、\(C^{p,\beta_p}\)（\(\nu=p+\beta_p\)）下，函数值算法复杂度为 \(O(\varepsilon^{-(d-1)})\)（\(\mathbb R^d\)）与 \(O(\varepsilon^{-(d-1)/\nu})\)（\([0,1]^d\)）；二维 \(C^{1,1}\) 随机化梯度查询为 \(O(\varepsilon^{-4/3})\)，打破确定性 \(\Theta(\varepsilon^{-2})\) 障碍。一般维度 \(p\ge2\) 时，函数值与梯度复杂度分别为 \(O(d^p+d^{p-1+\beta_p/(\nu-1)}\varepsilon^{-\nu/(\nu-1)})\) 和 \(O(d^{p-1}+d^{p-2+\beta_p/(\nu-1)}\varepsilon^{-\nu/(\nu-1)})\)，适用于 \(\mathbb R^d\) 近似稳定点与 \([0,1]^d\) 近似 KKT 点，精度指数在 \(\beta_p=1\) 时与经典 AR\(p\) 的 \((p+1)/p\) 一致。高维梯度-only 下界：\(C^{1,1}\cap C^{p,1}\) 为 \(\Omega(\varepsilon^{-(3/2+1/(2p))})\)，\(p=2\) 匹配上界、\(p=3\) 匹配至对数因子；无 \(C^{1,1}\) 假设时任意固定 \(p\ge3\) 为 \(\Omega(\varepsilon^{-5/3})\)。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了高阶光滑下近似稳定点的查询复杂度，揭示了随机化积分对梯度-only 方法的加速作用，并给出一般维度最优量级及高维下界。

Abstract: We study the query complexity of finding approximate stationary points under higher-order smoothness using only function values or only gradients. Our results cover different dimensions, oracle models, randomization, and constraints.
  For constant dimension $d$ and $C^{p,β_p}$ objectives, with $ν:=p+β_p$, we give deterministic function-value gradient trapping algorithms with query complexities $O(\varepsilon^{-(d-1)})$ on $\mathbb R^d$ and $O(\varepsilon^{-(d-1)/ν})$ on $[0,1]^d$. High-order line integration enables gradient-only trapping. For two-dimensional $C^{1,1}$ objectives, randomized integration yields $O(\varepsilon^{-4/3})$ gradient queries on $\mathbb R^2$, breaking the deterministic gradient-only $Θ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ barrier.
  For general dimension, we introduce a trapping-or-descent framework that lazily reuses boundary models. For $C^{p,β_p}$ objectives with $p\ge2$, it achieves deterministic query complexities $O(d^p+d^{p-1+β_p/(ν-1)} \varepsilon^{-ν/(ν-1)})$ using function values and $O(d^{p-1}+d^{p-2+β_p/(ν-1)} \varepsilon^{-ν/(ν-1)})$ using gradients. These bounds apply to approximate stationary points on $\mathbb R^d$ and approximate KKT points on $[0,1]^d$. Their dimension-independent accuracy exponent matches the classical AR$p$ exponent $(p+1)/p$ when $β_p=1$.
  For sufficiently high dimension, we prove randomized lower bounds for gradient-only methods. For $C^{1,1}\cap C^{p,1}$ objectives, our lower bound is $Ω(\varepsilon^{-(3/2+1/(2p))})$, matching known upper bounds for $p=2$ and, up to logarithmic factors, for $p=3$. Without the $C^{1,1}$ assumption, we obtain an $Ω(\varepsilon^{-5/3})$ lower bound for every fixed $p\ge3$.

</details>


### [256] [When Can an Infinite-Stream Evaluation Support Dynamic Programming? Compatibility, Recursive Compression, and Generalized Summation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02842)
*Yingzhe Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为面向非标准无限期准则的动态规划建立了"可评估性"理论框架：区分了定义域可容许性、有限维连续自治可实现性、标量充分性与上下文序一致性，并证明在绝对可加权的准则下，精确连续自治持续状态的最小欧氏维数恰等于时间Hankel秩，从而给出有限Hankel秩是该非平稳性可被有限内生状态增广替代的充要刻画，且下界对非线性连续编码器也成立；同时指出Candelpergher–Ramanujan求和的共同持续检验更早失效，而Abel求和可作为prefix-closed基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非标准无限期准则下的动态规划不能仅靠有限流值（finite stream values）支撑，因为一步控制的替换必须保持可评估性（evaluable），且持续信息需递归传播。因此需要明确界定何种条件下无限期目标函数能够被有限的、连续自治的内生状态所替代，并区分若干层级的概念（定义域可容许性、可实现性、标量充分性、序一致性），以说明其界限所在。

Method: 针对期望阶段奖励流：形式上分离并定义四类性质——定义域可容许性、有限维连续自治可实现性、标量充分性、上下文序一致性。对绝对可加权的准则，采用算子/Hankel矩阵观点，将时间非平稳性编码为时间Hankel算子，证明精确连续自治持续状态的最小欧氏维数等于时间Hankel秩，并论证该下界对非线性连续编码器仍成立且在最简单的单状态MDP中即已出现。此外对Candelpergher–Ramanujan求和与Abel求和分别检验共同持续（common-continuation）性质。

Result: 在绝对可加权准则下，精确连续自治持续状态的最小欧氏维数等于时间Hankel秩，故有限Hankel秩恰好刻画时间非平稳性可由有限内生状态增广替代的情形；相应下界对非线性连续编码器依然成立，并已在单状态MDP中体现。对字面意义的Candelpergher–Ramanujan求和，共同持续检验更早失效：仅改变当前动作即可使评估脱离其定义域。相对地，Abel求和给出一个prefix-closed的对照基准。

Conclusion: 非标准无限期准则的动态规划需要以"可评估性 + 递归持续"为核心，而非仅依赖有限流值。时间Hankel秩给出了可实现有限维连续自治持续状态的精确维数下界与充要判据；Candelpergher–Ramanujan求和则连定义域稳定性都不满足，Abel求和可作为更稳健的prefix-closed参照。

Abstract: Dynamic programming for nonstandard infinite-horizon criteria requires more than finite stream values: one-step control substitutions must remain evaluable, and continuation information must propagate recursively. For expected stage-reward streams, we separate domain admissibility, finite-dimensional continuous autonomous realizability, scalar sufficiency, and contextual order consistency. For absolutely summable weighted criteria, we prove that the minimum Euclidean dimension of an exact continuous autonomous continuation state equals the temporal Hankel rank. Thus finite Hankel rank exactly characterizes when temporal nonstationarity can be replaced by finite endogenous state augmentation; the lower bound applies even to nonlinear continuous encoders and already holds in a one-state MDP. For literal Candelpergher Ramanujan summation, the same common-continuation test fails earlier: changing only the current action can leave the evaluation domain. Abel summation provides a contrasting prefix-closed benchmark.

</details>


### [257] [Quantitative Mean-Field Limits for Recent Variants of Consensus-Based Optimization Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02901)
*Konstantin Haug*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本论文为三种基于共识的优化（CBO）变体——δ-CBO、共识冻结（CF）、共识跳跃（CH）——严格推导混沌传播结果，证明N→∞时相互作用粒子系统的经验测度收敛到相应平均场方程的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBO是一类稳健灵活的零阶多粒子全局优化方法，能在高维非凸/非光滑问题上给出可证明的解，其框架建立在随机探索与确定性向共识点收缩之间的权衡之上。然而其变体（δ-CBO、CF、CH）的理论刻画尚不完整，尤其是粒子系统与平均场极限之间的定量联系（混沌传播）需要严格建立。

Method: 采用耦合方法（coupling method）推导混沌传播估计。针对每个变体，证明经验测度随粒子数N趋于无穷时收敛到对应平均场方程的解。处理两种情形：其一在高概率集上，经验测度一致有界；其二在无限制情形下，利用矩估计并细致处理粒子间依赖性，在不施加有利事件条件的情况下获得收敛。

Result: 对δ-CBO、CF、CH三种方案均建立了混沌传播结果，得到定量的混沌传播估计，并证明粒子系统在大量粒子极限下忠实地逼近理想化的平均场动力学。

Conclusion: 论文系统地为三类CBO变体提供了严格的定量混沌传播理论，涵盖有界与无限制两种情形，确立了粒子系统与平均场动力学之间的一致性，为CBO方法的理论可靠性提供了支撑。

Abstract: Consensus-based optimization (CBO) is a robust and flexible zero-order, multi-particle class of methods devised to provide provable solutions to high-dimensional global optimization problems, including truly nonconvex and/or nonsmooth cases. The algorithmic framework is grounded in a principled trade-off between stochastic exploration of the search space and deterministic contraction toward a consensus point. This thesis is concerned with three recently established variants of CBO. The $δ$-CBO method is similar to the classic CBO method, with the difference that the diffusion function is constant. The consensus freezing (CF) scheme can be understood as a piecewise CBO method. The overall time horizon is divided into intervals in which the consensus point is fixed. The consensus hopping (CH) scheme can be understood as a deterministic iterative scheme that jumps at each iteration to a Gaussian distribution centered at the consensus point of the previous distribution. The central contribution of this thesis is the rigorous derivation of propagation of chaos results for all three schemes. For each variant, we establish that the empirical measure of the interacting particle system converges, as the number of particles $N$ approaches infinity, to the solution of the corresponding mean-field equation. Two regimes are treated: first, on a high-probability set on which the empirical measures are uniformly bounded; second, in the unrestricted setting, where moment bounds and a careful treatment of the inter-particle dependence yield convergence without imposing conditioning on favorable events. The propagation of chaos estimates are derived by the coupling method. Together, these results provide quantitative propagation of chaos estimates for all three CBO variants and establish that the particle systems faithfully approximate the idealized mean-field dynamics in the large-particle limit.

</details>


### [258] [An Adaptive Symplectic Proximal Point Algorithm: Faster Convergence and Improved Numerical Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02963)
*Yi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一种自适应辛普积加速邻近点算法（ASPPA），证明了其在平方范数项上的收敛率为 O(1/k^2)，并在强单调包含问题上具有指数收敛率，数值实验显示其性能良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邻近点算法（PPA）是求解单调包含问题的基础方法，且若干关键凸优化算法都是其特例。尽管加速PPA变体受到广泛关注，但在某些特定情形下，PPA的收敛速度反而快于加速PPA，因此有必要研究更灵活的自适应加速方案。

Method: 提出自适应辛普积邻近点算法（ASPPA），即辛普积加速PPA的自适应版本，并对其收敛性进行理论分析。

Result: 证明ASPPA在平方范数项上的收敛率为O(1/k^2)，与部分加速PPA相当；在求解强单调包含问题时具有指数收敛率。数值实验表明ASPPA性能良好。

Conclusion: ASPPA兼具加速收敛与自适应特性，在一般和强单调情形下均具备良好收敛保证，是一种有前景的加速PPA变体。

Abstract: The proximal point algorithm (PPA) stands as a fundamental approach for solving monotone inclusion problems. Notably, several key convex optimization algorithms have been proven to be specific instances of PPA. Given the importance of the PPA, there has been growing interest in developing its accelerated variants. However, for some specific cases, the PPA converges faster than the accelerated PPAs. In this paper, we mainly study an adaptive version of symplectic accelerated PPA, called adaptive symplectic proximal point algorithm (ASPPA). We first prove that the convergence rate of ASPPA with respect to the square norm term is $O(1/k^2)$, which is the same as the convergence rate of some accelerated PPAs.
